JanusMesh: Fast and Zero-Shot 3D Visual Illusion Generation via Cross-Space Denoising
Authors: Siang-Ling Zhang, Huai-Hsun Cheng, Tsung-Ju Yang, Yu-Lun Liu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:55+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Creating 3D visual illusions, a single 3D mesh that reveals entirely different semantics from various viewing angles, is a fascinating but tough challenge. Existing optimization-based methods are slow and can produce oversaturated colors. In contrast, naive stitching approaches fail to produce geometrically coherent objects. This results in visible unnatural seams and semantic leaks. In this paper, we present a fast and training-free framework for generating text-driven 3D visual illusions. Our approach decouples the generation into two stages. First, we propose a cross-space dual-branch denoising process. This process dynamically decodes 3D latents into voxel space for CLIP-guided orientation alignment and Signed Distance Field (SDF) blending, which ensures seamless geometric fusion. Second, we introduce a view-conditioned texture synthesis module that projects and aggregates view-specific 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method generates highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in just 3-5 minutes. It significantly outperforms existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic recognizability, and efficiency. Project page: https://siang1105.github.io/JanusMesh.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：JanusMesh：通过跨空间去噪实现快速零样本3D视觉错觉生成
生成3D视觉错觉——即单个3D网格从不同视角呈现完全不同的语义——是一个有趣但极具挑战性的问题。现有基于优化的方法速度慢且易产生过饱和颜色；而简单的拼接方法则无法生成几何连贯的物体，导致可见的不自然接缝和语义泄露。本文提出一种快速且无需训练的文本驱动3D视觉错觉生成框架。我们的方法分为两个阶段：首先，提出跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态将3D潜在表示解码至体素空间，实现CLIP引导的朝向对齐与有符号距离场（SDF）融合，确保无缝几何融合；其次，引入视图条件纹理合成模块，将视图特异性2D扩散先验投影并聚合至融合几何体上。大量实验表明，我们的方法仅需3-5分钟即可生成高度逼真、双语义3D错觉，显著优于现有方法在几何完整性、语义可识别性及效率方面。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fast, zero-shot generation of 3D visual illusions that reveal different semantics from varying viewpoints, this paper introduces JanusMesh, a training-free framework that decouples generation into two stages. The first stage employs a cross-space dual-branch denoising process to align 3D latents via CLIP-guided orientation and SDF blending, ensuring geometric coherence. The second stage uses a view-conditioned texture synthesis module to project and aggregate 2D diffusion priors onto the fused geometry. Experiments show that JanusMesh produces highly realistic, dual-semantic 3D illusions in 3–5 minutes, significantly outperforming existing methods in geometric integrity, semantic clarity, and speed.
为解决从不同视角呈现不同语义的3D视觉错觉生成难题，本文提出JanusMesh框架，无需训练且生成速度快。该方法分为两阶段：第一阶段采用跨空间双分支去噪过程，动态解码3D潜在表示至体素空间，通过CLIP引导的朝向对齐与SDF融合确保几何一致性；第二阶段引入视角条件纹理合成模块，将视角特异性2D扩散先验投影并聚合至融合几何体上。实验表明，该方法可在3–5分钟内生成高真实感、双语义3D错觉，显著优于现有方法在几何完整性、语义可识别性与效率方面。
MemoryWAM: Efficient World Action Modeling with Persistent Memory
Authors: Sizhe Yang, Juncheng Mu, Tianming Wei, Chenhao Lu, Xiaofan Li, Linning Xu, Zhengrong Xue, Zhecheng Yuan, Dahua Lin, Jiangmiao Pang, Huazhe Xu
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robust robotic manipulation in the real world requires not only an understanding of the current observation, but also memory and dynamics modeling. World action models (WAMs) possess these capabilities by jointly modeling visual foresight and actions conditioned on both current and historical observations, making them a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. However, existing WAMs face a fundamental trade-off: methods with efficient inference typically condition only on a bounded window of recent observations and therefore struggle in non-Markovian environments, whereas methods that preserve long histories incur time and space costs that grow substantially with sequence length. To address this challenge, we introduce MemoryWAM, a world action model with efficient persistent memory. MemoryWAM uses a hybrid memory design that combines recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens that summarize long-range history. A tailored attention mechanism enables retrieval of both detailed short-term context and compressed long-term context, supporting memory-dependent decision-making with reduced inference latency and GPU memory usage. Across long-horizon, memory-dependent manipulation tasks in both simulation and the real world, MemoryWAM outperforms strong vision-language-action (VLA) and WAM baselines while maintaining favorable computational efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MemoryWAM：具备持久记忆的高效世界动作建模
在真实世界中实现稳健的机器人操作不仅需要理解当前观测，还需要记忆与动态建模能力。世界动作模型（WAM）通过联合建模视觉前瞻与基于当前及历史观测的动作，具备这些能力，因而成为机器人操作的有前景范式。然而，现有WAM面临根本性权衡：高效推理的方法通常仅依赖有限窗口的近期观测，在非马尔可夫环境中表现不佳；而保留长历史的方法则随序列长度增长导致时间和空间开销显著增加。为解决此问题，我们提出MemoryWAM——一种具备高效持久记忆的世界动作模型。MemoryWAM采用混合记忆设计，结合近期帧、事件边界锚点帧与压缩的概要令牌，以总结长程历史。定制化的注意力机制支持同时检索详细短期上下文与压缩长期上下文，从而在降低推理延迟和GPU内存占用的同时，支持基于记忆的决策。在仿真与真实世界中，MemoryWAM在长时程、依赖记忆的操作任务上优于强视觉-语言-动作（VLA）及WAM基线方法，同时保持良好的计算效率。
Summary / 总结
To enable robust robotic manipulation in non-Markovian environments, MemoryWAM introduces a world action model that balances memory retention with computational efficiency. It employs a hybrid memory architecture combining recent frames, event-boundary anchor frames, and compact gist tokens to summarize long-range history, paired with a tailored attention mechanism that retrieves both short-term and compressed long-term context. Experimental results show MemoryWAM outperforms existing vision-language-action and WAM baselines on long-horizon manipulation tasks in both simulation and real-world settings, while maintaining lower inference latency and GPU memory usage.
为实现非马尔可夫环境下的鲁棒机器人操作，MemoryWAM提出一种兼顾记忆保留与计算效率的世界动作模型。其采用混合记忆架构，结合近期帧、事件边界锚点帧和压缩的概要令牌以总结长程历史，并通过定制注意力机制高效检索短期与压缩长期上下文。实验表明，MemoryWAM在仿真与真实世界中的长时程操作任务上优于现有视觉-语言-动作及WAM基线模型，同时保持更低的推理延迟与GPU内存占用。
TimeProVe: Propose, then Verify for Efficient Long Video Temporal Reasoning in Activities of Daily Living
Authors: Arkaprava Sinha, Dominick Reilly, Siddharth Krishnan, Hieu Le, Srijan Das
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long Video Question Answering (LVQA) requires identifying sparse, query-relevant evidence within hours-long untrimmed videos. Existing approaches either process videos densely with large vision-language models (VLMs), incurring prohibitive computational cost, or rely on sparse caption-based reasoning, which often misses temporally localized and motion-centric evidence. We introduce TimeProVe, a cost-efficient hybrid framework for temporally grounded reasoning in long videos. TimeProVe first employs lightweight modules to generate action-grounded answer--evidence hypotheses and subsequently invokes an expensive VLM only for targeted verification. The core of our framework lies in the Action-based Candidate Evidence (ACE) module, which converts temporally localized actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and supporting evidence windows through lightweight LLM reasoning. We further introduce OpenTSUBench (OTB), an open-ended benchmark designed to evaluate temporally grounded reasoning in real-world Activities of Daily Living (ADL) scenarios. Experiments show that TimeProVe outperforms the strongest baseline on OTB by 7.3%, while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%. Furthermore, without explicit temporal grounding training, TimeProVe achieves competitive performance on Charades-STA, and reaches state-of-the-art results when enhanced with grounding VLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TimeProVe：先提出再验证，用于高效长视频日常活动时序推理
长视频问答（LVQA）需在数小时长的未修剪视频中识别稀疏且与查询相关的证据。现有方法要么使用大型视觉-语言模型（VLM）密集处理视频，导致计算成本过高，要么依赖稀疏字幕推理，常遗漏时序定位和运动中心的证据。我们提出TimeProVe，一种成本高效的混合框架，用于长视频中的时序推理。TimeProVe首先使用轻量模块生成动作锚定的答案-证据假设，随后仅对目标进行昂贵的VLM验证。框架核心是动作基础候选证据（ACE）模块，通过轻量LLM推理将时序定位的动作转化为查询条件的候选答案和支持证据窗口。我们进一步引入OpenTSUBench（OTB），一个开放性基准，用于评估真实世界日常活动（ADL）场景中的时序推理。实验表明，TimeProVe在OTB上比最强基线提升7.3%，同时减少VLM调用75%、推理成本降低93%。此外，无需显式时序锚定训练，TimeProVe在Charades-STA上表现竞争力，结合锚定VLM后达到最先进水平。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high computational cost of dense video processing and the limitations of sparse caption-based reasoning in long video question answering, TimeProVe introduces a hybrid framework that first generates action-grounded answer-evidence hypotheses using lightweight modules, then verifies them with a large vision-language model only when necessary. The core ACE module converts temporal actions into query-conditioned candidate answers and evidence windows via lightweight LLM reasoning. Experiments on OpenTSUBench show TimeProVe outperforms the best baseline by 7.3% while reducing VLM calls by 75% and inference cost by 93%, and achieves competitive or state-of-the-art results on Charades-STA without explicit temporal grounding training.
为解决长视频问答中密集处理成本高昂和稀疏字幕推理易遗漏时序关键证据的问题，TimeProVe提出一种混合框架：先用轻量模块生成基于动作的答案-证据假设，再仅对关键假设调用昂贵的视觉语言模型进行验证。其核心ACE模块通过轻量级LLM推理，将时序动作转化为查询条件下的候选答案与证据窗口。在OpenTSUBench基准上，TimeProVe相较最强基线提升7.3%，同时减少VLM调用75%、推理成本降低93%；在无显式时序对齐训练下于Charades-STA表现竞争力，结合时序对齐VLM后达到SOTA水平。
How Transparent is DiffusionGemma?
Authors: Joshua Engels, Callum McDougall, Bilal Chughtai, Janos Kramar, Senthoran Rajamanoharan, Cindy Wu, Arthur Conmy, Asic Q Chen, Jean Tarbouriech, Min Ma, Brendan O'Donoghue, João Gabriel Lopes de Oliveira, Rohin Shah, Neel Nanda
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:46+00:00
Comments: 20 main text pages and 6 pages of references and appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM reasoning transparency is a critical affordance for understanding model decisions, mitigating misuse and misalignment, and debugging surprising model behaviors. However, DiffusionGemma performs a larger fraction of its computation in a continuous latent space; does this make its reasoning less transparent? We study this question by decomposing transparency into two components: variable transparency, whether we understand intermediate snapshots of a model's computational state; and algorithmic transparency, whether we can use these snapshots to reconstruct the process by which the model arrived at its outputs. Naively, DiffusionGemma has poor variable transparency: its opaque serial depth, the amount of serial computation that occurs in between interpretable model states, seems at first 28.6X higher than the corresponding autoregressive Gemma 4 model. However, we show that we can map the information flowing between denoising steps through an interpretable token bottleneck with no decrease in downstream performance. Treating these intermediate states as interpretable reduces the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X that of Gemma 4. Algorithmic transparency is harder for diffusion models than for autoregressive models because all token predictions in the canvas can change at every denoising step, giving the model the power to implement complicated distributed algorithms during the denoising process. To begin bridging this gap, we conduct a suite of interpretability case studies, uncovering initial evidence of novel diffusion-specific phenomena such as non-chronological reasoning, token and sequence smearing, and intermediate-context reasoning. Finally, we test monitorability, a key application of transparency that measures whether model outputs are useful for downstream tasks. We find that DiffusionGemma is similarly monitorable to Gemma 4.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DiffusionGemma的透明度如何？
大语言模型的推理透明度对于理解模型决策、防止误用和对齐偏差、调试意外模型行为至关重要。然而，DiffusionGemma在连续潜在空间中执行了更大比例的计算；这是否使其推理更不透明？我们通过将透明度分解为两个组成部分进行研究：变量透明度（是否理解模型计算状态的中间快照）和算法透明度（是否能利用这些快照重建模型生成输出的过程）。直观上，DiffusionGemma的变量透明度较差：其不透明的串行深度（即可解释模型状态之间的串行计算量）似乎比对应的自回归Gemma 4模型高出28.6倍。然而，我们证明可以通过一个可解释的令牌瓶颈映射去噪步骤间的信息流动，且下游性能无损。将这些中间状态视为可解释的，可将不透明串行深度降低至Gemma 4的1.1倍。扩散模型的算法透明度比自回归模型更难，因为画布上所有令牌预测在每次去噪步骤中都可能改变，使模型有能力在去噪过程中实现复杂的分布式算法。为弥合这一差距，我们开展了一系列可解释性案例研究，发现了扩散模型特有的新现象，如非时序推理、令牌与序列扩散、中间上下文推理。最后，我们测试了监控性——透明度的关键应用，衡量模型输出是否对下游任务有用。结果发现，DiffusionGemma与Gemma 4的监控性相似。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess reasoning transparency in DiffusionGemma, the paper investigates whether its use of a continuous latent space reduces interpretability. It decomposes transparency into variable transparency—understanding intermediate states—and algorithmic transparency—reconstructing the reasoning process from those states. While naive analysis suggests DiffusionGemma’s opaque serial depth is 28.6X higher than Gemma 4’s, the authors show that an interpretable token bottleneck enables mapping information between denoising steps without performance loss, reducing the opaque serial depth to just 1.1X. Algorithmic transparency remains challenging due to simultaneous token updates during denoising, leading to novel phenomena like non-chronological reasoning and token smearing. Despite these complexities, monitorability tests reveal DiffusionGemma performs similarly to Gemma 4 in downstream task utility.
为评估DiffusionGemma在连续潜在空间中计算是否降低推理透明度，研究将透明度分解为变量透明度与算法透明度两个维度。结果显示，尽管DiffusionGemma初始的‘不透明串行深度’比自回归Gemma 4高28.6倍，但通过可解释的token瓶颈映射潜在信息后，该差距缩小至仅1.1倍。算法透明度因每步去噪时所有token预测动态变化而更难实现，但案例研究揭示了扩散模型特有的现象，如非时序推理、token与序列扩散及中间上下文推理。最终，监控性测试表明DiffusionGemma在下游任务中的输出效用与Gemma 4相当，证明其扩散架构未损害实用性。
UNIEGO: Proxies as Mediators for Unified Egocentric Video Representation Learning
Authors: Wenhao Chi, Arkaprava Sinha, Dominick Reilly, Hieu Le, Srijan Das
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Egocentric video understanding is inherently limited by the narrow perspective of wearable cameras: a single viewpoint, a single modality, a single model cannot capture the full richness of human action. We argue that a truly expressive egocentric representation must subsume complementary knowledge across viewpoints, modalities, and foundation model representations, yet remain deployable from egocentric video alone. To this end, we introduce a hierarchical multi-teacher distillation framework that produces UNIEGO, a unified egocentric encoder trained with nine teachers spanning ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, and four foundation models. Rather than distilling directly from heterogeneous teachers whose incompatible architectures and feature geometries induce conflicting gradients, our framework interposes a layer of representation-specific Proxy models that translate diverse teacher knowledge into a homogeneous egocentric space. A second distillation stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), then adaptively selects, for each training sample, the subset of proxies that are both correct and confident, distilling exclusively from reliable supervision and suppressing erroneous signals. SPD is further stabilized by initializing UNIEGO as a learned convex combination of proxy parameters, placing the unified model in a well-conditioned region of the loss landscape before distillation begins. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art performance across three egocentric video understanding tasks - action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation on three challenging ego-exo benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher distillation baselines and demonstrating that structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer yields richer and more discriminative egocentric representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UNIEGO：代理作为统一第一人称视频表征学习的中介
第一人称视频理解受限于可穿戴摄像头的狭窄视角：单一视角、单一模态、单一模型无法捕捉人类动作的全部丰富性。我们认为，真正表达力强的第一人称表征必须融合多视角、多模态及基础模型表征的互补知识，同时仍能仅从第一人称视频中部署。为此，我们提出一种分层多教师蒸馏框架，生成UNIEGO——一种通过九位教师（涵盖第一人称与第三人称视角、RGB、深度及骨架模态）及四位基础模型训练的统一第一人称编码器。不同于直接从异构教师蒸馏（其不兼容的架构与特征几何会引发冲突梯度），我们的框架引入了表示特定的代理模型层，将多样教师知识翻译至统一的第一人称空间。第二阶段蒸馏——选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）——针对每个训练样本自适应选择正确且置信度高的代理子集，仅从可靠监督中蒸馏，抑制错误信号。SPD通过将UNIEGO初始化为代理参数的学得凸组合进一步稳定，使统一模型在蒸馏前处于损失景观的良好条件区域。UNIEGO在三个具有挑战性的第一人称-第三人称基准测试中，于动作识别、视频检索和动作分割三项任务上均达到最先进性能，超越朴素多教师蒸馏基线，证明结构化、代理中介的知识迁移可生成更丰富、更具判别力的第一人称表征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-view, single-modal egocentric video understanding, this work proposes UNIEGO, a unified encoder trained via hierarchical multi-teacher distillation using nine diverse teachers covering ego-exo viewpoints, RGB, depth, and skeleton modalities, along with four foundation models. To resolve architectural and geometric incompatibilities among teachers, the framework introduces proxy models that translate heterogeneous teacher knowledge into a shared egocentric representation space. A second stage, Selective Proxy Distillation (SPD), adaptively selects only confident and correct proxy signals per training sample, suppressing noise, and stabilizes training by initializing UNIEGO as a convex combination of proxy parameters. UNIEGO achieves state-of-the-art results on three egocentric tasks—action recognition, video retrieval, and action segmentation—across three benchmarks, outperforming naive multi-teacher baselines and demonstrating the effectiveness of structured, proxy-mediated knowledge transfer.
为克服单视角、单模态第一人称视频理解的局限性，本文提出UNIEGO，通过分层多教师蒸馏框架训练统一编码器，融合九种教师（涵盖第一/第三人称视角、RGB、深度、骨架模态）及四个基础模型的知识。为解决异构教师带来的梯度冲突，框架引入代理模型将教师知识映射至统一第一人称空间，随后通过选择性代理蒸馏（SPD）动态筛选每样本中准确且置信的代理信号，避免错误监督。UNIEGO以代理参数的凸组合初始化，确保蒸馏前处于损失函数的良好区域，最终在三个挑战性第一人称基准上的三项任务（动作识别、视频检索、动作分割）中达到最先进性能，显著优于朴素多教师蒸馏基线，验证了结构化代理中介知识迁移的有效性。
Optimal Deterministic Multicalibration and Omniprediction
Authors: Georgy Noarov, Aaron Roth
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A model is multicalibrated on a collection of group weights $G$ if it is calibrated -- i.e. unbiased even conditional on its prediction -- not just overall, but also after reweighting contexts by each $g \in G$. It is a useful property for many downstream applications and is a basic desideratum of trustworthy machine learning. Before this work, all predictors known to attain the minimax-optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity rate for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration were randomized, while deterministic predictors were known only with substantially worse sample complexity. Whether randomization is necessary for optimal sample complexity in multicalibration was explicitly asked by [CLNR26] and implicitly in several prior works. We resolve this open problem by giving a minimax-optimal multicalibration algorithm that outputs a deterministic predictor. We then generalize the algorithm to produce optimal deterministic predictors that satisfy outcome indistinguishability (OI) with respect to finite or finitely covered collections of tests. As an application, this also gives deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors with optimal sample complexity, resolving open problems posed by [OKK25] and [BHHLZ25].
中文标题/摘要
标题：最优确定性多校准与全预测
若一个模型在一组组权重 $G$ 上多校准，则它不仅在整体上校准（即即使在预测条件下也无偏），而且在对每个 $g \in G$ 重新加权上下文后仍保持校准。这一性质对许多下游应用非常有用，也是可信机器学习的基本要求。此前，所有已知能实现 $\varepsilon$-多校准的最小最大最优样本复杂度 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的预测器均为随机化预测器，而确定性预测器的样本复杂度则显著更差。[CLNR26] 明确提出了随机化是否是多校准中实现最优样本复杂度的必要条件，此前多项工作也隐含提出了这一问题。我们通过提供一个最小最大最优的多校准算法，该算法输出确定性预测器，解决了这一开放问题。随后，我们将该算法推广，使其能生成满足有限或有限覆盖测试集合的输出不可区分性（OI）的最优确定性预测器。作为应用，这也给出了具有最优样本复杂度的确定性全预测器和泛预测器，解决了 [OKK25] 和 [BHHLZ25] 提出的开放问题。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the open question of whether randomization is necessary to achieve minimax-optimal sample complexity for multicalibration, a property ensuring calibration across reweighted groups. The authors present a deterministic algorithm that achieves the optimal $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ sample complexity for $\varepsilon$-multicalibration, resolving a long-standing gap between randomized and deterministic predictors. They further extend this algorithm to produce deterministic predictors satisfying outcome indistinguishability with respect to finite or finitely covered test collections, enabling optimal deterministic omnipredictors and panpredictors, thereby solving open problems from prior works.
本文解决了多校准中是否需要随机化才能达到最优样本复杂度的开放问题。作者提出了一种确定性算法，实现了 $\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})$ 的最优样本复杂度，填补了此前确定性方法与随机方法之间的差距。该算法进一步推广至满足有限或有限覆盖测试集的输出不可区分性，从而实现了最优的确定性全预测器和泛预测器，解决了先前工作中提出的开放问题。
Thinking in Boxes: 3D Editing in Real Images Made Easy
Authors: Pradhaan S Bhat, Naveen Chandra R, Rishubh Parihar, Vaibhav Vavilala, R. Venkatesh Babu, D. A. Forsyth, Anand Bhattad
First: 2026-06-18T17:59:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:59:05+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://thinking-in-boxes.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Text and 2D-conditioning interfaces provide weak, ambiguous control over spatial transformations in image editing -- particularly under large object motions and camera changes. Prior work has used 3D primitives such as boxes, but only as loose conditioning signals indicating approximate object location rather than specifying the transformation. We instead use 3D boxes as structured specifications: the user provides the input and output boxes of the edit, casting editing as a well-posed geometry problem. This ``thinking in boxes'' interface, where each box face is color-coded to convey 3D orientation, gives precise control over translation, rotation, scaling, and viewpoint changes in real images while preserving scene and object identity, and recovering previously unseen object regions. To ground transformations in scene appearance, we introduce a depth-aligned planar floor as a global reference frame, shaded with depth-aware cues. Conditioned on this structure, an image generator produces consistent results under large transformations. Trained in two stages -- on synthetic multi-object scenes and a small set of real-world videos from Objectron -- the system generalizes to complex, in-the-wild real images. Our method operates directly on real photographs and substantially outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on large 3D edits.
中文标题/摘要
标题：框中思考：真实图像中的3D编辑变得简单
文本和2D条件界面在图像编辑中对空间变换提供弱而模糊的控制，特别是在大物体运动和相机变化时。先前工作使用3D原语（如盒子），但仅作为松散的条件信号，指示物体的大致位置，而非指定变换。我们则将3D盒子作为结构化规范：用户提供编辑的输入和输出盒子，将编辑视为一个良定义的几何问题。该“框中思考”界面中，每个盒子面用颜色编码以传达3D方向，从而在真实图像中精确控制平移、旋转、缩放和视角变化，同时保持场景和物体身份，并恢复先前未见的物体区域。为将变换与场景外观关联，我们引入深度对齐的平面地板作为全局参考框架，并用深度感知线索着色。在该结构条件下，图像生成器在大变换下仍能产生一致结果。系统分两阶段训练——在合成多物体场景和Objectron的小规模真实视频数据集上训练——从而泛化到复杂的真实野外图像。我们的方法直接作用于真实照片，并在大3D编辑任务上显著优于近期最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the ambiguity of text and 2D-conditioning in controlling 3D image edits, this work introduces a novel interface where users specify edits via input and output 3D boxes, treating editing as a precise geometry problem. By color-coding box faces to indicate orientation, the method enables fine-grained control over translation, rotation, scaling, and viewpoint changes while preserving object identity and recovering unseen regions. To anchor transformations in real-world scenes, it uses a depth-aligned planar floor as a global reference, shaded with depth cues. Trained in two stages on synthetic scenes and real videos, the system generalizes to complex real images and outperforms recent state-of-the-art methods on large 3D edits.
为了解决文本和2D条件控制在图像编辑中对空间变换表达模糊的问题，该研究提出将3D盒子作为精确的结构化编辑规范。用户通过指定输入与输出盒子来控制平移、旋转、缩放和视角变化，盒子面通过颜色编码指示三维朝向。方法引入深度对齐的平面地板作为全局参考系，以锚定场景外观中的变换，使生成模型能在大幅变换下保持一致性。该系统分两阶段训练，先在合成场景上训练，再在少量真实视频上微调，最终能泛化到复杂真实图像，并在大范围3D编辑任务上显著优于当前最先进方法，同时保持物体身份并恢复未见区域。
Structuring and Tokenizing Distributed User Interest Context for Generative Recommendation
Authors: Ruizhong Qiu, Yinglong Xia, Dongqi Fu, Hanqing Zeng, Ren Chen, Xiangjun Fan, Hong Li, Hong Yan, Hanghang Tong
First: 2026-06-18T17:58:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:58:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative recommendation is an emerging paradigm that has shown promise in industrial recommendation systems, aiming to predict users' next interactions from their historical behaviors. At the core of generative recommendation lies item tokenization, which bridges item semantics and recommendation models. However, existing methods often struggle to effectively organize and inject complex user-behavioral and item-semantic contexts into recommendation models simultaneously. On the one hand, existing graph-based integration methods, such as graph serialization and graph neural networks, either suffer from scalability issues or exploit only local graph information. On the other hand, existing semantic tokenization methods typically rely on heuristics and lack explicit supervision signals, which may lead to inaccurate or suboptimal semantic representations. To address these limitations in user interest context modeling, we propose G2Rec, a scalable framework that unifies holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization for industrial-scale generative recommendation. Overall, G2Rec enables recommendation models to capture holistic and semantically grounded user interest prototypes without requiring ground-truth user interests, thereby providing more comprehensive and accurate modeling of user behavior contexts in industrial sequential recommendation. Online deployment across product surfaces and extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate the superiority of G2Rec over existing methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向生成式推荐的分布式用户兴趣上下文结构化与分词方法
生成式推荐是一种新兴范式，在工业推荐系统中展现出潜力，旨在从用户历史行为中预测其下一次交互。生成式推荐的核心在于物品分词，它连接了物品语义与推荐模型。然而，现有方法往往难以有效组织并同时注入复杂的用户行为与物品语义上下文。一方面，现有基于图的集成方法（如图序列化与图神经网络）或存在可扩展性问题，或仅利用局部图信息；另一方面，现有语义分词方法通常依赖启发式规则，缺乏明确的监督信号，可能导致语义表示不准确或次优。为解决用户兴趣上下文建模中的上述局限，我们提出G2Rec框架，该框架可扩展地统一了全局图结构用户协同参与建模与语义分词，适用于工业级生成式推荐。总体而言，G2Rec使推荐模型能够在无需真实用户兴趣标签的情况下，捕捉全面且语义基础的用户兴趣原型，从而更全面、精准地建模工业序列推荐中的用户行为上下文。在产品页面上的线上部署及在公开数据集上的广泛实验均表明，G2Rec优于现有方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to better model complex user interest contexts in generative recommendation, this paper introduces G2Rec, a scalable framework that integrates holistic graph-based user co-engagement modeling with semantic tokenization. G2Rec addresses limitations of existing methods by avoiding scalability issues of graph-based approaches and overcoming heuristic-driven tokenization without ground-truth supervision. Experimental results on public datasets and online deployment show G2Rec outperforms prior methods in capturing comprehensive, semantically grounded user interest prototypes, leading to more accurate and context-aware recommendations.
为提升生成式推荐中用户兴趣上下文的建模能力，本文提出G2Rec框架，融合全局图结构建模与语义化分词技术。该方法克服了传统图方法的可扩展性不足和语义分词依赖启发式规则、缺乏监督信号的缺陷。实验表明，G2Rec在公开数据集和线上部署中显著优于现有方法，能无需真实用户兴趣标签，实现更全面、更精准的用户兴趣原型建模。
Easy Reads: A Python program for making Scientific Papers on arXiv more Reader Friendly and Accessible
Authors: Vishal Verma
First: 2026-06-18T17:57:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:57:26+00:00
Comments: 9 pages. Open-source software project available at: https://github.com/Curious-flow/Easy-Reads
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Scientific papers are frequently dense and characterized by features such as small fonts and line spacing, double columns of text, and tightly arranged figures. While these features make papers more compact, they can hinder readability, make them less accessible, and can strain the reader. arXiv is a premier open-access repository for scientific papers across different fields and is used extensively by researchers, including those in the physics and astrophysics communities. Easy Reads is an automated, end-to-end, open-source Python program that helps address the stated challenge by making papers from arXiv more reader-friendly and accessible. Easy Reads can automatically fetch a paper from arXiv via its URL and work with the source TeX file to allow custom formatting of the paper features, primarily the font size, and the number of columns used. The main goal of Easy Reads is to facilitate ease of reading of scientific papers.

标题：Easy Reads：一个使arXiv科学论文更易读、更易访问的Python程序

Generating Robot Hands from Human Demonstrations
Authors: Sha Yi, Nicklas Hansen, Xueqian Bai, Carmelo Sferrazza, Michael T. Tolley, Xiaolong Wang
First: 2026-06-18T17:57:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:57:21+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robot learning has advanced rapidly in learning control, but learning the physical body of a robot remains much more difficult because jointly searching over design and control creates a very large combinatorial problem. Here, we present a data-driven framework for generating robot hands from human demonstrations. Instead of learning a complex controller together with each candidate design, we generate robot hand designs using the same simple control policy used after fabrication: matching fingertip positions through inverse kinematics. Using more than 4 million frames of human fingertip motion from everyday manipulation, our algorithm optimizes tree-structured robot hands to reproduce desired target motions. The framework produced both a 6-degree-of-freedom (DoF) general-purpose hand and lower-DoF task-specific hands with spatial four-bar mimic joints. To accelerate the search over designs, we trained a reinforcement-learning (RL) actor to propose good hand designs and joint angles, reducing search time from hours to minutes. We fabricated the mechanisms directly as one-piece articulated structures with print-in-place joints. In real-world experiments, the 6-DoF hand achieved highly accurate teleoperated fingertip tracking better than available commercial robot hands, whereas the specialized 3-DoF hands reproduced structured human and synthetic trajectories with reduced mechanical complexity. These results showed that large-scale human motion data can be used not only to train robot controllers but also as a reference for optimizing and generating the physical embodiment of robots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从人类演示生成机器人手
机器人学习在控制学习方面进展迅速，但学习机器人的物理结构仍更为困难，因为同时搜索设计与控制会形成一个非常大的组合问题。本文提出了一种基于数据的框架，从人类演示生成机器人手。我们不学习与每个候选设计配套的复杂控制器，而是使用制造后相同的简单控制策略——通过逆运动学匹配指尖位置——生成机器人手设计。利用超过400万帧日常操作中的人类指尖运动数据，我们的算法优化树状结构的机器人手以重现目标运动。该框架生成了6自由度通用手和低自由度任务专用手，后者采用空间四连杆仿生关节。为加速设计搜索，我们训练了一个强化学习（RL）策略网络，提出优良的手部设计和关节角度，将搜索时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。我们直接将机构制造为一体式铰接结构，关节采用原位打印。在真实实验中，6自由度手实现了比现有商用机器人手更精确的遥操作指尖跟踪，而专用3自由度手则以更低机械复杂度再现了结构化的人类与合成轨迹。这些结果表明，大规模人类运动数据不仅可用于训练机器人控制器，也可作为优化和生成机器人物理实体的参考。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of jointly optimizing robot body design and control, this work proposes a data-driven method to generate robot hands from human motion demonstrations. It uses a simple inverse kinematics-based control policy to guide the design optimization of tree-structured hands, leveraging over 4 million human fingertip motion frames. A reinforcement learning actor accelerates design search, reducing optimization time from hours to minutes. Experimental results show that the generated 6-DoF hand achieves superior teleoperated fingertip tracking compared to commercial hands, while task-specific 3-DoF hands successfully replicate structured motions with reduced mechanical complexity, demonstrating that human motion data can inform both control and physical embodiment.
为解决机器人身体设计与控制联合优化的难题，该研究提出一种基于人类演示数据生成机器人手的方法。通过逆运动学控制策略引导树状结构手部设计，优化超过400万帧人类指尖运动数据，结合强化学习加速设计搜索，将优化时间从数小时缩短至数分钟。所制造的单体打印结构中，6自由度通用手在遥操作指尖跟踪精度上优于商用产品，而任务专用3自由度手则以更简化的机械结构复现结构化的人类与合成轨迹，表明大规模人类运动数据不仅能训练控制器，也可用于优化和生成机器人物理形态。
The Token Is a Group Element: On Lie-Algebra Attention over Matrix Lie Groups
Authors: Przemyslaw Musialski
First: 2026-06-18T17:56:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:56:17+00:00
Comments: preprint, 19 pages, 3 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We place the attention token on the group: a token is an element $g_i$ of a matrix Lie group $G$ -- a bare transformation, with no feature payload and no external action $ρ(g)$ carrying it. To our knowledge this is the first attention construction whose tokens are bare matrix Lie group elements: their score is the closed-form algebra norm of the relative pose rather than a learned kernel, and it reaches the affine full-frame groups that every irrep- or surjective-exp-based method must exclude. We call it Lie-Algebra Attention. Once tokens are group elements, the rest follows with none of the usual representation-theoretic machinery. The relative geometry of a pair is canonical, $g_i^{-1} g_j$, so the pairwise invariant $w_{ij} = \log(g_i^{-1} g_j)$ is intrinsic rather than designed; equivariance under the diagonal $G$-action is tautological, and the cocycle condition holds automatically. The attention score is the negative squared algebra norm, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$: the canonical proximity kernel under a block-weighted Frobenius inner product, with no irreducible representations, spherical harmonics, Clebsch-Gordan products, or learned kernel. The construction applies to any matrix Lie group on a chosen logarithm chart containing the relative poses, including the non-compact non-abelian affine groups with scale and shear that no vector-token attention method reaches: neither the irrep tradition nor surjective-exp methods. Three sequence-completion experiments, on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2), bear this out: the closed-form score matches a learned MLP kernel on the same invariant and outperforms it on SE(2), using 50 to 80x fewer score parameters, while a vector-token baseline breaks invariance by five to twelve orders of magnitude.
中文标题/摘要
标题：令牌是群元素：关于矩阵李群上的李代数注意力
我们将注意力令牌置于群上：令牌是矩阵李群G中的元素gi——一个裸露的变换，无特征负载，也无外部作用ρ(g)携带它。据我们所知，这是首个令牌为裸露矩阵李群元素的注意力构造：其得分是相对姿态的闭式代数范数，而非学习核，且能覆盖所有不可约表示或指数映射方法必须排除的仿射全帧群。我们称之为李代数注意力。一旦令牌成为群元素，其余部分无需常规表示论工具即可推导。一对元素的相对几何是规范的，gi⁻¹gj，因此其不变量wij = log(gi⁻¹gj)是内在的而非人为设计；在对角G作用下的等变性是显然的，且挠量条件自动成立。注意力得分是负平方代数范数，sij = -||log(gi⁻¹gj)||λ²/τ：在块加权Frobenius内积下的规范邻近核，无需不可约表示、球面调和、Clebsch-Gordan积或学习核。该构造适用于任何矩阵李群，在包含相对姿态的选定对数图中，包括向量令牌注意力方法无法触及的非紧致非阿贝尔仿射群（含尺度与剪切）：既非不可约表示传统，亦非指数映射方法所能及。在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的三个序列补全实验验证了这一点：闭式得分与同不变量下的学习MLP核匹配，并在SE(2)上优于后者，参数量少50至80倍，而向量令牌基线则因不变性破坏达五至十二个数量级。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for attention mechanisms that respect geometric structure without relying on learned kernels or representation theory, this work introduces Lie-Algebra Attention, where attention tokens are directly modeled as elements of matrix Lie groups. The method computes attention scores using the closed-form algebra norm of the relative group pose, $s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$, avoiding irreducible representations and learned components. Experiments on SE(2), SO(3), and Aff(2) show that this approach achieves performance matching or exceeding learned MLP baselines while using 50 to 80 times fewer parameters, and the vector-token baseline fails to maintain invariance by orders of magnitude.
为构建尊重几何结构且无需依赖学习核或表示理论的注意力机制，本文提出Lie-Algebra Attention，其注意力令牌直接建模为矩阵李群元素。该方法通过相对群姿态的闭式代数范数计算注意力分数，$s_{ij} = -\|\log(g_i^{-1} g_j)\|_λ^2/τ$，避免了不可约表示、球谐函数或学习函数。在SE(2)、SO(3)和Aff(2)上的实验表明，该方法性能可媲美或超越学习MLP基线，参数量少50至80倍，而向量令牌基线则因失真严重而失败。
Automated Standardization of Legacy Biomedical Metadata Using an Ontology-Constrained LLM Agent
Authors: Josef Hardi, Martin J. O'Connor, Marcos Martinez-Romero, Jean G. Rosario, Stephen A. Fisher, Mark A. Musen
First: 2026-03-10T18:47:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:56:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific metadata are often incomplete and noncompliant with community standards, limiting dataset findability, interoperability, and reuse. Even when standard metadata reporting guidelines exist, they typically lack machine-actionable representations. Producing FAIR datasets requires encoding metadata standards as machine-actionable templates with rich field specifications and precise value constraints. Recent work has shown that LLMs guided by field names and ontology constraints can improve metadata standardization, but these approaches treat constraints as static text prompts, relying on the model's training knowledge alone. We present an LLM-based metadata standardization system that queries standard reporting guidelines and authoritative biomedical terminology services in real time to retrieve canonically correct standards on demand. We evaluate this approach on 839 legacy metadata records from the Human BioMolecular Atlas Program (HuBMAP) using an expert-curated gold standard for exact-match assessment. Our evaluation shows that augmenting the LLM with real-time tool access consistently improves prediction accuracy over the LLM alone across both ontology-constrained and non-ontology-constrained fields, demonstrating a practical approach to automated standardization of biomedical metadata.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用本体约束的大语言模型代理自动标准化遗留生物医学元数据
科学元数据常不完整且不符合社区标准，限制了数据集的可发现性、互操作性和重用性。即使存在标准元数据报告指南，它们通常缺乏机器可操作的表示形式。生成FAIR数据集需要将元数据标准编码为具有丰富字段规范和精确值约束的机器可操作模板。近期研究表明，通过字段名称和本体约束引导的大语言模型（LLM）可提升元数据标准化效果，但这些方法将约束视为静态文本提示，仅依赖模型训练知识。我们提出一种基于LLM的元数据标准化系统，该系统可实时查询标准报告指南和权威生物医学术语服务，按需获取规范正确的标准。我们在人类生物分子图谱计划（HuBMAP）的839条遗留元数据记录上评估该方法，采用专家编纂的黄金标准进行精确匹配评估。评估结果表明，为LLM增加实时工具访问能力，在本体约束和非本体约束字段中均显著提升预测准确性，展示了一种实用的生物医学元数据自动化标准化方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to standardize incomplete and noncompliant biomedical metadata to enable FAIR data practices, this study introduces an LLM-based system that dynamically queries authoritative reporting guidelines and biomedical ontologies to guide metadata standardization. Unlike prior methods that treat constraints as static prompts, the system retrieves canonical standards in real time, enhancing the LLM’s ability to produce accurate, machine-actionable metadata. Evaluated on 839 legacy records from HuBMAP against an expert gold standard, the approach consistently outperformed LLMs without real-time tool access across both ontology-constrained and unconstrained fields, demonstrating its effectiveness for automated metadata standardization.
为解决生物医学元数据不完整、不符合标准导致数据难以发现与重用的问题，本研究提出一种基于LLM的元数据标准化系统，该系统实时查询权威报告指南和生物医学本体库，动态获取标准规范以指导元数据生成。与以往将约束视为静态提示的方法不同，该系统通过实时工具调用增强LLM的准确性。在HuBMAP的839条历史元数据记录上，以专家标注的黄金标准评估，该方法在有无本体约束的字段中均显著优于仅依赖LLM的基线模型，验证了其在生物医学元数据自动化标准化中的有效性。
Predictability as a Fine-Grained Measure for Privacy
Authors: Linda Lu, Karthik Sridharan
First: 2026-06-18T17:55:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:55:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Differential privacy (DP) ensures rigorous individual-level privacy guarantees against even the most knowledgeable attackers, but its worst-case nature can impose a costly privacy-accuracy tradeoff. We introduce privacy via predictability, a fine-grained framework that explicitly incorporates the attacker's core knowledge, a compromised portion of the dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a specified family of queries. Predictability measures privacy leakage as the incremental gain in an attacker's ability to predict sensitive information about unknown individuals after observing the algorithm's output, beyond what can already be inferred from the compromised data. We show that predictability and DP are generally incomparable: each can be small while the other is large. However, in the worst-case regime where all but one individual is compromised, and all binary queries are considered sensitive, predictability implies mutual-information DP. More generally, predictability provides a finer-grained privacy metric tailored to specific sensitive information and specific attacker models. We introduce a general framework, using the generalized method of moments (GMM), to analyze asymptotic predictability when the compromised data is generated by a stationary, ergodic, mixing process. Using this analysis, we derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM. Our approach is complementary to DP and can be used alongside DP to provide fine-grained privacy control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：可预测性作为细粒度隐私度量
差分隐私（DP）能对最知情的攻击者提供严格的个体级隐私保障，但其最坏情况性质可能导致高昂的隐私-准确性权衡。我们引入“可预测性”作为隐私度量，这是一种细粒度框架，明确纳入攻击者的核心知识、由随机过程生成的被泄露数据子集，以及指定的查询族。可预测性将隐私泄露定义为：在观察算法输出后，攻击者预测未知个体敏感信息的能力增量，超出仅从泄露数据中可推断的能力。我们证明，可预测性与DP通常不可比较：二者可同时较小或较大。但在最坏情况（除一人外所有个体均被泄露，且所有二元查询均视为敏感）下，可预测性蕴含互信息DP。更一般地，可预测性提供针对特定敏感信息和特定攻击模型的细粒度隐私度量。我们引入基于广义矩方法（GMM）的通用框架，分析当泄露数据由平稳、遍历、混合过程生成时的渐近可预测性。基于此分析，我们为ERM推导出可预测性校准的输出扰动方案。本方法与DP互补，可与DP结合以实现细粒度隐私控制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of differential privacy’s worst-case guarantees, this work proposes predictability as a fine-grained privacy metric that explicitly models an attacker’s knowledge, a compromised dataset generated by a stochastic process, and a family of queries. Predictability quantifies privacy leakage as the increase in an attacker’s ability to predict sensitive attributes of unseen individuals after observing algorithm outputs, beyond what is already inferable from compromised data. The authors show predictability and DP are generally incomparable, yet predictability implies mutual-information DP in the worst-case setting where nearly all individuals are compromised and all binary queries are sensitive. Using a generalized method of moments framework, they analyze asymptotic predictability for stationary, ergodic, mixing data and derive a predictability-calibrated output perturbation scheme for ERM, offering a complementary, fine-grained privacy control mechanism alongside DP.
为克服差分隐私在最坏情况下的高代价隐私-准确率权衡，本文提出“可预测性”作为更精细的隐私度量，显式建模攻击者知识、由随机过程生成的泄露数据集及查询族。该方法将隐私泄露定义为攻击者在观察算法输出后，对未知个体敏感属性预测能力的增量提升，超出仅凭泄露数据可推断的部分。通过广义矩方法分析，发现可预测性与差分隐私通常不可比较，但在最坏情形下（除一人外全部个体泄露，所有二元查询视为敏感）可预测性蕴含互信息差分隐私。该框架支持对ERM输出进行可预测性校准的扰动，为隐私控制提供互补且更精细的手段。
Current World Models Lack a Persistent State Core
Authors: Jinpeng Lu, Dexu Zhu, Haoyuan Shi, Linghan Cai, Guo Tang, Yinda Chen, Jie Cao, Duyu Tang, Yi Zhang, Yong Dai, Xiaozhu Ju
First: 2026-06-18T17:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:55:15+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 16 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models are increasingly regarded as a decisive step toward artificial general intelligence, yet modeling the physical world demands more than rendering convincing frames on demand: it requires an internal world state that keeps evolving over time, decoupled from observation, so that objects endure and events run to their conclusions whether or not a camera is watching, much as the moon holds to its orbit when no one is looking. This requirement is a blind spot of existing benchmarks, which reward surface properties such as fidelity, motion, and camera controllability while never asking whether a generated world keeps evolving once it is unobserved. We introduce \textbf{WRBench}, the first systematic diagnostic benchmark that treats camera motion as an intervention on observability and resolves evaluation into a human-calibrated chain that asks whether the camera executes the requested interaction, whether the scene stays continuous and identifiable while in view, and whether a returning target remains consistent with the event that was set in motion. Across 9{,}600 videos from 23 models spanning four control paradigms, one finding proves stubborn: current systems maintain the observed world as a tracking shot, resuming a returning target in the state at which it was abandoned rather than advancing the event while it went unseen. Because this failure recurs across control paradigms, model families, and increments of scale, robust world-state evolution does not follow from cleaner imagery, tighter control, richer geometric priors, or sheer parameter count We therefore argue that the stability of the physical state kernel and the consistency of worldlines under viewpoint intervention should become first-class objectives of world-model design, so that a world model captures how the world will unfold rather than how the next frame appears.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当前世界模型缺乏持久的状态核心
世界模型正日益被视为迈向人工通用智能的关键一步，但建模物理世界的要求远不止于按需生成逼真的画面：它需要一个内部世界状态，该状态随时间持续演化，且独立于观测，从而使物体得以持续存在、事件得以自然发展，无论是否被摄像头观察，正如月亮在无人注视时仍遵循其轨道运行。这一要求是现有评估基准的盲点，因为它们仅奖励表面属性（如保真度、运动流畅性、摄像头可控性），却从不检验生成的世界在未被观测时是否持续演化。我们提出了\textbf{WRBench}，这是首个系统性诊断基准，将摄像头运动视为对可观测性的干预，并将评估分解为一条由人类校准的链条，依次检验：摄像头是否执行了请求的交互、场景在被观察期间是否保持连续且可识别、返回目标是否与此前启动的事件保持一致。在涵盖23个模型、跨越四种控制范式的9,600个视频中，一个发现始终顽固存在：当前系统将已观测世界维持为跟踪镜头，在目标返回时仅恢复其被放弃时的状态，而非在未被观测期间推进事件发展。由于该失败在不同控制范式、模型家族及规模增量中反复出现，我们得出结论：更清晰的图像、更精准的控制、更丰富的几何先验或单纯更大的参数量，并不能保证世界状态的稳健演化。因此，我们主张世界模型设计应将物理状态核的稳定性及视角干预下世界线的一致性作为首要目标，使模型捕捉世界如何演化，而非仅关注下一帧如何呈现。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that current world models fail to maintain a persistent internal state that evolves independently of observation, which is essential for realistic physical simulation. To address this, the authors introduce WRBench, a diagnostic benchmark that evaluates whether generated worlds continue evolving coherently when unobserved, by testing camera interventions and scene continuity. Across 9,600 videos from 23 models across four control paradigms, the results consistently show that models revert returning objects to their last observed state rather than advancing events during occlusion, indicating a fundamental lack of persistent world-state dynamics regardless of model scale or complexity.
该论文指出当前世界模型缺乏一个独立于观测而持续演化的内部状态，这是实现真实物理模拟的关键。为此，作者提出了WRBench，首个系统性评估世界模型在相机干预下是否保持场景连续性和事件演进的基准。在覆盖23个模型、9600个视频的测试中，结果一致显示模型在相机离开后重新出现时，会回到目标被离开时的状态，而非继续推进事件。这一失败与模型规模、架构或视觉质量无关，表明世界模型设计应优先保障物理状态的稳定性和世界线的一致性，而非仅追求画面真实感或控制精度。
Toward Calibrated Mixture-of-Experts Under Distribution Shift
Authors: Gina Wong, Drew Prinster, Suchi Saria, Rama Chellappa, Anqi Liu
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:55:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:55:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Calibration aligns a model's predictive uncertainty with the frequencies of its empirical outcomes and is important for understanding and trusting reported probabilities. Recent work shows that enforcing calibration at the level of individual predictors can improve ensemble accuracy and calibration, with mixture-of-experts (MoE) models showing strong empirical improvements in particular; however, the conditions under which calibration helps MoE are not well understood. In this work, we study how MoE models behave under distribution shift, focusing on how routing mechanisms interact with expert-level calibration. We show that expert calibration is sufficient to ensure calibration of the overall model under a broad class of distribution shifts in hard-routed models, but is insufficient for calibrating soft-routed models. To address this, we propose an adversarial reweighting that penalizes calibration errors of the routed aggregate under distribution shift, and we demonstrate that it improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff both on average and on difficult subsets of the data, across model classes, prediction tasks, and distribution shifts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向分布偏移下的校准混合专家模型
校准使模型的预测不确定性与经验结果频率对齐，对于理解与信任报告概率至关重要。近期研究表明，在个体预测器层面强制校准可提升集成模型的准确性和校准性，尤其在混合专家（MoE）模型中表现显著；然而，校准如何帮助MoE的条件尚不明确。本文研究MoE模型在分布偏移下的行为，重点关注路由机制与专家级校准的交互。我们证明，在硬路由模型中，专家级校准足以保证整体模型在广泛分布偏移下的校准性，但在软路由模型中则不足。为此，我们提出一种对抗性重加权方法，惩罚分布偏移下路由聚合的校准误差，并证明该方法在各类模型、预测任务和分布偏移下，均能改善准确率与校准性的权衡，尤其在困难数据子集上表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ensure reliable probabilistic predictions under distribution shifts, this work investigates how calibration at the expert level affects overall model calibration in mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures. It finds that hard-routed MoE models remain calibrated under broad distribution shifts when experts are individually calibrated, but soft-routed models fail to inherit this property. To remedy this, the authors introduce an adversarial reweighting mechanism that penalizes calibration errors in the routed aggregate, which improves the accuracy-calibration tradeoff across diverse models, tasks, and shift conditions.
为提升模型在分布偏移下的预测可靠性，本文研究专家级校准对混合专家模型的影响。研究发现，在硬路由模型中，专家级校准足以保证整体模型在广泛分布偏移下的校准性，但在软路由模型中则无效。为此，作者提出一种对抗性重加权方法，通过惩罚路由聚合的校准误差，有效改善了模型在全局及困难子集上的准确率-校准权衡，适用于多种模型结构、任务和分布偏移场景。
SSD: Spatially Speculative Decoding Accelerates Autoregressive Image Generation
Authors: Shilong Xiang, Zirui Zhang, Lijun Yu, Chengzhi Mao
First: 2026-06-18T17:52:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:52:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autoregressive models excel in visual generation by treating images as 1D sequences of discrete tokens, mirroring language modeling. However, this flattening discards the intrinsic 2D spatial locality of visual signals, creating severe computational bottlenecks during inference. We introduce Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD), a framework that aligns the predictive objective with the natural geometry of images. Rather than predicting only the immediate next token in a 1D sequence, our model simultaneously predicts the adjacent horizontal token and the token directly below it. By capitalizing on this 2D spatial correlation, spatially speculative decoding overcomes the memory wall in visual inference. Our approach accelerates autoregressive image generation by up to 13.3x while maintaining high fidelity on DPG-Bench and GenEval. Our results suggest that respecting the underlying geometry of vision unlocks massive computational efficiencies, paving the way for real-time, high-resolution autoregressive generative models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SSD：空间推测解码加速自回归图像生成
自回归模型通过将图像视为离散标记的1D序列，模仿语言建模，在视觉生成中表现优异。然而，这种扁平化处理忽略了视觉信号固有的2D空间局部性，导致推理时出现严重的计算瓶颈。我们提出了空间推测解码（SSD）框架，使预测目标与图像的自然几何结构对齐。与仅预测1D序列中的下一个标记不同，我们的模型同时预测相邻水平标记和正下方标记。通过利用这种2D空间相关性，空间推测解码克服了视觉推理中的内存墙问题。我们的方法在DPG-Bench和GenEval上保持高保真度的同时，将自回归图像生成速度提升最高达13.3倍。结果表明，尊重视觉的底层几何结构可释放巨大的计算效率，为实时、高分辨率的自回归生成模型铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of autoregressive image generation caused by ignoring 2D spatial structure in 1D token sequences, the authors propose Spatially Speculative Decoding (SSD), which simultaneously predicts the next token and its adjacent horizontal and vertical neighbors to exploit spatial locality. Experiments show SSD accelerates image generation by up to 13.3x while preserving high fidelity on DPG-Bench and GenEval, demonstrating that aligning decoding with image geometry enables significant speedups without sacrificing quality.
为了解决自回归图像生成中因忽略二维空间结构而导致的计算瓶颈，作者提出了空间推测解码（SSD），该方法同时预测当前token及其相邻水平和垂直方向的token，以利用空间相关性。实验表明，SSD在DPG-Bench和GenEval等基准数据集上将生成速度提升最高达13.3倍，同时保持高保真度，证明尊重图像固有几何结构可显著提升效率而不牺牲质量。
CalTennis: Large Multi-View Tennis Video Dataset and Benchmark of Monocular-to-3D Pose Estimation
Authors: Ilona Demler, Xinran Xie, Blake Werner, Anna Szczuka, Pietro Perona
First: 2026-06-18T17:52:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:52:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Caltech Tennis Dataset (CalTennis) is a large-scale video benchmark for evaluating monocular-to-3D pose estimation in the wild. CalTennis comprises over 11 million frames (51 hours) of tennis practice and match play from 40 players, captured with 2-6 synchronized cameras at 60 Hz. It is 10 times larger than existing in-the-wild human motion video datasets and 3 times larger than existing MOCAP-ground-truthed datasets, and it is the first large-scale benchmark to provide synchronized multi-view recordings of expert athletic motion. The multi-view setup enables inexpensive, label-free evaluation of monocular-to-3D pose estimation algorithms. We describe a simple, standardized protocol that enables data collection without specialized equipment or expertise, along with fully automated video calibration and synchronization. Benchmarking state-of-the-art monocular-to-3D pose methods on CalTennis, we find that while 3D joint angle recovery is now quite accurate, all models struggle to estimate depth and foot contact consistently. We further propose two novel performance metrics, footwork and stability, as well as qualitatively study body shape inconsistency. These metrics expose previously underexplored failure modes and point to concrete opportunities for improvement in pose estimation and action analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CalTennis：大规模多视角网球视频数据集与单目到三维姿态估计基准
Caltech网球数据集（CalTennis）是一个用于评估野外单目到三维姿态估计的大规模视频基准。CalTennis包含40名运动员在2至6台同步相机（60Hz）下拍摄的超过1100万帧（51小时）的网球训练与比赛视频。该数据集规模是现有野外人体运动视频数据集的10倍，是现有MOCAP真值数据集的3倍，也是首个提供专家运动员运动同步多视角记录的大规模基准。多视角设置使得单目到三维姿态估计算法能够以低成本、无需标注的方式进行评估。我们提出了一种简单标准化的数据采集协议，无需专业设备或专业知识，同时支持全自动视频校准与同步。在CalTennis上对当前最先进的单目到三维姿态方法进行基准测试发现，虽然三维关节角度恢复已相当准确，但所有模型在深度和脚部接触估计方面仍存在不一致性。我们进一步提出了两个新颖的性能指标——步法与稳定性，并定性研究了身体形状不一致性。这些指标揭示了此前未被充分探索的失败模式，并指出了姿态估计与动作分析的具体改进方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for large-scale, real-world benchmarks to evaluate monocular-to-3D pose estimation, CalTennis introduces a dataset of over 11 million frames from 40 tennis players captured with synchronized multi-view cameras. The dataset enables label-free, inexpensive evaluation by leveraging multi-view geometry, and includes a standardized, automated collection protocol. Experimental results show that while 3D joint angles are accurately recovered, models consistently fail to estimate depth and foot contact reliably. The authors introduce new metrics—footwork and stability—to quantify these failures and highlight gaps in current pose estimation systems, particularly in capturing dynamic athletic motion.
为评估单目到三维姿态估计算法，CalTennis构建了一个包含40名网球运动员、超过1100万帧的多视角视频数据集。该数据集通过同步多视角摄像机采集，支持无需标注的低成本评估。实验表明，虽然3D关节角度恢复准确，但模型在深度估计和脚部接触预测上表现不佳。作者提出“步法”和“稳定性”两个新指标，揭示了此前未被充分研究的失败模式，并指明了姿态估计与动作分析的改进方向。
The Voice Behind the Words: Quantifying Intersectional Bias in SpeechLLMs
Authors: Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish, Christoph Minixhofer, Maria Teleki, James Caverlee, Ondřej Klejch, Peter Bell, Gustav Eje Henter, Éva Székely
First: 2026-03-15T16:54:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:51:14+00:00
Comments: 5 pages, 3 figures, 1 table, Accepted to Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speech Large Language Models (SpeechLLMs) process spoken input directly, retaining cues such as accent and perceived gender that were previously removed in cascaded pipelines. This introduces speaker identity dependent variation in responses. We present a large-scale intersectional evaluation of accent and gender bias in three SpeechLLMs using 2,880 controlled interactions across six English accents and two gender presentations, keeping linguistic content constant through voice cloning. Using pointwise LLM-judge ratings, pairwise comparisons, and Best-Worst Scaling with human validation, we detect recurring directional disparities. Eastern European-accented speech receives lower helpfulness scores, particularly for female-presenting voices. Responses remain polite but differ in helpfulness. While LLM judges capture the directional trend of these biases, human evaluators exhibit significantly higher sensitivity, showing stronger accent-level contrasts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：话语背后的声源：量化语音大语言模型中的交叉性偏见
语音大语言模型（SpeechLLMs）直接处理语音输入，保留了此前在级联管道中被移除的口音和性别感知等线索，从而引入了与说话者身份相关的响应变化。我们对三种SpeechLLMs进行了大规模交叉性评估，通过2880次受控交互，涵盖六种英语口音和两种性别呈现方式，在语音克隆保持语言内容不变的前提下，使用点式LLM裁判评分、成对比较及人类验证的Best-Worst Scaling方法，检测到反复出现的方向性差异：东欧口音语音获得较低的有用性评分，尤其在女性呈现语音中更为明显。响应保持礼貌但有用性存在差异。虽然LLM裁判能捕捉到这些偏见的方向性趋势，但人类评估者表现出显著更高的敏感度，显示出更强的口音层面对比。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how speaker identity—specifically accent and gender—affects responses from Speech Large Language Models, motivated by the fact that these models process speech directly, preserving cues like accent and gender that are stripped in traditional pipelines. The researchers conducted a large-scale intersectional evaluation using 2,880 controlled interactions across six English accents and two gender presentations, maintaining identical linguistic content via voice cloning. They employed pointwise LLM-judge ratings, pairwise comparisons, and Best-Worst Scaling validated by humans to measure bias, finding consistent directional disparities: Eastern European-accented speech, especially when female-presenting, received significantly lower helpfulness ratings despite maintaining politeness. Human evaluators showed markedly greater sensitivity to accent-based differences than LLM judges.
该研究旨在探究口音与性别如何共同影响语音大语言模型的响应，动机在于这些模型直接处理语音输入，保留了此前被移除的口音和性别等说话者特征。研究通过2880次受控交互，覆盖六种英语口音和两种性别呈现方式（语音克隆保持语言内容一致），采用LLM裁判评分、成对比较及人类验证的Best-Worst Scaling方法进行评估。结果发现存在一致的方向性偏差：东欧口音尤其在女性呈现时，获得的帮助性评分显著偏低，尽管回应仍保持礼貌。LLM裁判能捕捉到偏差趋势，但人类评估者对口音差异的敏感度明显更高。
Multi-Task Bayesian In-Context Learning
Authors: Qingyang Zhu, Eric Karl Oermann, Kyunghyun Cho
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:50:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:50:10+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Bayesian predictive inference provides a principled framework for uncertainty quantification, data efficiency, and robust generalization. However, exact inference is often intractable, and scalable approximations may remain computationally expensive or require restrictive modeling assumptions that degrade predictive performance. Prior-Data Fitted and in-context models have recently emerged as an amortized alternative by learning to map datasets directly to predictive distributions, but existing approaches are tightly coupled to the support of the training prior and lack explicit mechanisms for adapting to new priors at test time, resulting in limited robustness under distribution shift. We introduce a multi-task in-context learning framework for amortized hierarchical Bayesian predictive inference that explicitly represents prior information as a prefix of in-context datasets. A transformer trained on sequences of prior and target tasks learns to adapt its predictions across families of priors. On a suite of evaluations with increasing difficulty, including out-of-meta-distribution priors and priors with high-dimensional latent structures, our method matches oracle Bayesian predictors while being orders of magnitude faster. We further demonstrate its practical relevance on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark. Code is available at https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多任务贝叶斯上下文学习
贝叶斯预测推断提供了一种用于不确定性量化、数据效率和鲁棒泛化的原理性框架。然而，精确推断往往不可行，而可扩展的近似方法可能计算成本高昂或需要限制性的建模假设，从而降低预测性能。近期出现的先验-数据拟合和上下文模型作为一种摊销替代方案，通过直接将数据集映射到预测分布，但现有方法紧密依赖训练先验的支持集，缺乏在测试时显式适应新先验的机制，导致在分布偏移下鲁棒性有限。我们提出了一种用于摊销分层贝叶斯预测推断的多任务上下文学习框架，显式将先验信息表示为上下文数据集的前缀。一个在先验与目标任务序列上训练的Transformer模型，能够学习在不同先验家族间自适应调整预测。在一系列难度递增的评估中，包括超出元分布的先验和具有高维潜在结构的先验，我们的方法在速度上快几个数量级的同时，与理想贝叶斯预测器表现相当。我们还在现实世界时空温度预测基准上展示了其实际应用价值。代码见 https://github.com/martianmartina/multi-task-bayesian-icl/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable and robust Bayesian inference, this work proposes a multi-task in-context learning framework that learns to map prior-data sequences to predictive distributions using a transformer architecture. The method explicitly encodes prior information as a prefix in input sequences, enabling the model to adapt predictions across different prior families without retraining. Experiments show it matches oracle Bayesian performance on challenging tasks involving out-of-meta-distribution priors and high-dimensional latent structures, while being orders of magnitude faster than exact Bayesian methods. It also performs well on a real-world spatiotemporal temperature prediction benchmark, demonstrating practical utility.
为实现可扩展且鲁棒的贝叶斯推断，本文提出一种多任务上下文学习框架，通过将先验信息作为输入序列前缀，使模型能适应不同先验家族的预测分布。采用Transformer架构在先验与目标任务序列上训练，实现分层贝叶斯推断而无需显式后验采样。实验表明，该方法在包含元分布外先验和高维潜在结构的挑战性基准上，性能可媲美理想贝叶斯预测器，且速度提升数个数量级，并在真实时空温度预测任务中验证了实用性。
Execution-State Capsules: Graph-Bound Execution-State Checkpoint and Restore for Low-Latency, Small-Batch, On-Device Physical-AI Serving
Authors: Liang Su
First: 2026-06-18T17:49:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:49:36+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mainstream LLM serving systems reuse prefix work mainly through paged or radix key-value (KV) caches. This is highly effective for high-throughput, high-concurrency serving, but it manages only one positional fragment of execution state: the KV cache. We study the opposite regime: low-latency, small-batch, on-device physical-AI serving, where interactive LLM agents, speech systems, and robot policies repeatedly branch, reset, interrupt, and re-enter under tight responsiveness budgets. We introduce execution-state capsules, a graph-bound checkpoint and restore mechanism for the complete restorable state at a committed boundary. FlashRT is a white-box, backend-facing kernel runtime whose evaluated NVIDIA CUDA backend runs captured graph plans over contiguous static buffers with no block-table indirection. Because the live state is a closed set of named buffers, a capsule can snapshot, restore, fork, or roll back the whole execution boundary, including KV, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. This moves reuse from token-addressed KV fragments to graph-bound execution-state boundaries. On an RTX 5090, capsule restore is byte-exact at the stored-state level and token-identical under greedy decode. A KV-only ablation diverges, showing that recurrent state is load-bearing. GPU-resident snapshot and restore are sub-millisecond, and TTFT speedup over cold prefill grows from 3.9x at 2k tokens to 27x at 16k tokens. On Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark, the same correctness and structural properties hold. Capsules are not a replacement for high-throughput KV-cache serving; they define a complementary latency-first serving point for explicit execution-state reuse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：执行状态胶囊：面向低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务的图绑定执行状态检查点与恢复
主流大语言模型（LLM）服务系统主要通过分页或基数键值（KV）缓存重用前缀工作，这对高吞吐、高并发服务非常有效，但仅管理执行状态的一个位置片段：KV缓存。我们研究相反的场景：低延迟、小批量、设备端物理AI服务，其中交互式LLM代理、语音系统和机器人策略需在严格的响应预算下反复分支、重置、中断和重新进入。我们引入执行状态胶囊，一种图绑定的检查点与恢复机制，用于在已承诺边界处完整恢复执行状态。FlashRT 是一个白盒、面向后端的内核运行时，其评估的 NVIDIA CUDA 后端在连续静态缓冲区上运行捕获的图计划，无需块表间接寻址。由于实时状态是命名缓冲区的封闭集合，胶囊可对整个执行边界进行快照、恢复、分叉或回滚，包括 KV、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP 状态和元数据。这将重用从基于令牌的 KV 片段转向图绑定的执行状态边界。在 RTX 5090 上，胶囊恢复在存储状态层面字节精确，在贪心解码下令牌完全一致。仅 KV 的消融实验显示，循环状态是关键负载。GPU 居留的快照与恢复耗时低于毫秒，TTFT 加速从 2k 令牌时的 3.9 倍提升至 16k 令牌时的 27 倍。在 Jetson AGX Thor 和 DGX Spark 上，相同的正确性与结构属性同样成立。胶囊并非高吞吐 KV 缓存服务的替代方案，而是定义了以延迟优先、显式执行状态重用的互补服务点。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for low-latency, small-batch, on-device AI serving where execution states must be frequently checkpointed and restored, the paper introduces execution-state capsules—a graph-bound mechanism that captures and restores the complete execution state including KV cache, recurrent state, convolution state, MTP state, and metadata. Implemented via FlashRT, a backend-facing kernel runtime optimized for NVIDIA CUDA, capsules enable byte-exact restore and token-identical decoding under greedy decoding, with sub-millisecond snapshot and restore times on RTX 5090. Experimental results show TTFT speedups ranging from 3.9x to 27x across token lengths from 2k to 16k, confirming recurrent state’s critical role in performance and demonstrating correctness and structural consistency on Jetson AGX Thor and DGX Spark.
为满足低延迟、小批量、边缘设备上AI服务对执行状态频繁快照与恢复的需求，本文提出执行状态胶囊（execution-state capsules），一种基于图结构的完整执行状态快照与恢复机制，涵盖KV缓存、循环状态、卷积状态、MTP状态及元数据。通过FlashRT白盒内核运行时（针对NVIDIA CUDA优化），胶囊在连续静态缓冲区上操作，无需块表间接寻址，实现字节级精确恢复与贪心解码下的词级一致性。在RTX 5090上，快照与恢复耗时低于毫秒，TTFT加速比从2k词长时的3.9倍提升至16k词长时的27倍，且在Jetson AGX Thor与DGX Spark上保持正确性。该方法作为传统KV缓存服务的补充，专为延迟敏感型工作负载设计。
The FID Lottery: Quantifying Hidden Randomness in Generative-Model Evaluation
Authors: Nicolas Dufour, Alexei A. Efros, Patrick Pérez
First: 2026-06-18T17:49:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:49:32+00:00
Comments: Website: https://kyutai.org/fid-lottery
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Frechet Inception Distance (FID) is the de facto arbiter of image generation, yet most papers report just a single number from a single trained model using a single sampling seed. How reproducible is that number if we retrain the model, or merely resample from it? In this paper, we treat FID as a random variable on a two-axis panel of training and generation seeds, and measure its variance directly on several hundred SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet 256x256. We report surprising findings: (a) Retraining the model using the same recipe with a different seed moves FID 3.2x more (in Inception feature space) than redrawing samples from a fixed network. (b) That gap is driven by three factors: random initialisation, data ordering, and the per-step Gaussian noise of the flow-matching loss. (c) Increasing compute or model size barely tightens the spread, holding the FID coefficient of variation (CoV) inside a 1-2% band. (d) Per-cell classifier-free-guidance tuning halves the spread but reshuffles which seeds work best, and a lucky training seed reaches the same FID with up to 2x less compute than an unlucky one. Based on these findings, we recommend a new FID evaluation protocol: evaluate under per-cell optimal guidance, treat any FID gap below the empirically measured ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report an error bar over several training seeds rather than a single FID number.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FID抽奖：量化生成模型评估中的隐藏随机性
弗雷歇Inception距离（FID）是图像生成的权威评估指标，但多数论文仅报告单一训练模型在单一采样种子下的单个数值。若重新训练模型或仅重新采样，该数值的可重复性如何？本文将FID视为训练种子与生成种子构成的二维面板上的随机变量，并在数百个在类条件ImageNet 256x256上训练的SiT网络上直接测量其方差。我们报告了惊人发现：(a) 使用相同配方但不同种子重新训练模型，其FID在Inception特征空间中变化幅度是固定网络重采样的3.2倍。(b) 该差距由三个因素驱动：随机初始化、数据排序及流匹配损失的每步高斯噪声。(c) 增加计算量或模型规模几乎无法缩小分布范围，FID的变异系数（CoV）始终维持在1-2%区间。(d) 每单元分类器自由引导调优可将分布范围减半，但会重新分配最优种子；幸运训练种子可比不幸种子节省高达2倍计算量达到相同FID。基于这些发现，我们建议新的FID评估协议：在每单元最优引导下评估，将实测约1.3% CoV以下的FID差距视为无结论，并报告多个训练种子的误差范围而非单一FID数值。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the variability of Frechet Inception Distance (FID) scores in generative models, motivated by the lack of reproducibility when evaluating image generation quality. The authors treat FID as a random variable influenced by both training and sampling seeds, measuring its variance across hundreds of SiT networks trained on class-conditional ImageNet. Key findings reveal that retraining a model with a different seed causes FID to vary 3.2x more than resampling from a fixed model, driven by random initialization, data ordering, and noise in the flow-matching loss. Increasing model size or compute barely reduces this variance, which remains within a 1-2% coefficient of variation. Classifier-free guidance tuning halves the spread but shifts optimal seeds, and lucky seeds can achieve the same FID with up to twice less compute. The authors thus propose a new evaluation protocol: use per-cell optimal guidance, treat FID gaps below ~1.3% CoV as inconclusive, and report error bars over multiple training seeds rather than a single number.
该论文研究生成模型中Frechet Inception Distance（FID）分数的变异性，动机在于单个FID值缺乏可重复性。作者将FID视为不同训练和采样种子下的随机变量，在数百个SiT模型上测量其方差，这些模型在类条件ImageNet 256x256上训练。主要发现包括：使用新种子重训练导致FID变化幅度是固定模型重采样的3.2倍，主要由随机初始化、数据顺序和流匹配损失中的高斯噪声驱动。增加模型规模或计算量对降低方差影响甚微，FID的变异系数维持在1-2%区间。分类器自由引导调参可将方差减半，但会改变最优种子分布，幸运种子可仅用一半计算量达到相同FID。作者建议采用每单元最优引导评估FID，将低于约1.3%变异系数的FID差异视为无结论，并报告多个种子的误差范围而非单一数值。
How Do Instructions Shape Speech? Cross-Attention Attribution for Style-Captioned Text-to-Speech
Authors: Nityanand Mathur, Hamees Sayed, Wasim Madha, Apoorv Singh, Sameer Khurana, Akshat Mandloi, Sudarshan Kamath
First: 2026-06-18T17:47:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:47:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Style-captioned text-to-speech systems use natural language to control voice characteristics, but how individual words influence acoustic output remains unclear. Understanding this is critical for diagnosing failure modes and improving controllability in expressive TTS. We propose cross-attention attribution for speech diffusion models, adapting the DAAM framework to the speech domain for the first time, and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS. Our method extracts per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps. We analyze 3,600 (style caption, text transcript) combinations comprising 120 style captions conditioning the generation of 30 text transcripts each, revealing how caption tokens shape waveforms. Results show: (1) style tokens have lower temporal variance than content/function tokens, confirming global conditioning; (2) style attention correlates with F0 and energy; (3) style conditioning peaks in early steps and deep layers; (4) attention entropy reaches its minimum at layer 17, co-occurring with the style importance peak, indicating maximal network selectivity at the most style-critical stage. This is the first study of how natural language influences cross-attention in speech diffusion models
中文标题/摘要
标题：指令如何塑造语音？面向风格描述文本到语音的跨注意力归因
风格描述文本到语音系统利用自然语言控制语音特征，但单个词如何影响声学输出仍不明确。理解这一点对诊断失败模式和提升表达性TTS的可控性至关重要。我们提出针对语音扩散模型的跨注意力归因方法，首次将DAAM框架适配至语音领域，并应用于CapSpeech-TTS。该方法提取了跨25层和24个ODE步骤的每个词热图。我们分析了3600组（风格描述，文本转录）组合，每组包含120个风格描述条件生成30个文本转录，揭示风格描述词如何塑造波形。结果表明：(1) 风格词的时间方差低于内容/功能词，证实全局条件控制；(2) 风格注意力与基频F0和能量相关；(3) 风格条件在早期步骤和深层网络中达到峰值；(4) 注意力熵在第17层达到最小值，与风格重要性峰值同时出现，表明网络在最风格关键阶段具有最大选择性。这是首次研究自然语言如何影响语音扩散模型中的跨注意力。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how natural language instructions influence acoustic output in style-captioned text-to-speech systems, motivated by the need to understand and improve controllability in expressive speech synthesis. The authors adapt the DAAM framework to speech diffusion models and apply it to CapSpeech-TTS, generating per-token heatmaps across 25 layers and 24 ODE steps for 3,600 style-caption-text combinations. Results show that style tokens exhibit lower temporal variance than content tokens, correlate with pitch (F0) and energy, peak in early diffusion steps and deep layers, and reach maximal network selectivity at layer 17, where attention entropy is minimized — indicating the most critical stage for style influence.
该研究旨在探究自然语言指令如何影响语音扩散模型的声学输出，以提升可控性并诊断系统失效模式。研究首次将DAAM框架适配至语音扩散模型，应用于CapSpeech-TTS，通过跨注意力归因方法生成25层和24步的逐词热图。对3600组（风格描述，文本转录）样本的分析表明：风格词的时间方差低于内容词，与基频和能量相关，其注意力在早期步骤和深层达到峰值，且在第17层注意力熵最低，表明该层对风格特征的选择性最强。
Latent Gaussian Splatting for 4D Panoptic Occupancy Tracking
Authors: Maximilian Luz, Rohit Mohan, Thomas Nürnberg, Yakov Miron, Daniele Cattaneo, Abhinav Valada
First: 2026-02-26T16:34:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:46:09+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Capturing 4D spatiotemporal scene structure is crucial for the safe and reliable operation of robots in dynamic environments. However, existing approaches typically address only part of the problem: they either provide coarse geometric tracking via bounding boxes or detailed 3D occupancy estimates that lack explicit temporal association and instance-level reasoning. In this work, we present Latent Gaussian Splatting (LaGS) for 4D Panoptic Occupancy Tracking (4D-POT). We revisit the underlying representation and model 3D features as a sparse set of feature-bearing Gaussians. These act as dynamic, volume-oriented keypoints that enable spatially continuous, distance-weighted aggregation of multi-view features before being splatted into a voxel grid for decoding. This point-centric formulation enables flexible, data-dependent receptive fields and long-range spatial interactions that are difficult to capture with local and dense voxel-based operators. A hierarchical Gaussian representation further enables multi-scale reasoning by combining global context from coarse super-points with fine-grained detail from higher-resolution streams. Extensive experiments on Occ3D nuScenes and Waymo demonstrate state-of-the-art performance for 4D-POT. We provide code and models at https://lags.cs.uni-freiburg.de/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于4D全景占据跟踪的潜在高斯点云
捕捉4D时空场景结构对于机器人在动态环境中的安全可靠运行至关重要。然而，现有方法通常仅解决部分问题：它们要么通过边界框提供粗略的几何跟踪，要么提供缺乏显式时间关联和实例级推理的详细3D占据估计。在本工作中，我们提出了用于4D全景占据跟踪（4D-POT）的潜在高斯点云（LaGS）。我们重新审视了底层表示方法，将3D特征建模为一组稀疏的携带特征的高斯点。这些高斯点作为动态、面向体积的关键点，能够在被映射到体素网格解码前，对多视角特征进行空间连续、距离加权的聚合。这种以点为中心的表述方式，支持灵活、数据依赖的接收域和难以通过局部密集体素操作捕捉的长程空间交互。分层高斯表示进一步通过结合粗粒度超级点的全局上下文与高分辨率流的细粒度细节，实现多尺度推理。在Occ3D nuScenes和Waymo上的大量实验表明，LaGS在4D-POT任务上达到当前最优性能。代码和模型可在https://lags.cs.uni-freiburg.de/获取。
Summary / 总结
To enable robots to safely navigate dynamic environments, this work addresses the challenge of capturing 4D spatiotemporal scene structure, which existing methods either approximate coarsely or lack temporal and instance-level reasoning. Latent Gaussian Splatting (LaGS) models 3D features as sparse, feature-bearing Gaussians acting as dynamic keypoints, allowing spatially continuous, distance-weighted aggregation of multi-view inputs before voxel-grid decoding. This point-centric approach supports flexible receptive fields and long-range interactions, while a hierarchical Gaussian structure enables multi-scale reasoning by integrating coarse global context with fine-grained detail. Experiments on Occ3D nuScenes and Waymo show LaGS achieves state-of-the-art performance in 4D panoptic occupancy tracking.
为使机器人在动态环境中安全运行，本文旨在解决捕捉四维时空场景结构的挑战，现有方法或仅粗略建模，或缺乏时序与实例级推理能力。Latent Gaussian Splatting（LaGS）将3D特征建模为稀疏动态高斯点，作为体积导向的关键点，通过距离加权空间融合多视角特征后进行体素网格解码。该点中心化方法支持灵活感受野与长程交互，结合层次化高斯结构，融合粗粒度全局上下文与细粒度细节。在Occ3D nuScenes和Waymo数据集上的实验表明，LaGS在四维全景占用跟踪任务中达到最先进性能。
VisDom: Sparse Novel View Synthesis with Visible Domain Constraint
Authors: Mariia Gladkova*, Tarun Yenamandra*, Edmond Boyer, Robert Maier, Tony Tung, Daniel Cremers
First: 2026-06-18T17:45:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:45:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse novel view synthesis (NVS) remains challenging due to the ambiguity of recovering 3D geometry from few input views. While NeRF- and Gaussian Splatting (GS)-based methods perform well with dense supervision, they often overfit in sparse settings, producing floating artifacts and inconsistent geometry. Silhouette consistency is commonly used as a regularizer, but it remains insufficient, as silhouette-consistent regions can extend beyond the true object geometry. We introduce VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that augments classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement. Specifically, we define a visible domain as the subset of 3D space observed by at least $K$ views and use it as an additional filtering criterion on top of standard silhouette-based reconstruction. This provides a stronger spatial prior in sparse-view settings. We integrate VisDom into both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (GS) pipelines by restricting volumetric sampling and guiding Gaussian placement during optimization. Experiments on three challenging datasets show consistent improvements in sparse-view NVS, enabling high-quality object-centric reconstruction from as few as four input images. Our method is domain-agnostic, requires only silhouettes, and introduces no learned parameters, making it a simple complement to existing approaches. Applying VisDom on top of GaussianObject further improves performance on Omni3D and MipNeRF360, while matching or surpassing it at 22 $\times$ lower training cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VisDom：基于可见域约束的稀疏新视角合成
由于从少量输入视角恢复3D几何存在歧义，稀疏新视角合成（NVS）仍具挑战性。虽然基于NeRF和高斯点绘（GS）的方法在密集监督下表现良好，但在稀疏设置中常过拟合，产生漂浮伪影和不一致的几何结构。轮廓一致性常被用作正则化器，但其仍不充分，因为轮廓一致区域可能超出真实物体几何范围。我们提出VisDom，一种无学习的几何约束，通过强制执行最小多视角可见性要求，增强传统基于雕刻的视觉体重建。具体而言，我们将可见域定义为至少被K个视角观测到的3D空间子集，并将其作为标准轮廓重建基础上的额外过滤准则。这在稀疏视角设置中提供了更强的空间先验。我们通过限制体素采样和引导高斯点放置，将VisDom集成到隐式（NeRF）和显式（GS）流程中。在三个具有挑战性的数据集上的实验表明，VisDom在稀疏视角NVS中持续提升性能，仅需四张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建。该方法与领域无关，仅需轮廓，且不引入任何学习参数，是现有方法的简单补充。将VisDom应用于GaussianObject，进一步提升了其在Omni3D和MipNeRF360上的性能，同时在训练成本低22倍的情况下达到或超越其表现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of sparse novel view synthesis due to ambiguous 3D geometry recovery from few views, the paper proposes VisDom, a learning-free geometric constraint that enhances classical carving-based visual hull reconstruction by enforcing a minimum multi-view visibility requirement.
