为了解决稀疏视角下3D几何恢复困难的问题，本文提出VisDom，一种无需学习的几何约束方法，通过强制要求至少K个视角可见的3D空间区域，增强传统基于轮廓的视觉体重建。该方法将可见域作为额外筛选标准，强化空间先验。VisDom可集成至NeRF与Gaussian Splatting流程中，通过限制采样与引导高斯点分布，显著提升稀疏视角下的新视角合成质量。在三个基准数据集上实验表明，仅需四张输入图像即可实现高质量物体中心重建，且在GaussianObject等方法上进一步提升性能，同时训练成本降低22倍。
LedgerAgent: Structured State for Policy-Adherent Tool-Calling Agents
Authors: Md Nayem Uddin, Amir Saeidi, Eduardo Blanco, Chitta Baral
First: 2026-06-18T17:41:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:41:56+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Policy-adherent tool-calling agents in customer-service domains must maintain task states across turns while calling tools and obeying domain policies. Task states consist of relevant facts, identifiers, constraints, and conditions observed through user interaction and tool calls. In standard agents, task states are not represented separately. Observations, tool returns, and policy instructions are placed in the prompt, leaving agents to reconstruct the relevant states from the prompt each time they decide what to do next. This design makes state management implicit, creating two common failure modes. An agent may retrieve the right facts but later ground its decision in stale, missing, or incorrect information; and a syntactically valid tool call may still violate a domain policy that depends on the current task state. We introduce \textsc{LedgerAgent}, an inference-time method for tool-calling agents that maintains observed task states in a separate ledger and renders the states into the prompt. The ledger is also used to check state-dependent policy constraints before environment-changing tool calls are executed, blocking policy violations. Across four customer-service domains and a mixed panel of open- and closed-weight models, \textsc{LedgerAgent} improves average pass\textasciicircum{}k over a standard prompt-based tool-calling approach, with the largest gains under stricter multi-trial consistency metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LedgerAgent：面向策略合规工具调用代理的结构化状态管理
在客户服务领域，策略合规的工具调用代理必须在多次交互中维护任务状态，同时调用工具并遵守领域策略。任务状态包括通过用户交互和工具调用观察到的相关事实、标识符、约束和条件。在标准代理中，任务状态未单独表示，观察结果、工具返回和策略指令均置于提示中，代理每次决策时需从提示中重构相关状态。这种设计使状态管理隐式化，导致两种常见失败模式：代理可能正确检索事实，但后续决策基于过时、缺失或错误的信息；或语法合法的工具调用仍可能违反依赖当前任务状态的领域策略。我们提出\textsc{LedgerAgent}，一种推理时方法，通过独立账本维护观察到的任务状态，并将其渲染至提示中。账本还用于在执行改变环境的工具调用前检查依赖状态的策略约束，阻止策略违规。在四个客户服务领域及混合开放与闭源模型的测试中，\textsc{LedgerAgent}相较标准提示驱动的工具调用方法显著提升平均通过率，尤其在更严格的多轮一致性指标下收益最大。
Summary / 总结
To address the failure modes of implicit state management in policy-adherent tool-calling agents, LedgerAgent introduces a structured ledger that explicitly maintains task states—such as facts, identifiers, and constraints—separate from the prompt. This ledger is rendered into the prompt for decision-making and used to validate state-dependent policy constraints before executing environment-changing tool calls. Experiments across four customer-service domains and multiple model types show that LedgerAgent consistently outperforms standard prompt-based agents, with the largest improvements observed under strict multi-trial consistency metrics.
为了解决客服领域政策合规工具调用代理中状态管理隐式导致的错误，作者提出LedgerAgent方法，在推理时将任务状态显式存入独立账本，并在提示中呈现状态，同时在执行环境改变型工具调用前用账本验证政策约束。在四个客服领域和多种模型上实验表明，该方法相比传统基于提示的代理显著提升平均pass^k表现，尤其在严格多轮一致性指标下收益最大。
StylisticBias: A Few Human Visual Cues Drive Most Social Biases in MLLMs
Authors: Shaghayegh Kolli, Timo Cavelius, Nafiseh Nikeghbal, Samantha Dalal, Jana Diesner
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:39:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:39:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the non-archival workshops AI4Good and Culture x AI at ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly deployed in personally and societally consequential settings, yet the visual cues that shape how these models judge people remain poorly understood. Prior work often compares different (groups of) individuals, making it difficult to separate appearance effects from identity differences. We introduce StylisticBias, a controlled benchmark for evaluating attribute-level social bias in MLLMs. We generate 500 photorealistic base faces and create about 50 single-attribute variations per face, producing about 25K images. This design keeps identity fixed and changes one visual attribute at a time. It lets us measure how specific cues shift model judgments. We evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment scenarios. We find that age and body type dominate identity-level effects, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. We further find that about 15 attributes account for nearly 80\% of the total variation, showing that bias is concentrated in a small set of visual cues. Sensitivity is strongest in judgments that are semantically aligned with appearance, especially socioeconomic and style-related judgments. We release StylisticBias as a benchmark for fine-grained bias evaluation in multimodal models. Code and dataset: https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias and https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset.
中文标题/摘要
标题：风格偏见：少数人类视觉线索驱动MLLMs中的大部分社会偏见
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正越来越多地部署在个人和社会后果重大的场景中，但塑造这些模型如何评判人的视觉线索仍不甚明了。以往研究常比较不同（群体）个体，难以区分外貌效应与身份差异。我们引入StylisticBias，一个用于评估MLLMs属性层面社会偏见的受控基准。我们生成500张逼真基础人脸，并为每张脸创建约50种单属性变体，共生成约2.5万张图像。该设计固定身份，每次仅改变一个视觉属性，从而可测量特定线索如何影响模型判断。我们在25个二元社会判断场景中评估六种MLLMs。发现年龄和体型主导身份层面效应，而时尚风格及其他视觉线索驱动属性层面最大变化。进一步发现约15个属性解释了总变异的近80%，表明偏见集中于少数视觉线索。对与外貌语义对齐的判断（尤其是社会经济和风格相关判断）敏感性最强。我们发布StylisticBias作为多模态模型细粒度偏见评估的基准。代码与数据集：https://github.com/timo-cavelius/StylisticBias 和 https://hf.co/datasets/shaghayegh/stylistic-bias-dataset。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the lack of understanding about how visual cues influence social bias in multimodal large language models (MLLMs). They evaluate six MLLMs across 25 binary social judgment tasks and find that age and body type exert the strongest identity-level bias, while fashion style and other visual cues drive the largest attribute-level shifts. Crucially, only about 15 visual attributes account for nearly 80% of total bias variation, indicating bias is concentrated in a small set of cues, with highest sensitivity in judgments aligned with appearance, such as socioeconomic and style-related assessments.
该研究旨在解决多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中视觉线索如何影响社会判断这一关键问题，因为这些模型正日益应用于具有重大社会影响的场景。作者提出了StylisticBias基准测试，通过生成25,000张人脸图像，每次仅改变一个视觉属性而保持身份不变，从而控制变量。在对六种MLLMs进行25项二元社会判断任务评估后发现，年龄和体型对身份层面的偏见影响最大，而时尚风格等视觉线索则主导属性层面的判断变化。更重要的是，仅约15个视觉属性就解释了近80%的总偏见变异，表明偏见高度集中于少数视觉特征，且在与外貌语义对齐的判断（如社会经济和风格相关判断）中敏感性最强。
DeepSWIP: Quotient-WMC Counterfactuals for Neural Probabilistic Logic Programs
Authors: Saimun Habib, Vaishak Belle, Fengxiang He
First: 2026-06-18T17:39:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:39:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Neurosymbolic systems such as DeepProbLog combine neural perception with probabilistic logic, but standard inference is associational. Counterfactual reasoning additionally requires a causal semantics for interventions and evidence. We introduce DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics for DeepProbLog programs. Using neural materialization, we reduce fixed-context neural predicates to ordinary ProbLog choices, apply Single World Intervention Programs (SWIPs), and compute counterfactuals by weighted model counting (WMC) over a single transformed program. Under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions, DeepSWIP is exact relative to the learned materialized FCM. The standard quotient-WMC form of ProbLog conditionals identifies active neural probabilities and explains intervention cleaning, calibration sensitivity, and rare-evidence instability. Experiments on MPI3D confirm the transformation against a DeepTwin construction against 12,000 queries, as predicted and a 2.14$\times$ inference speedup from avoiding the Twin's endogenous duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment shows that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a correctly scoped randomized-policy AIPW estimator removes most first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands. Code is at https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepSWIP：用于神经概率逻辑程序的商数-WMC反事实语义
神经符号系统如DeepProbLog结合了神经感知与概率逻辑，但标准推理是关联性的。反事实推理还需干预和证据的因果语义。我们引入DeepSWIP，为DeepProbLog程序提供单世界反事实语义。通过神经材料化，我们将固定上下文的神经谓词简化为普通ProbLog选择，应用单世界干预程序（SWIP），并通过单个变换程序上的加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实。在有限接地和唯一支持模型假设下，DeepSWIP相对于学习到的材料化FCM是精确的。ProbLog条件式的标准商数-WMC形式识别活跃神经概率，并解释干预清理、校准敏感性和稀有证据不稳定性。在MPI3D上的实验验证了该变换，与DeepTwin构造相比，在12,000个查询中预测准确，且避免Twin内生复制带来2.14倍推理加速。SUMO HOV实验表明，神经校准退化会偏倚插值估计，而正确范围的随机化策略AIPW估计量可消除大部分总体均值和ATE估计量的一阶偏差。代码见https://github.com/saibib/deep_SWIP。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for causal counterfactual reasoning in neurosymbolic systems like DeepProbLog, this work introduces DeepSWIP, a single-world counterfactual semantics that integrates neural materialization with ProbLog’s weighted model counting (WMC). By transforming neural predicates into discrete ProbLog choices and applying Single World Intervention Programs, DeepSWIP computes exact counterfactuals under finite grounding and unique-supported-model assumptions relative to the learned FCM. Experiments on MPI3D validate the transformation against a DeepTwin baseline across 12,000 queries, achieving a 2.14x inference speedup by avoiding redundant neural duplication. A SUMO HOV experiment further demonstrates that neural calibration degradation biases plug-in estimates, while a randomized-policy AIPW estimator effectively removes first-order bias for population mean and ATE estimands.
为在神经符号系统（如DeepProbLog）中实现因果反事实推理，本文提出DeepSWIP，一种单世界反事实语义框架。该方法通过神经材料化将神经谓词转化为离散ProbLog选择，应用单世界干预程序（SWIP），并基于加权模型计数（WMC）计算反事实。在有限接地与唯一支持模型假设下，DeepSWIP相对于学习到的材料化FCM可获得精确结果。MPI3D实验表明，该方法在12,000个查询中与DeepTwin预测一致，并通过避免冗余神经复制实现2.14倍推理加速。SUMO HOV实验显示，神经校准退化会偏倚插值估计，但基于随机策略的AIPW估计器可有效消除对总体均值和ATE估计量的首阶偏差。
SARLO-80: Worldwide Slant SAR Language Optic Dataset 80cm
Authors: Solène Debuysère, Nicolas Trouvé, Nathan Letheule, Elise Colin, Georgia Channing
First: 2026-06-18T17:38:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:38:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal foundation models have advanced rapidly thanks to large optical benchmarks, but comparable resources for synthetic aperture radar (SAR) remain limited. Existing SAR--optical datasets largely rely on low-resolution, intensity-only Ground Range Detected~(GRD) products and do not preserve complex-valued SAR measurements or native acquisition geometry, which restricts physically grounded multimodal learning. In particular, large-scale public datasets combining very-high-resolution (VHR) SAR SLC, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions are still lacking. We present a VHR SAR--optical--text dataset built from open-access Umbra spotlight acquisitions distributed as Sensor Independent Complex Data (SICD). From around 2,500 worldwide scenes (VV/HH, 20cm--2m native resolution), we standardize all SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling and tile the imagery into 1024 by 1024 patches. For each SAR patch, we retrieve a high-resolution optical tile and warp it into the SAR grid using local coordinate correspondences for local pixel-level alignment. We further generate three caption variants (SHORT/MID/LONG) per sample to support vision--language training and evaluation. Our dataset contains 119,566 triplets (complex and amplitude slant-range SAR patch, aligned optical patch, natural-language description) covering 257 locations across 72 countries and a broad range of land types and infrastructures. We release fixed train/validation/test splits and the full preprocessing and baseline code to enable reproducible benchmarks for multimodal alignment on cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry. The dataset is publicly available on the Hugging Face Hub at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SARLO-80：全球斜距SAR语言光学数据集（80厘米）
得益于大型光学基准数据集，多模态基础模型发展迅速，但合成孔径雷达（SAR）领域的同类资源仍十分有限。现有SAR-光学数据集主要依赖低分辨率、仅强度信息的地面距离检测（GRD）产品，未保留复数值SAR测量或原始获取几何信息，这限制了物理基础的多模态学习。特别是，结合高分辨率（VHR）SAR SLC、对齐光学影像和自然语言描述的大规模公开数据集仍属空白。我们基于开放获取的Umbra聚束模式采集数据（以传感器独立复数数据SICD格式分发），构建了该VHR SAR-光学-文本数据集。从约2500个全球场景（VV/HH，原生分辨率20厘米至2米）出发，我们通过带限FFT重采样将所有SAR数据标准化为80厘米斜距网格，并将影像切分为1024×1024块。对每个SAR块，我们检索高分辨率光学块，并利用局部坐标对应关系将其变形至SAR网格，实现像素级局部对齐。我们进一步为每个样本生成三种描述变体（SHORT/MID/LONG），以支持视觉-语言训练与评估。该数据集包含119,566组三元组（复数与幅度斜距SAR块、对齐光学块、自然语言描述），覆盖72个国家的257个地点，涵盖广泛的土地类型与基础设施。我们提供固定划分的训练/验证/测试集及完整预处理与基线代码，以支持跨模态检索与原生SAR几何条件生成的可复现基准。数据集已公开发布于Hugging Face Hub：https://huggingface.co/datasets/ONERA/SARLO-80。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality, multimodal SAR-optical datasets for training physically grounded vision-language models, this work introduces SARLO-80, a large-scale dataset combining very-high-resolution SAR SLC data, aligned optical imagery, and natural-language descriptions. The method standardizes SAR data to an 80cm slant-range grid via band-limited FFT resampling, tiles imagery into 1024x1024 patches, and aligns optical images using local coordinate correspondences. Three caption variants per sample are generated to support diverse vision-language tasks. The dataset comprises 119,566 triplets across 257 global locations, enabling cross-modal retrieval and conditional generation in native SAR geometry, with fixed splits and code released for reproducibility.
为弥补高分辨率SAR与光学图像联合语义数据的不足，作者构建了SARLO-80数据集，整合了高分辨率SAR SLC数据、对齐的光学图像及自然语言描述。通过带限FFT重采样将SAR数据标准化为80cm斜距网格，将图像切分为1024x1024块，并利用局部坐标对应关系实现光学图像与SAR网格的像素级对齐。每样本生成三类描述（短/中/长），支持多任务视觉-语言训练。最终数据集包含119,566组三元组，覆盖72国257个地点，涵盖多种地物与基础设施，支持跨模态检索与条件生成任务的可复现基准评估。
HumanScale: Egocentric Human Video Can Outperform Real-Robot Data for Embodied Pretraining
Authors: Juncheng Ma, Jianxin Bi, Yufan Deng, Xuanran Zhai, Kewei Zhang, Ye Huang, Bo Liang, Shukai Gong, Jiankai Tu, Xiaotian Tang, Jiaxin Li, Kaiqi Chen, Duomin Wang, Yuqi Wang, Bingyi Kang, Eric Huang, Zhiyang Dou, Zhen Dong, Enze Xie, Wojciech Matusik, Tat-Seng Chua, Daquan Zhou
First: 2026-06-18T17:37:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:37:34+00:00
Comments: Github: https://github.com/DAGroup-PKU/HumanNet/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Embodied foundation models are expected to benefit from data scaling like large language models, but face a much tighter data bottleneck. Teleoperated real-robot trajectories remain the dominant pretraining source due to their precise action supervision and embodiment alignment, yet their scalability is limited by high collection cost, acquisition difficulty, and low behavioral and environmental diversity. These limitations have sparked interest in egocentric human video as a scalable, substantially lower-cost, and more diverse alternative for embodied model pretraining. However, its effectiveness compared to teleoperated real-robot data remains underexplored. To address this question, we conduct a systematic study comparing egocentric human video and teleoperated real-robot trajectories as pretraining data sources for embodied foundation models, under fixed post-training and validation protocols. Surprisingly, we find that egocentric data, when processed through a carefully designed filtering and labeling pipeline, is not merely a viable substitute for model pretraining but can lead to superior performance. With the same amount of pretraining data, models pretrained on egocentric data achieve a 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, as well as 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution real-robot task execution, respectively. This finding verifies a scalable paradigm for embodied foundation models: pretrain on egocentric human video to learn diverse world representations, then adapt with a small amount of labeled real-robot data for action-space alignment. We hope this study encourages broader exploration of egocentric data and offers guidance for data quality assessment before costly robot data collection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanScale：以第一人称视角的人类视频可优于真实机器人数据用于具身预训练
具身基础模型有望像大语言模型一样从数据扩展中获益，但面临更紧的数据瓶颈。由于其精确的动作监督和具身对齐，远程操控的真实机器人轨迹仍是主流预训练数据来源，但其可扩展性受限于高昂的采集成本、获取难度以及行为和环境多样性不足。这些局限性激发了人们对第一人称人类视频作为更具扩展性、成本更低且多样性更高的具身模型预训练替代方案的兴趣。然而，其相对于远程操控真实机器人数据的有效性尚未充分研究。为回答这一问题，我们在固定后训练和验证协议下，系统性地比较了第一人称人类视频与远程操控真实机器人轨迹作为具身基础模型预训练数据源的效果。令人惊讶的是，我们发现经过精心设计的过滤与标注流程处理后的第一人称数据，不仅可作为模型预训练的可行替代方案，还能带来更优性能。在相同预训练数据量下，使用第一人称数据预训练的模型在真实机器人动作预测上的验证损失降低24%，在分布内和分布外真实机器人任务执行上的成功率分别提升52.5%和90%。这一发现验证了具身基础模型的一种可扩展范式：先在第一人称人类视频上预训练以学习多样化的世界表征，再用少量标注的真实机器人数据进行微调以实现动作空间对齐。我们希望本研究能鼓励更广泛探索第一人称数据，并为昂贵机器人数据采集前的数据质量评估提供指导。

Through a controlled comparison under fixed post-training protocols, the researchers found that after applying a tailored filtering and labeling pipeline, egocentric data outperformed real-robot data: models pretrained on human video achieved 24% lower validation loss on real-robot action prediction, and 52.5% and 90% higher success rates on in-distribution and out-of-distribution robot tasks, respectively. These results suggest that egocentric video can be used to pretrain models for diverse world representations, followed by fine-tuning with minimal real-robot data for action alignment.
鉴于真实机器人数据采集成本高、多样性低，本研究探讨以第一人称人类视频作为可扩展的替代方案。在固定后训练协议下，作者发现经过精心过滤和标注处理后，人类视频预训练模型在真实机器人动作预测任务上验证损失低24%，在分布内和分布外任务成功率分别高出52.5%和90%。这表明人类视频可用于学习多样化的世界表征，再结合少量真实机器人数据即可完成动作空间对齐，为具身模型提供了一种可扩展的预训练范式。
Sovereign Execution Brokers: Enforcing Certificate-Bound Authority in Agentic Control Planes
Authors: Jun He, Deying Yu
First: 2026-06-18T17:36:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:36:46+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Autonomous agents are increasingly connected to cloud, deployment, and data-control workflows, but production mutation authority should not reside inside non-deterministic reasoning processes. Existing access-control mechanisms authorize identities, while assurance layers certify proposed actions; neither alone provides a mandatory enforcement point for certified authority at the moment of mutation. This paper introduces the Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB), a runtime enforcement boundary for certificate-bound agentic infrastructure. SEB consumes certificates issued by the Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB), verifies that the requested mutation matches the certified execution contract, checks validity windows, policy epochs, revocation epochs, and live-state drift, mints scoped execution identity, invokes infrastructure APIs, and records signed decision and outcome records. By separating proposal, admission, and execution, SEB turns certified authority into a short-lived, revocable, auditable runtime capability, provided that production mutation APIs reject non-broker identities. We present the SEB execution model, certificate and replay-verification predicates, scoped identity semantics, bypass-prevention deployment patterns, failure behavior, and a concrete prototype implementation. We evaluate the prototype on AWS and Kubernetes clusters, measuring latency overheads, revocation propagation, drift detection, and security under fault injection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主权执行代理：在代理控制平面中强制执行证书绑定的权限
自主代理正越来越多地连接到云、部署和数据控制工作流，但生产环境中的变更权限不应存在于非确定性推理过程中。现有访问控制机制授权身份，而保障层则认证提议的操作；两者单独均无法在变更发生时提供强制执行的认证权限点。本文引入主权执行代理（SEB），作为绑定证书的代理基础设施的运行时强制执行边界。SEB接收主权保障边界（SAB）颁发的证书，验证所请求的变更是否符合已认证的执行合约，检查有效期窗口、策略周期、撤销周期及实时状态漂移，生成限定范围的执行身份，调用基础设施API，并记录签名的决策与结果记录。通过分离提议、准入与执行，SEB将认证权限转化为短暂、可撤销、可审计的运行时能力，前提是生产变更API拒绝非代理身份。本文展示了SEB执行模型、证书与重放验证谓词、限定身份语义、绕过预防部署模式、故障行为及具体原型实现。我们在AWS和Kubernetes集群上评估了原型，测量了延迟开销、撤销传播、漂移检测及故障注入下的安全性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enforce certified authority at the moment of mutation in agentic control planes, this paper proposes the Sovereign Execution Broker (SEB), a runtime enforcement boundary that validates certificates issued by the Sovereign Assurance Boundary (SAB). SEB checks execution contracts against validity windows, policy epochs, revocation states, and live-state drift before minting scoped execution identities and invoking infrastructure APIs, while recording signed decisions. Experimental evaluation on AWS and Kubernetes clusters demonstrates that SEB introduces minimal latency overhead, enables rapid revocation propagation, detects state drift effectively, and maintains security under fault injection, confirming its viability as a mandatory enforcement point for certified authority.
为在代理系统中实现运行时的认证授权，本文提出主权执行代理（SEB），作为运行时强制执行层，仅在证书由主权保障边界签发且所请求的变更符合执行契约时才执行变更。SEB在执行前验证证书有效期、策略周期、撤销状态及实时状态漂移，生成受限执行身份并调用基础设施API，同时记录签名决策与结果。在AWS和Kubernetes集群上的实验表明，SEB引入的延迟开销极小，撤销传播迅速，能有效检测状态漂移，并在故障注入测试中保持安全，验证其作为认证授权强制执行点的可行性。
FlowEdit: Associative Memory for Lifelong Pronunciation Adaptation in Flow-Matching TTS
Authors: Harshit Singh, Ayush Pratap Singh, Nityanand Mathur
First: 2026-06-18T17:36:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:36:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Flow-matching text-to-speech systems achieve remarkable zero-shot quality but remain static after deployment: pronunciation errors on out-of-vocabulary proper nouns persist unless the model is retrained. We introduce FlowEdit, a life-long adaptation framework for frozen flow-matching TTS that learns pronunciation corrections as latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. When corrective feedback is provided, FlowEdit optimizes a token-level perturbation in the text embedding space, then stores the correction in a Modern Hopfield Network serving as content-addressable episodic memory. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate, enabling fuzzy morphological matching. On our curated benchmark of 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families, FlowEdit reduces target-word Phoneme Error Rate by 92.7% relative to the zero-shot baseline while maintaining identical general-speech quality. Corrections complete in approximately 15 seconds on a single GPU.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowEdit：面向流匹配TTS的终身发音自适应的关联记忆方法
流匹配文本到语音系统在零样本场景下表现卓越，但部署后保持静态：对未登录专有名词的发音错误无法自动修正，除非重新训练模型。我们提出FlowEdit，一种针对冻结的流匹配TTS系统的终身自适应框架，通过学习作为潜在条件编辑的发音修正，而非权重更新。当提供纠正反馈时，FlowEdit在文本嵌入空间中优化词级扰动，并将修正存储于作为内容可寻址事件记忆的现代Hopfield网络中。推理时，通过带相似性门控的软注意力检索修正，支持模糊形态匹配。在我们精心构建的包含18个语系、312个多语言专有名词的基准测试中，FlowEdit相较零样本基线将目标词音素错误率降低92.7%，同时保持通用语音质量不变。单GPU上修正耗时约15秒。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the static nature of flow-matching TTS systems that cannot adapt pronunciation errors for out-of-vocabulary proper nouns without retraining, FlowEdit introduces a lifelong adaptation framework that modifies pronunciation via latent conditioning edits rather than weight updates. It learns corrections by optimizing token-level perturbations in text embedding space and stores them in a Modern Hopfield Network for content-addressable recall. At inference, corrections are retrieved via soft attention with a similarity gate for fuzzy morphological matching. Experiments on 312 multilingual proper nouns across 18 language families show a 92.7% relative reduction in phoneme error rate compared to zero-shot baseline, with no degradation in general speech quality and corrections completed in about 15 seconds per query on a single GPU.
为了解决冻结的流匹配TTS系统无法在不重新训练的情况下适应生僻专有名词发音错误的问题，FlowEdit提出了一种终身适应框架，将发音修正作为潜在条件编辑存储在现代Hopfield网络中。当提供纠正反馈时，系统在文本嵌入空间中优化词级扰动并将其存入作为内容可寻址的事件记忆。推理时通过带相似度门控的软注意力检索修正，支持模糊形态匹配。在涵盖18个语系的312个多语言专有名词基准测试中，相比零样本基线，发音错误率降低92.7%，且通用语音质量保持不变，单GPU下每个词修正耗时约15秒。
Multi-LCB: Extending LiveCodeBench to Multiple Programming Languages
Authors: Maria Ivanova, Pavel Zadorozhny, Rodion Levichev, Ivan Petrov, Adamenko Pavel, Ivan Lopatin, Alexey Kutalev, Dmitrii Babaev
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:35:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:35:57+00:00
Comments: ICLR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LiveCodeBench (LCB) has recently become a widely adopted benchmark for evaluating large language models (LLMs) on code-generation tasks. By curating competitive programming problems, constantly adding fresh problems to the set, and filtering them by release dates, LCB provides contamination-aware evaluation and offers a holistic view of coding capability. However, LCB remains restricted to Python, leaving open the question of whether LLMs can generalize across the diverse programming languages required in real-world software engineering. We introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs across twelve programming languages, including Python. Multi-LCB transforms Python tasks from the LCB dataset into equivalent tasks in other languages while preserving LCB's contamination controls and evaluation protocol. Because it is fully compatible with the original LCB format, Multi-LCB will automatically track future LCB updates, enabling systematic assessment of cross-language code generation competence and requiring models to sustain performance well beyond Python. We evaluated 24 LLMs for instruction and reasoning on Multi-LCB, uncovering evidence of Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and substantial disparities in multilingual performance. Our results establish Multi-LCB as a rigorous new benchmark for multi-programming-language code evaluation, directly addressing LCB's primary limitation and exposing critical gaps in current LLM capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Multi-LCB：扩展LiveCodeBench至多种编程语言
LiveCodeBench（LCB）近期已成为评估大语言模型（LLMs）在代码生成任务上的广泛基准。通过精选编程竞赛题目、持续添加新题目并按发布日期过滤，LCB提供污染感知评估，全面反映编码能力。然而，LCB仍局限于Python，留下LLMs能否在现实软件工程中跨多种编程语言泛化的问题。 我们引入Multi-LCB，一个评估LLMs在十二种编程语言（包括Python）上的基准。Multi-LCB将LCB数据集中的Python任务转换为其他语言的等价任务，同时保留LCB的污染控制与评估协议。由于完全兼容原LCB格式，Multi-LCB将自动追踪未来LCB更新，实现跨语言代码生成能力的系统性评估，要求模型在Python之外持续保持性能。 我们在Multi-LCB上评估了24个LLMs的指令与推理能力，发现Python过拟合、语言特异性污染及多语言性能显著差异的证据。我们的结果确立Multi-LCB为多语言代码评估的严格新基准，直接解决LCB的主要局限，并揭示当前LLMs能力的关键差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess large language models' ability to generalize across programming languages beyond Python, the authors introduce Multi-LCB, a benchmark that extends LiveCodeBench to twelve languages by translating Python problems while preserving contamination controls and evaluation protocols. Evaluated on 24 LLMs, the benchmark reveals significant Python overfitting, language-specific contamination, and large performance disparities across languages, demonstrating that current models struggle to maintain consistent code-generation quality outside Python and highlighting Multi-LCB as a critical tool for measuring cross-language competence.
为评估大语言模型在非Python编程语言上的泛化能力，作者构建了Multi-LCB基准，通过将LiveCodeBench中的Python题目翻译为其他十二种语言，同时保留原评测的污染控制与评估协议。在24个模型上的实验发现，模型普遍存在Python过拟合、语言特异性污染及跨语言性能差异显著的问题，表明当前模型在非Python语言上的代码生成能力仍存在明显短板。
S-Agent: Spatial Tool-Use Elicits Reasoning for Spatial Intelligence
Authors: Yalun Dai, Hao Li, Shulin Tian, Runmao Yao, Yuhao Dong, Fangzhou Hong, Zhaoxi Chen, Fangfu Liu, Baoliang Tian, Dingwen Zhang, Tao Wang, Kim-Hui Yap, Ziwei Liu
First: 2026-06-18T17:34:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:34:55+00:00
Comments: Project Page : https://Ropedia.github.io/S-Agent
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Real-world spatial intelligence requires reasoning over a continuous and evolving 3D world, yet existing VLMs and tool-augmented agents largely remain tied to static, stateless inference from isolated visual observations. We introduce \textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}, a spatial tool-use agentic paradigm for understanding and reasoning over continuous multi-view images and videos. By formulating spatial reasoning as spatio-temporal evidence accumulation rather than isolated frame-level prediction, \textsc{S-Agent} reshapes spatial perception into scene-centric understanding beyond frame-centric recognition. Specifically, \textsc{S-Agent} casts the VLM as a semantic planner that decides what evidence is needed, while a hierarchy of spatial tools and experts grounds objects in 2D, lifts them into 3D geometric evidence, and aggregates this evidence into high-level spatial knowledge (\textit{e.g.}, counting, measurement, orientation, and relative position). Additionally, a temporal memory mechanism, including Scene Memory for maintaining the evolving scene state and Agent Memory for accumulating reasoning context, enables evidence integration across frames and reasoning steps. Comprehensive experiments on multi-view and video spatial reasoning benchmarks show that \textsc{S-Agent} consistently improves both open-source and closed-source VLMs in a training-free manner. Beyond inference-time augmentation, supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on \textsc{S-Agent}-generated spatial trajectories \textsc{S-300K} yields \textsc{S-Agent-8B}, a compact spatial agent that significantly surpasses similar-scale baselines (e.g., Qwen3-VL-8B) and performs comparably to advanced closed-source models (e.g., GPT-5.4 and Gemini 3).
中文标题/摘要
标题：S-Agent：空间工具使用激发空间智能推理
现实世界中的空间智能需要对连续演化的3D世界进行推理，但现有的视觉语言模型（VLM）和工具增强型代理大多仍依赖于从孤立视觉观察中进行静态、无状态的推理。我们提出了\textbf{\textsc{S-Agent}}，一种用于理解和推理连续多视角图像与视频的空间工具使用代理范式。通过将空间推理建模为时空证据累积而非孤立帧级预测，\textsc{S-Agent}将空间感知重塑为以场景为中心的理解，超越了以帧为中心的识别。具体而言，\textsc{S-Agent}将VLM视为语义规划器，决定所需证据；而多层次空间工具与专家则将物体锚定在2D平面，将其提升为3D几何证据，并聚合这些证据形成高层空间知识（如计数、测量、朝向和相对位置）。此外，一个包含场景记忆（用于维护场景状态演化）和代理记忆（用于累积推理上下文）的时间记忆机制，支持跨帧和推理步骤的证据整合。在多视角与视频空间推理基准上的全面实验表明，\textsc{S-Agent}在无需训练的情况下，持续提升开源与闭源VLM的表现。除推理时增强外，基于\textsc{S-Agent}生成的空间轨迹\textsc{S-300K}进行监督微调（SFT）得到\textsc{S-Agent-8B}，这是一个紧凑型空间代理，显著超越同规模基线（如Qwen3-VL-8B），并可与先进闭源模型（如GPT-5.4和Gemini 3）相媲美。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for continuous spatial reasoning in dynamic 3D environments, S-Agent introduces a tool-use agentic framework that transforms spatial perception from frame-level recognition to scene-centric understanding through spatio-temporal evidence accumulation. It employs a semantic planner to guide evidence gathering, a hierarchy of spatial tools to ground 2D objects into 3D geometric data, and a dual-memory system (Scene Memory and Agent Memory) to integrate evidence across frames and reasoning steps. Experiments show S-Agent boosts both open-source and closed-source VLMs without training, and its SFT-derived S-Agent-8B model outperforms comparable baselines and rivals advanced closed-source models.
为解决现有视觉语言模型难以处理连续3D空间推理的问题，S-Agent提出一种基于空间工具使用的代理范式，将空间推理建模为跨时空证据累积过程。该框架包含语义规划器指导证据获取、分层空间工具将2D对象升维至3D几何证据、以及双记忆机制实现帧间与推理步骤间的证据整合。实验表明，S-Agent无需训练即可提升各类视觉语言模型性能，其通过监督微调生成的S-Agent-8B模型在空间推理任务中显著优于同规模基线模型，且性能可媲美主流闭源模型。
Probe-and-Refine Tuning of Repository Guidance for Coding Agents
Authors: Asa Shepard, Jeannie Albrecht
First: 2026-06-18T17:30:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:30:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-based coding agents need higher-level operational knowledge about a repository (which files house which subsystems, how to run the test suite, which workflows have historically led to wrong fixes) that does not exist in the code itself. Engineers typically maintain \texttt{AGENTS.md} files to supply this context as instructions for coding agents, but whether they help is contested: recent studies disagree on whether LLM-generated guidance improves or harms agent performance. In this paper we show that how the guidance is produced is the decisive variable, and introduce \emph{probe-and-refine tuning}: a procedure that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively diagnose and patch a repository's guidance file through single-shot LLM calls, with no agent loop or tool use during tuning. On SWE-bench Verified across four independent trials with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B at 200 steps, probe-and-refine achieves 33.0\,\% mean resolve rate vs.\ 28.3\,\% for the static knowledge base used to initialize it and 25.5\,\% for an unguided baseline ($p < 0.001$ for both probe-and-refine contrasts). The improvement comes from coverage rather than precision: refined guidance produces evaluable patches for 14.5 percentage points (pp) more instances while per-patch precision remains statistically constant ($\sim$59\,\%, $p = 0.119$), showing that improved guidance helps agents reach the correct file rather than improving the quality of the changes they make. Further, a step-budget experiment shows that guidance is what lets the agent use a larger step budget productively, and a cross-model experiment with NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B finds that the tuning loop degrades when the model cannot generate sufficiently diagnostic output, though per-patch precision remains constant even then.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于探针与精炼调优的代码代理仓库指导
基于大语言模型的代码代理需要关于仓库的更高层次操作知识（如哪些文件包含哪些子系统、如何运行测试套件、哪些工作流曾导致错误修复），而这些知识并不存在于代码本身。工程师通常通过\texttt{AGENTS.md}文件提供此类上下文作为对代码代理的指令，但其有效性存在争议：近期研究对LLM生成的指导是否提升或损害代理性能意见不一。本文表明，指导的生成方式是决定性因素，并引入\emph{探针与精炼调优}：一种利用合成缺陷修复探针，通过单次LLM调用迭代诊断并修补仓库指导文件的程序，调优过程中无需代理循环或工具使用。在SWE-bench验证中，使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B模型在200步内进行四次独立试验，探针与精炼调优实现33.0\,\%平均解决率，显著优于初始化时使用的静态知识库（28.3\,\%）和无指导基线（25.5\,\%）（p < 0.001）。改进源于覆盖范围而非精确度：精炼后的指导使14.5个百分点（pp）更多实例生成可评估的补丁，而每补丁的精确度保持统计恒定（\sim59\,\%，p = 0.119），表明改进的指导帮助代理定位正确文件，而非提升其修改质量。此外，步数预算实验显示，指导使代理能更有效地使用更大步数预算，而跨模型实验（NVIDIA-Nemotron-3-Nano-30B-A3B）发现，当模型无法生成足够诊断性输出时，调优循环性能下降，但每补丁精确度仍保持恒定。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge that LLM-based coding agents require external repository knowledge—such as file-to-subsystem mappings and test workflows—that is not encoded in code itself. To tackle this, the authors introduce 'probe-and-refine tuning,' a method that uses synthetic bug-fix probes to iteratively improve a repository’s guidance file via single-shot LLM calls, without requiring agent loops or tool use. Evaluated on SWE-bench with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B across four trials, the tuned guidance achieved a 33.0% mean resolve rate, outperforming both the static knowledge base (28.3%) and an unguided baseline (25.5%), with statistically significant differences. The gain stems from increased coverage—refined guidance enabled patches for 14.5 percentage points more instances—without improving per-patch precision (~59%). Further experiments show guidance enables agents to use larger step budgets effectively and that tuning degrades when models cannot generate diagnostic probes, though precision remains stable.
该论文针对LLM编码代理需要外部仓库知识（如文件与子系统映射、测试流程）而这些知识未编码在代码中的问题，提出“探针与精调”方法，通过合成的修复探针，利用单次LLM调用迭代优化指导文件，无需代理循环或工具调用。在SWE-bench上使用Qwen3.5-35B-A3B模型评估，该方法平均解决率达33.0%，显著优于静态知识库（28.3%）和无引导基线（25.5%），差异具有统计显著性。性能提升源于覆盖范围扩大——精调后指导文件使14.5个百分点更多实例生成可评估补丁，而单补丁精度（约59%）基本不变，表明改进指导帮助代理定位正确文件而非提升补丁质量。进一步实验表明，指导使代理能更高效使用更大步数预算，且当模型无法生成诊断性输出时，精调效果下降，但精度仍保持稳定。
Efficient and Sound Probabilistic Verification for AI Agents
Authors: Alaia Solko-Breslin, Pramod Kaushik Mudrakarta, Mihai Christodorescu, Somesh Jha, Krishnamurthy Dj Dvijotham
First: 2026-06-18T17:27:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:27:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Securing AI agents that operate in complex digital environments has become a critical need, and runtime monitoring approaches that formulate and enforce policies expressed in a formal language like Datalog offer a promising solution. However, existing approaches are restricted to deterministic policies. In many practical applications of AI agents, there is a need to enforce security policies in the face of ambiguity, leading to probabilistic predicates or state transitions (for example, a declassifier or Personally Identifiable Information (PII) detector that has some failure probability on each invocation). Furthermore, in many such applications, one cannot easily make the independence assumptions necessary to invoke prior work on probabilistic inference in Datalog. We address this by introducing a sound and efficient framework for such verification based on distributionally robust optimization, computing sound upper bounds on the probability of policy violation regardless of possible correlations between predicates. On standard benchmarks for terminal and tool calling agents, we demonstrate that our approach outperforms prior art and improves the security-utility trade-off while ensuring rigorous bounds on the probability of policy violation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AI代理的概率验证：高效且可靠
保障在复杂数字环境中运行的AI代理的安全已成为关键需求，而采用形式化语言（如Datalog）表达并执行策略的运行时监控方法提供了有前景的解决方案。然而，现有方法仅限于确定性策略。在许多AI代理的实际应用中，需要在面对不确定性时执行安全策略，这导致了概率谓词或状态转换（例如，具有每次调用失败概率的脱敏器或个人身份信息（PII）检测器）。此外，在许多此类应用中，难以轻易满足调用Datalog概率推理先前工作的独立性假设。我们通过引入基于分布鲁棒优化的框架来解决这一问题，该框架可计算在谓词间可能相关性下政策违反概率的可靠上界。在终端和工具调用代理的标准基准测试中，我们证明了该方法优于现有技术，同时改善了安全与效用之间的权衡，并确保对政策违反概率的严格界限。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to secure AI agents operating in uncertain digital environments, this work addresses the limitation of existing deterministic policy enforcement methods by introducing a sound and efficient probabilistic verification framework based on distributionally robust optimization. The method computes rigorous upper bounds on the probability of policy violation without assuming independence between probabilistic predicates, making it suitable for real-world scenarios involving correlated failures, such as declassifiers or PII detectors with non-zero error rates. Experimental results on standard benchmarks for terminal and tool calling agents show that the proposed approach outperforms prior methods, achieving better security-utility trade-offs while maintaining provable guarantees on violation probabilities.
为应对在复杂数字环境中运行的AI代理的安全需求，本文提出了一种兼顾效率与严谨性的概率验证框架，以克服现有确定性策略验证方法的局限。该框架基于分布鲁棒优化，无需假设谓词间独立性，即可计算策略违规概率的上界。在终端与工具调用代理的标准基准测试中，实验表明该方法优于现有技术，在安全与效用之间取得更优平衡，同时保持严格的概率保证。
What Do Safety-Aligned LLMs Learn From Mixed Compliance Demonstrations?
Authors: Sihui Dai, Mann Patel
First: 2026-06-18T17:25:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:25:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Prior work has shown that in-context demonstrations can jailbreak language models, but it remains unclear how models interpret different types of compliance demonstrations. We study this by mixing benign compliance demonstrations (non-harmful request, helpful response) with harmful compliance demonstrations (harmful request, helpful response) and testing three hypotheses about how demonstration composition drives harmful compliance. Across four models, we find that benign and harmful demonstrations are not interchangeable: benign demonstrations can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. We further show that preference optimization is the critical training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from increasing harmful compliance, that demonstration ordering exhibits strong recency bias, and that models differ in how refusal interacts with in-context learning: some adopt demonstrated formatting even when refusing, while others override all in-context signals upon refusal. Taken together, this work moves beyond showing that demonstration-based jailbreaking works to characterizing how it works: what models extract from compliance demonstrations depends on demonstration content, ordering, and training methodology.
中文标题/摘要
标题：安全对齐的大语言模型从混合合规演示中学习了什么？
先前研究表明，上下文演示可绕过语言模型的安全限制，但模型如何解释不同类型的合规演示仍不明确。我们通过混合良性合规演示（无害请求，有益响应）与有害合规演示（有害请求，有益响应），测试三种假设，探讨演示组合如何驱动有害合规。在四个模型中发现，良性与有害演示不可互换：良性演示对不同模型可能降低或增加有害合规。进一步表明，偏好优化是防止良性演示增加有害合规的关键训练阶段，演示顺序存在显著近期偏倚，且模型在拒绝时与上下文学习的交互方式不同：部分模型即使拒绝仍采用演示格式，而另一些模型则完全覆盖上下文信号。综合来看，本研究不仅证实演示型绕过有效，更刻画其机制：模型从合规演示中提取的内容取决于演示内容、顺序及训练方法。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how language models interpret mixed compliance demonstrations—combining benign and harmful examples—to understand the mechanisms behind demonstration-based jailbreaking. By testing three hypotheses across four models, the authors find that benign demonstrations do not uniformly suppress harmful behavior; instead, they can either reduce or increase harmful compliance depending on the model. The research identifies preference optimization as the key training stage that prevents benign demonstrations from exacerbating harmful outputs, reveals strong recency bias in demonstration ordering, and shows that models vary in how they handle refusal signals versus in-context learning, with some preserving demonstrated formatting even when refusing and others overriding all in-context cues upon refusal.
该研究探讨语言模型如何解读混合合规演示（同时包含良性与有害示例），以理解演示型越狱的机制。通过对四个模型测试三个假设，研究发现良性演示并不总是抑制有害行为，其效果取决于具体模型——有时降低有害输出，有时反而增加。研究指出偏好优化是关键训练阶段，可防止良性演示加剧有害输出；演示顺序存在显著近期偏倚；且模型在拒绝时处理上下文信号的方式不同：部分模型即使拒绝仍保留演示格式，另一些则完全覆盖上下文信号。这些发现超越了验证越狱可行性的层面，进一步刻画了影响模型行为的复杂因素。
FreeStyle: Free Control of Style-Content Dual-Reference Generation from Community LoRA Mining
Authors: Jinghong Lan, Wei Cheng, Yunuo Chen, Ziqi Ye, Peng Xing, Yixiao Fang, Rui Wang, Yufeng Yang, Xuanyang Zhang, Xianfang Zeng, Difan Zou, Gang Yu, Chi Zhang
First: 2026-06-18T17:24:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:24:27+00:00
Comments: 35 pages, 26figures. Project page: https://github.com/Blue2Giant/FreeStyle
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Style-content dual-reference generation aims to synthesize an image that preserves the structure and semantics of a content reference while adopting the style of a separate style reference.Despite recent progress, this setting remains challenging because models must balance content fidelity, style alignment, and instruction following avoiding semantic leakage from the style reference.A key bottleneck is the lack of large-scale triplet data with clean content-style separation and broad long-tail style coverage.In this work, we propose FreeStyle, a scalable dual-reference generation framework based on community LoRA mining.We treat community LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content, and design a rigorous generation and filtering pipeline to construct large-scale Style-Reference and Content-Reference triplets across multiple base models.To address content leakage, we adopt a two-stage curriculum with stage-specific disentanglement mechanisms: an attention-level enrichment constraint that suppresses style-reference leakage in the style-transfer stage, and a frequency-aware RoPE modulation strategy that targets positional-correspondence-based leakage in the harder dual-reference stage.We also introduce a benchmark covering both style-reference and dual-reference generation, with evaluations on style similarity, content preservation, aesthetics, instruction following, and leakage rejection. The benchmark incorporates a style-invariant Content Alignment Score (CAS) and introduces a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score for evaluating generation reliability and leakage suppression.Extensive experiments show that our model achieves a strong balance among style alignment, content preservation, and leakage suppression.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FreeStyle：基于社区LoRA挖掘的风格-内容双参考生成自由控制
风格-内容双参考生成旨在合成一张图像，保留内容参考的结构与语义，同时采用独立风格参考的风格。尽管近期取得进展，该任务仍具挑战性，因模型需平衡内容保真度、风格对齐与指令遵循，避免风格参考的语义泄露。关键瓶颈在于缺乏大规模具有清晰内容-风格分离且覆盖广泛长尾风格的三元组数据。本文提出FreeStyle，一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考生成框架。我们将社区LoRA视为风格与内容的组合锚点，设计严谨的生成与过滤流程，构建跨多个基础模型的大量风格参考与内容参考三元组。为解决内容泄露问题，我们采用两阶段课程学习，结合阶段特定的解耦机制：在风格迁移阶段，采用注意力级丰富约束抑制风格参考泄露；在更难的双参考阶段，采用频率感知的RoPE调制策略，针对基于位置对应性的泄露进行抑制。我们还引入涵盖风格参考与双参考生成的基准测试，评估风格相似度、内容保留度、美学、指令遵循及泄露抑制能力。该基准包含风格不变的内容对齐得分（CAS），并引入校准的VLM基线拒绝得分，用于评估生成可靠性与泄露抑制效果。大量实验表明，我们的模型在风格对齐、内容保留与泄露抑制之间实现了强平衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of generating images that faithfully preserve content while adopting a distinct style without semantic leakage, FreeStyle introduces a scalable framework leveraging community-contributed LoRAs as compositional anchors for style and content. It constructs large-scale triplets via a generation and filtering pipeline, then employs a two-stage curriculum with attention-level leakage suppression and frequency-aware RoPE modulation to disentangle style and content. Experimental results on a new benchmark demonstrate strong performance in style alignment, content preservation, and leakage rejection, validated by novel metrics including a style-invariant Content Alignment Score and a calibrated VLM-based Rejection Score.
为解决在保持内容结构的同时采用独立风格且避免语义泄露的图像生成难题，FreeStyle提出一种基于社区LoRA挖掘的可扩展双参考生成框架，将社区LoRA作为风格与内容的组合锚点，通过生成与过滤流水线构建大规模三元组数据。该框架采用两阶段课程学习，结合注意力层约束抑制风格迁移阶段的泄露，并引入频域感知RoPE调制策略应对双参考生成阶段的位置相关泄露。实验表明，该方法在风格对齐、内容保留与泄露抑制三方面取得良好平衡，新引入的风格不变内容对齐得分与校准视觉语言模型拒斥得分有效验证了其生成可靠性与泄露抑制能力。
Entropy Estimation in Multi-Qutrit Systems via Variational and Classical Neural Networks
Authors: Sai Sakunthala Guddanti, Anil Prabhakar, Ria Rushin Joseph
First: 2026-06-18T17:22:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:22:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a systematic study of von Neumann entropy estimation in multi-qutrit quantum systems using two complementary approaches: variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) and classical convolutional neural networks (CNNs), evaluated using an ideal (noise-free) quantum simulator. For systems up to three qutrits, we construct and evaluate 11 hardware-efficient SU(3)-inspired ansatzes. A parameter sweep shows that estimation accuracy is primarily determined by the number of trainable parameters, provided sufficient entanglement is present. Based on this study, we fix the parameter count to approximately 120 for subsequent experiments, observing that increasing entangling-gate counts beyond a threshold yields only marginal improvements. For larger systems (two to five qutrits), we use a CNN trained on measurement outcomes from tensor-product mutually unbiased bases. The model achieves accurate and stable predictions and exhibits a systematic improvement in performance with system size, with the highest errors for two-qutrit systems and the lowest for five-qutrit systems. Notably, using only 12.5% of the measurements required for full state tomography is sufficient to reach 90th-percentile absolute errors of approximately 0.13-0.16 nats for both four- and five-qutrit systems. The CNN model is also robust to shot noise and generalizes well to out-of-distribution states. Overall, within the simulated settings studied here, our results indicate a transition in practical methods: VQAs are effective for small systems, while CNN-based estimators offer improved scalability and robustness for larger qutrit systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过变分与经典神经网络对多三能级量子系统熵的估计
我们系统研究了使用两种互补方法——变分量子算法（VQAs）和经典卷积神经网络（CNNs）——对多三能级量子系统中的冯·诺依曼熵进行估计，评估基于理想（无噪声）量子模拟器。对于最多三个三能级系统，我们构建并评估了11种受SU(3)启发的硬件高效参数化方案。参数扫描表明，只要存在充分纠缠，估计精度主要由可训练参数数量决定。基于此研究，我们固定参数数量约为120，观察到超过阈值的纠缠门数量仅带来微小改进。对于更大系统（两个至五个三能级），我们使用CNN训练于张量积互不偏倚基的测量结果。该模型实现准确且稳定的预测，且随系统规模性能系统性提升，两三能级系统误差最高，五三能级系统最低。值得注意的是，仅需完整状态层析所需测量数据的12.5%，即可使四至五三能级系统90百分位绝对误差达到约0.13-0.16纳特。CNN模型对光子噪声鲁棒，且能良好泛化至分布外状态。总体而言，在本研究模拟设置下，结果表明实用方法出现转变：VQAs适用于小系统，而基于CNN的估计器在更大三能级系统中提供更优可扩展性与鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient entropy estimation in multi-qutrit quantum systems, this study compares variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) and classical convolutional neural networks (CNNs) under ideal simulation conditions. For up to three qutrits, 11 SU(3)-inspired VQA ansatzes were evaluated, revealing that estimation accuracy scales with trainable parameters when sufficient entanglement is present, leading to a fixed 120-parameter setting. For larger systems (two to five qutrits), a CNN trained on tensor-product mutually unbiased basis measurements achieved stable and accurate predictions, improving systematically with system size. The CNN required only 12.5% of full tomography data to achieve 90th-percentile errors of 0.13–0.16 nats for four- and five-qutrit systems, and remained robust to shot noise and generalization across out-of-distribution states, suggesting VQAs are suitable for small systems while CNNs offer superior scalability and reliability for larger ones.
为高效估计多qutrit量子系统的冯·诺依曼熵，本研究对比了变分量子算法（VQAs）与经典卷积神经网络（CNNs）在理想量子模拟器上的表现。针对最多三个qutrit系统，评估了11种SU(3)启发式VQA结构，发现当存在足够纠缠时，精度主要取决于可训练参数数量，故固定参数量为120。对于更大系统（二至五个qutrit），CNN通过训练张量积互 unbiased 基测量数据，实现稳定准确预测，性能随系统规模系统性提升——二qutrit系统误差最高，五qutrit系统最低。仅用全态层析所需数据的12.5%，CNN即达四、五qutrit系统90百分位绝对误差0.13–0.16 nats，且对光子噪声鲁棒、能泛化至分布外状态，表明VQAs适用于小系统，而CNN更适于大系统规模化与鲁棒性需求。
Calibration Without Comprehension: Diagnosing the Limits of Fine-Tuning LLMs for Vulnerability Detection in Systems Software
Authors: Arastoo Zibaeirad, Marco Vieira
First: 2026-06-18T17:19:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:19:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether LLMs scoring well on vulnerability benchmarks genuinely reason about security or merely pattern-match on contaminated data remains unresolved. We present CWE-Trace, a framework for LLM vulnerability detection built from 834 manually curated Linux kernel samples spanning 74 CWEs. The framework enforces a strict temporal split (pre-2025 historical set / post-cutoff leakage-free set), preserves context-aware vulnerable--patched pairs, and introduces two diagnostic metrics: the Directional Failure Index (DFI) and Hierarchical Distance and Direction (HDD). We evaluate eight vanilla LLMs and 15 LoRA fine-tuned variants across non-targeted detection, targeted detection, and CWE classification. Our analysis yields two key results. First, data contamination provides no measurable advantage. Function-level analysis shows that 84% of nominally contaminated samples carry no usable memorization signal: vulnerable functions are absent or cross-mapped across datasets, and ~31% of contaminated samples carry CWE misclassification. Second, backbone directional priors dominate fine-tuning. Models exhibit stable, systematic failure modes (DFI ranging from -85.5 to +94.8 pp) that persist from historical to post-cutoff data and resist correction. Fine-tuning shifts the output threshold without changing the decision policy. This is calibration without comprehension: output distributions adapt to training data while the underlying security reasoning remains absent. The weakest backbone at binary detection (DeepSeek-R1) gains the most in coarse CWE classification, revealing that detection and understanding are decoupled capabilities. The best detection score reaches only 52.1% (+2.1 pp above chance); exact CWE ranking remains below 1.3% Top-1 accuracy, confirming that current LLMs lack reliable security reasoning for systems software, regardless of fine-tuning strategy.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需理解的校准：诊断微调大语言模型在系统软件漏洞检测中的局限
大语言模型在漏洞基准测试中表现优异，但其是否真正理解安全问题，还是仅在污染数据上进行模式匹配，仍无定论。我们提出了CWE-Trace框架，该框架基于834个手工整理的Linux内核样本，涵盖74种CWE漏洞。框架采用严格的时序分割（2025年前历史数据集/2025年后无泄漏数据集），保留上下文感知的漏洞-补丁对，并引入两种诊断指标：方向性失败指数（DFI）和层次距离与方向（HDD）。我们评估了8个基础大语言模型和15个LoRA微调变体，在非目标检测、目标检测和CWE分类任务中的表现。分析得出两个关键结论：第一，数据污染无明显优势。函数级分析显示，名义上被污染的样本中84%无可用记忆信号：漏洞函数要么缺失，要么跨数据集交叉映射，约31%样本存在CWE误分类。第二，基础模型的方向性先验主导微调效果。模型表现出稳定、系统性的失败模式（DFI范围从-85.5到+94.8 pp），从历史数据到截止后数据均持续存在，且难以纠正。微调仅调整输出阈值，未改变决策策略。这是“无理解的校准”：输出分布适应训练数据，但底层安全推理仍缺失。在二元检测中表现最弱的基础模型（DeepSeek-R1）在粗粒度CWE分类中提升最大，揭示检测与理解能力可分离。最佳检测得分仅达52.1%（比随机高2.1 pp）；精确CWE排序Top-1准确率仍低于1.3%，证实当前大语言模型无论采用何种微调策略，均缺乏系统软件安全推理能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the unresolved question of whether LLMs perform well on vulnerability benchmarks due to true security reasoning or mere pattern-matching on contaminated data, the authors introduce CWE-Trace, a framework using 834 manually curated Linux kernel samples across 74 CWEs with strict temporal splits and diagnostic metrics (DFI and HDD). They evaluate 23 LLM variants (8 vanilla, 15 LoRA fine-tuned) across detection and classification tasks. Results show no measurable advantage from data contamination, as 84% of contaminated samples lack usable memorization and 31% misclassify CWEs. Fine-tuning does not improve reasoning but shifts output thresholds, revealing 'calibration without comprehension.' The best model achieves only 52.1% binary detection accuracy and 1.3% Top-1 CWE accuracy, indicating LLMs lack reliable security reasoning even after fine-tuning.
为探究大语言模型在漏洞检测中是真正理解安全逻辑还是仅靠数据模式匹配，研究构建了CWE-Trace框架，包含834个手动标注的Linux内核样本，覆盖74种CWE，采用严格时间分割和诊断指标（DFI与HDD）。评估23个模型（含15个LoRA微调版本）后发现，数据污染无实质优势，84%污染样本无可用记忆信号，31%样本CWE误分类；微调主要调整输出阈值而非决策逻辑，体现‘校准无理解’。最优模型二进制检测准确率仅52.1%，CWE分类Top-1准确率低于1.3%，表明当前LLM缺乏系统软件安全推理能力，无论是否微调。
Integrated Exploration-Aware UAV Route Optimization and Path Planning
Authors: Jimin Choi, Grant Stagg, Cameron K. Peterson, Max Z. Li
First: 2026-05-27T15:54:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:15:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs) are increasingly used for exploration-driven monitoring in hazardous environments such as disaster zones, contaminated sites, wildfire areas, and damaged infrastructure, where limited flight endurance must be allocated between visiting reported locations and gathering new information. In these settings, prior information regarding hazards is often incomplete, spatially imprecise, and subject to change during execution. For example, initial reports may identify a region where a hazard is likely to exist, but the actual hazard may be displaced, partially observed, or entirely unreported. We present an integrated exploration-aware UAV route optimization and path planning framework for hazard monitoring under uncertain and evolving prior information. The environment is represented as a spatial risk map, where each location has an associated belief of hazardous conditions. Reported hazards are modeled as uncertain regions of interest (ROIs) rather than confirmed target locations, requiring the UAV to inspect reported areas while also using its limited flight endurance to explore informative regions. The proposed method solves a vehicle routing problem over reported ROIs, augments the route with auxiliary pseudo-nodes to improve spatial coverage, allocates the remaining flight distance budget across route segments, and optimizes dynamically feasible B-spline trajectories for local exploration. During execution, UAV measurements update a grid-based belief map, and the remaining trajectory is replanned when new information and the remaining budget justify adaptation. Across 48 scenario configurations, online replanning improves average KL reduction by 15.9% over the offline optimized planner and 48.6% over straight-line traversal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：集成探索感知的无人机路径优化与规划
无人机在灾害区、污染区域、野火区和受损基础设施等危险环境中，日益用于探索驱动的监测任务，其中有限的飞行续航能力需在访问报告地点与收集新信息之间分配。在这些场景中，关于危险的先验信息往往不完整、空间精度不足，且在执行过程中可能发生变化。例如，初始报告可能指出某区域可能存在危险，但实际危险可能已移动、部分可见或完全未被报告。本文提出了一种集成探索感知的无人机路径优化与规划框架，用于在不确定且动态变化的先验信息下进行危险监测。环境以空间风险图表示，每个位置关联着危险条件的置信度。报告的危险被建模为不确定的兴趣区域（ROIs），而非确认目标点，要求无人机在检查报告区域的同时，利用有限飞行续航探索信息丰富的区域。所提方法在报告的ROIs上求解车辆路径问题，通过添加辅助伪节点以提升空间覆盖，将剩余飞行距离预算分配至路径段，并动态优化可执行的B样条轨迹以实现局部探索。执行过程中，无人机测量更新基于网格的置信度地图，当新信息和剩余预算支持时，重新规划剩余轨迹。在48种场景配置中，在线重规划使平均KL散度减少量比离线优化规划器提升15.9%，比直线遍历提升48.6%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient UAV monitoring in hazardous, information-uncertain environments like disaster zones or wildfire areas, this paper proposes an integrated route optimization and path planning framework that balances visiting reported hazard locations with active exploration. The method models hazards as uncertain regions of interest on a spatial risk map and solves a vehicle routing problem over these ROIs, inserting pseudo-nodes to enhance spatial coverage and dynamically allocating flight budget across segments. It generates feasible B-spline trajectories for local exploration and replans the route online as new measurements update the belief map. Across 48 scenarios, online replanning achieved 15.9% greater average KL divergence reduction than offline planning and 48.6% more than straight-line traversal.
为在飞行续航受限的情况下高效监测危险环境，本文提出一种融合探索感知的无人机路径优化与规划框架，以应对不确定且动态变化的先验信息。该方法将报告的危险区域建模为不确定的兴趣区域（ROI），在空间风险图上求解车辆路径问题，并通过添加伪节点提升空间覆盖。剩余飞行预算按路径段分配，生成动态可行的B样条轨迹用于局部探索。执行过程中，无人机测量更新网格信念图，当新信息或剩余预算允许时触发重规划。在48种场景中，在线重规划比离线规划平均KL减少率高15.9%，比直线遍历高48.6%。
Increasing Resilience of Continuum Robots via Motion Planning Algorithms
Authors: Oxana Shamilyan, Ievgen Kabin, Zoya Dyka, Oleksandr Sudakov, Peter Langendoerfer
First: 2026-06-18T17:10:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:10:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents an experimental study of motion planning for resilient continuum robots. In this study we mainly focused on multi-criteria decision-making, its application for path-planning algorithms, impact on the generated path and execution time. To do this, we used two well-known algorithms for path planning, namely Genetic algorithm and A star algorithm, and modified them by adding the Analytical Hierarchy Process algorithm to evaluate the quality of the paths generated. In our experiment the Analytical Hierarchy Process considers four different criteria, i.e. distance, motors damage, mechanical damage of the robot's arm and accuracy, each considered to contribute to the resilience of a continuum robot. The use of different criteria is necessary to increase the time to maintenance operations of the continuum robot. We conducted the experiments using two different simulated environments of the robot. Although we significantly simplified the robot's model and its environment, we still implemented some of the features of the environment based on the real robot prototype. In particular, one of the environments has single- as well as multi-path points, and other consists of the multi-path points only. The results show that, in contrast to A star, the performance time of Genetic algorithm does not depend on the environment's cardinality. It generates more diverse paths, which increases the robot's resilience.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过运动规划算法增强连续体机器人的韧性
本文提出了一项关于具有韧性的连续体机器人运动规划的实验研究。本研究主要关注多准则决策，其在路径规划算法中的应用、对生成路径及执行时间的影响。为此，我们使用了两种著名的路径规划算法——遗传算法和A*算法，并通过加入层次分析法来评估所生成路径的质量。在实验中，层次分析法考虑了四个不同准则：距离、电机损伤、机器人臂机械损伤和精度，每个准则均被认为对连续体机器人的韧性有贡献。使用不同准则的必要性在于延长连续体机器人维护操作的时间。我们使用了两种不同的机器人模拟环境进行实验。尽管我们显著简化了机器人的模型及其环境，但仍实现了一些基于真实机器人原型的环境特性。特别是，其中一个环境包含单路径点和多路径点，另一个仅包含多路径点。结果表明，与A*算法相比，遗传算法的执行时间不依赖于环境的基数，它生成更多样化的路径，从而提高了机器人的韧性。
Summary / 总结
This study aims to enhance the resilience of continuum robots through improved motion planning by integrating multi-criteria decision-making. It modifies two path-planning algorithms—Genetic Algorithm and A*—by incorporating the Analytical Hierarchy Process to evaluate paths based on four resilience-related criteria: distance, motor damage, mechanical damage, and accuracy. Experiments were conducted in two simulated environments, one with single and multi-path points and another with only multi-path points. Results indicate that while A* performance varies with environmental complexity, the Genetic Algorithm consistently maintains stable execution time and generates more diverse paths, thereby improving the robot’s resilience under varying conditions.
本研究旨在通过多准则决策优化运动规划，提升连续体机器人的鲁棒性。研究对遗传算法和A*算法进行了改进，引入层次分析法以依据四个关键指标——路径距离、电机损伤、机械损伤和精度——评估路径质量。实验在两种模拟环境中进行，一种含单路径与多路径点，另一种仅含多路径点。结果表明，相较于A*算法，遗传算法在环境复杂度变化下性能稳定，且生成路径更具多样性，从而有效提升机器人鲁棒性并降低维护频率。
Contagion Networks: Evaluator Bias Propagation in Multi-Agent LLM Systems
Authors: Zewen Liu
First: 2026-06-18T17:09:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:09:34+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When large language models serve as evaluators in multi-agent systems, their systematic evaluation biases propagate through the agent network. We introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework for measuring how evaluator biases spread across interacting LLM agents. In a controlled 3-agent experiment using DeepSeek-chat with three distinct evaluator bias profiles (structured, balanced, evidence-based), we measure the Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_3 and find that evaluator biases consistently propagate between agents (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]), even within the same underlying model. We identify three propagation regimes governed by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N), and demonstrate that homogeneous-model agents produce contagion coefficients 3-5x weaker than cross-model coefficients observed in prior work (MM-EPC: gamma approx 0.85-1.3), placing them in the suppression regime. We show that increasing evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, providing an actionable mitigation strategy. We release the open-source Contagion Network experimental framework.
中文标题/摘要
标题：传染网络：多智能体大语言模型系统中评估者偏见的传播
当大语言模型作为多智能体系统中的评估者时，其系统性评估偏见会通过智能体网络传播。我们提出了传染网络，一个用于衡量评估者偏见在交互式大语言模型智能体间传播的正式框架。在使用DeepSeek-chat进行的受控三智能体实验中，我们设置了三种不同的评估者偏见配置（结构化、平衡、证据导向），测量了跨智能体传染矩阵Gamma_3，发现评估者偏见在智能体间持续传播（gamma在[0.157, 0.352]区间），即使在相同基础模型内亦然。我们识别出三种传播模式，由矩阵Gamma_N的谱半径rho(Gamma_N)决定，并证明同质模型智能体的传染系数比先前研究（MM-EPC：gamma约0.85-1.3）中观察到的跨模型系数弱3-5倍，处于抑制模式。我们进一步表明，将评估委员会规模从k=1增加到k=3可使有效传染降低72.4%，提供了一种可操作的缓解策略。我们开源了传染网络实验框架。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the propagation of evaluator bias in multi-agent systems using large language models, motivated by the risk that biased evaluations can spread across agents. The authors introduce Contagion Networks, a formal framework to quantify bias transmission via a Cross-Agent Contagion Matrix Gamma_N, and conduct controlled experiments with three distinct bias profiles in a 3-agent DeepSeek-chat setup. Results show consistent bias propagation (gamma in [0.157, 0.352]) even within the same model, with propagation regimes determined by the spectral radius rho(Gamma_N). Homogeneous-model agents exhibit significantly weaker contagion (3-5x less than prior cross-model work) and fall into a suppression regime. Increasing the evaluator committee size from k=1 to k=3 reduces effective contagion by 72.4%, offering a practical mitigation strategy.
为应对多智能体LLM系统中评估偏见传播的风险，本文提出Contagion Networks框架，用于量化偏见在智能体间的传播。通过控制3智能体实验，使用DeepSeek-chat和三种不同评估偏见配置，测量跨智能体传染矩阵Gamma_3，发现即使在同一模型内，偏见仍持续传播（gamma在[0.157, 0.352]区间）。分析表明，传播行为由谱半径rho(Gamma_N)划分出三种传播模式，同模型智能体的传染系数仅为跨模型系统的3-5倍，处于抑制区。将评估委员会规模从k=1提升至k=3，可使有效传染降低72.4%，提供切实可行的缓解方案。
Fast Human Attention Prediction for Fixation-guided Active Perception in Autonomous Navigation
Authors: Fatma Youssef Mohammed, Grzegorz Malczyk, Kostas Alexis
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-18T17:08:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:08:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human visual attention relies on structured scanpaths to efficiently process scenes, yet instilling this behavior into robot autonomy is in its infancy and hindered by the high,computational costs of existing predictive models. To address this, we introduce GazeLNN, a computationally lightweight,scanpath prediction model that leverages Liquid Neural Networks as its recurrent engine and employs MobileNetV3 for feature extraction. Operating auto-regressively, the architecture predicts sequential fixation heatmaps conditioned on the current visual stimulus and fixation history. Despite requiring only 0.61 GFLOPs, GazeLNN achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MIT Low Resolution dataset achieving 0.47 ScanMatch score. It outperforms existing recurrent baselines across diverse evaluation metrics, while reducing computational costs by 99.40% and accelerating inference by up to six times. To investigate the role of human attention modeling in robot autonomy and demonstrate the practical utility of this highly efficient architecture, we integrate GazeLNN into an active camera-robot control policy trained via Reinforcement Learning. This integration enables human-fixation-guided perception during autonomous navigation, validated through successful real-world deployments on an aerial robot.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向自主导航的快速人类注意力预测与注视引导主动感知
人类视觉注意力依赖结构化扫视路径高效处理场景，但将此行为植入机器人自主系统尚处于起步阶段，受限于现有预测模型高昂的计算成本。为此，我们提出GazeLNN，一种计算轻量级的扫视路径预测模型，其循环引擎采用液态神经网络，特征提取采用MobileNetV3。该架构自回归式工作，根据当前视觉刺激与注视历史预测序列化注视热图。尽管仅需0.61 GFLOPs，GazeLNN在MIT低分辨率数据集上达到0.47的ScanMatch分数，性能领先当前最优。它在多种评估指标上超越现有循环基线，同时计算成本降低99.40%，推理速度提升最高达六倍。为探究人类注意力建模在机器人自主中的作用并验证该高效架构的实用价值，我们将GazeLNN集成至通过强化学习训练的主动相机-机器人控制策略中，实现自主导航中的注视引导感知，已在空中机器人真实部署中验证成功。
Summary / 总结
To enable efficient human-fixation-guided perception in autonomous robots, this work addresses the computational inefficiency of existing attention prediction models by introducing GazeLNN, a lightweight scanpath predictor using Liquid Neural Networks and MobileNetV3. The model predicts sequential fixation heatmaps autoregressively based on visual input and fixation history, achieving state-of-the-art 0.47 ScanMatch score on the MIT Low Resolution dataset with only 0.61 GFLOPs. It outperforms recurrent baselines across metrics while cutting computational cost by 99.40% and speeding up inference by up to six times. Integrated into a reinforcement learning-based robot control policy, GazeLNN enables real-world autonomous navigation with human-like attention guidance on an aerial robot.
为实现自主机器人中高效的人类注视引导感知，该研究提出轻量级扫描路径预测模型GazeLNN，采用液态神经网络与MobileNetV3结构，基于视觉输入与注视历史自回归预测序列注视热图。该模型在MIT低分辨率数据集上达到0.47的ScanMatch最优成绩，仅需0.61 GFLOPs，相较现有递归基线在多项指标上表现更优，计算成本降低99.4%，推理速度提升6倍。将其集成至强化学习训练的机器人控制策略中，成功引导无人机在自主导航中实现注视引导的视觉感知，已在真实场景中验证其实用价值。
A Smart-Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) EKF-FGO State Estimation
Authors: Eric Levy, Soosan Beheshti
First: 2026-06-14T23:13:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:07:20+00:00
Comments: This work has been accepted for presentation/publication at the 2026 IEEE Canadian Conference on Electrical and Computer Engineering (CCECE). The final published version will appear in IEEE Xplore
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable state estimation in robotics and control re quires balancing estimation accuracy against computational cost. While filtering-based methods such as the Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) provide efficient real-time updates, and optimisation based formulations using factor graphs improve global consistency, the role of optimisation scheduling is often treated implicitly rather than examined as an explicit design variable. This paper presents an experimental study that explicitly isolates optimisation scheduling using a Smart Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) EKF-FGO framework as a controlled testbed. By combining EKF-based state propagation with periodically invoked batch optimisation and holding solver structure and effort fixed, the main contribution of this work is the experimental characterisation of optimisation scheduling as an independent design variable governing the trade-off between intermediate estimation accuracy and computational cost. Simulation results in a planar SLAM environment show that scheduling strongly influences pre optimisation drift, transient error behaviour, and runtime. In particular, the results identify operating regimes in which most of the benefit of global optimisation can be retained at a fraction of the computational cost, highlighting optimisation scheduling as an under-explored yet critical consideration in hybrid state estimation systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：智能调度混合（SSH）EKF-FGO状态估计
机器人与控制领域中可靠的状态估计需要在估计精度与计算成本之间取得平衡。虽然基于滤波的方法（如扩展卡尔曼滤波器EKF）能提供高效的实时更新，而基于优化的因子图方法可提升全局一致性，但优化调度的作用常被隐式处理，而非作为显式设计变量进行考察。本文通过智能调度混合（SSH）EKF-FGO框架作为受控实验平台，显式隔离优化调度的影响。通过结合EKF状态传播与周期性调用批处理优化，并固定求解器结构与计算强度，本研究的主要贡献是实验性地刻画优化调度作为独立设计变量，调控中间估计精度与计算成本之间的权衡。在平面SLAM仿真环境中，结果表明调度显著影响优化前漂移、瞬态误差行为和运行时间。特别是，结果识别出若干工作区域，在这些区域中，可保留大部分全局优化的优势，同时仅需极小的计算成本，凸显优化调度在混合状态估计系统中被低估却至关重要的作用。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the need to balance estimation accuracy and computational cost in hybrid state estimation systems, particularly in robotics. It introduces a Smart-Scheduled Hybrid (SSH) framework that combines EKF-based state propagation with periodically triggered batch optimisation using factor graphs, while keeping solver structure and effort constant. Experimental results in a planar SLAM environment demonstrate that scheduling optimisation explicitly affects pre-optimisation drift, transient error, and runtime, revealing regimes where global optimisation benefits can be preserved at significantly reduced computational cost, thereby highlighting scheduling as a critical yet under-explored design variable.
该研究旨在解决混合状态估计算法中精度与计算成本之间的平衡问题，传统EKF方法虽能实现实时更新但缺乏全局一致性，而因子图优化虽能提升精度却代价高昂。作者提出一种智能调度混合（SSH）EKF-FGO框架，结合EKF状态传播与周期性触发的批处理优化，固定求解器结构与计算强度以将调度作为独立设计变量。在平面SLAM仿真中，实验结果表明调度显著影响优化前漂移、瞬态误差和运行时间，发现存在若干工作区间可在大幅降低计算成本的同时保留全局优化的大部分优势，凸显调度作为关键但尚未充分探索的参数在混合估计算法中的重要性。
Benign overfitting beyond prediction: The ordinary least squares interpolator
Authors: Dennis Shen, Dogyoon Song, Peng Ding, Jasjeet S. Sekhon
First: 2023-09-27T16:41:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:07:01+00:00
Comments: This work is accepted for publication in Biometrika
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in deep learning have highlighted the phenomenon of benign overfitting in overparameterized statistical models, sparking significant interest in understanding its foundations. Owing to its simplicity and practical relevance, the ordinary least squares (OLS) interpolator has become a key object of study for gaining theoretical insight into this phenomenon. While the properties of OLS are well understood in classical underparameterized settings, its behavior in the overparameterized regime -- unlike that of ridge regression or the lasso -- remains comparatively less explored. We contribute to this growing literature by deriving new algebraic and statistical results for the minimum $\ell_2$-norm OLS interpolator. In contrast to much of the existing work, which focuses on prediction risk, we center our analysis on parameter estimation and inference, which are fundamental for many statistics and causal inference applications. Specifically, we establish overparameterized analogues of (i) the leave-$k$-out formulas, (ii) the omitted variable bias formula, and (iii) the Frisch-Waugh-Lovell theorem. Under the Gauss-Markov model, we further extend the Gauss-Markov theorem and analyze variance estimation under homoskedasticity in the overparameterized setting. Collectively, these results provide a systematic framework for studying parameter estimation and inference in overparameterized linear models, offering a novel perspective on benign overfitting beyond its implications for prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越预测的良性过拟合：普通最小二乘插值器
深度学习的最新进展凸显了在过度参数化统计模型中出现的良性过拟合现象，激发了对其基础机制的广泛研究兴趣。由于其简单性和实际相关性，普通最小二乘（OLS）插值器已成为理解该现象的重要理论研究对象。尽管在经典欠参数化设置下OLS的性质已被充分理解，但在过度参数化情形下——与岭回归或Lasso不同——其行为仍相对较少被探索。本文通过推导最小ℓ₂范数OLS插值器的新代数与统计结果，为这一新兴文献做出贡献。与现有多数聚焦于预测风险的研究不同，本文重点分析参数估计与推断，这对许多统计学和因果推断应用至关重要。具体而言，我们建立了过度参数化情形下的（i）留一k法公式、（ii）遗漏变量偏差公式和（iii）弗里施-沃格-洛维尔定理的类比。在高斯-马尔可夫模型下，我们进一步扩展了高斯-马尔可夫定理，并分析了在同方差假设下的方差估计问题。这些结果共同构建了一个系统框架，用于研究过度参数化线性模型中的参数估计与推断，为理解良性过拟合现象提供了超越预测影响的全新视角。

Motivated by the need to understand benign overfitting beyond prediction risk, this paper investigates the ordinary least squares (OLS) interpolator in overparameterized linear models, where its parameter estimation properties are less studied than those of ridge or lasso. The authors derive new algebraic and statistical results, including overparameterized analogues of leave-k-out, omitted variable bias, and Frisch-Waugh-Lovell theorems, and extend the Gauss-Markov theorem to analyze variance estimation under homoskedasticity. 为理解良性过拟合在预测之外的统计特性，本文研究了在过参数化线性模型中普通最小二乘（OLS）插值器的参数估计性质，其研究尚不及岭回归或Lasso深入。作者推导了最小 $\ell_2$-范数OLS解的新代数与统计结果，聚焦于推断与估计而非预测风险，建立了留一法、遗漏变量偏差及弗里施-沃-洛夫定理在过参数化情形下的类比形式，并扩展高斯-马尔可夫定理以分析同方差条件下的方差估计。这些成果为过参数化模型中的参数推断提供了系统理论框架，表明OLS即使在过拟合情况下仍可提供准确估计与有效推断。
How Fragile Are Training-Free AI-Generated Image Detectors? A Controlled Audit of Score Direction, Preprocessing, and Compression
Authors: Jingwen Zhou, Mingzhe Wang
First: 2026-06-18T17:05:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:05:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training-free detectors of AI-generated images promise generator-agnostic deployment without classifier training, yet their reported numbers are rarely compared under a single controlled protocol. We audit two representative training-free scores -- an autoencoder-reconstruction score (AEROBLADE-style) and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score (RIGID-style) -- plus a naive feature-kNN control, on a common 1,500-image GenImage-derived benchmark spanning seven generators and JPEG compression at quality 70 and 50. The audit yields three cautionary findings. (i) Implementation details masquerade as method differences: replacing the LPIPS backbone (AlexNet -> VGG-16) changes overall AUROC by +0.085, and switching between resize-to-512 and native-resolution preprocessing flips per-generator conclusions by up to 0.38 AUROC. (ii) Score direction is not a property of the method but of its hyperparameters: the RIGID-style score is inverted (AUROC < 0.5) on SD1.5 and Wukong at noise level sigma=0.05, recovers to >0.5 for every generator at sigma=0.01, and collapses to 0.15 at sigma=0.3. (iii) Dataset format bias inflates robustness claims: without unified re-encoding, AUROC under JPEG-50 exceeds the clean condition for the AlexNet-backbone reconstruction score; after bias correction the residual anomaly localizes to a single generator (BigGAN). The audited scores have complementary per-generator failure sets, but naive z-score fusion does not beat the best single score, indicating that exploiting complementarity requires direction-aware combination.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无训练AI生成图像检测器有多脆弱？对评分方向、预处理和压缩的受控审计
无训练AI生成图像检测器承诺无需分类器训练即可实现生成器无关部署，但其报告数值极少在统一受控协议下比较。我们审计了两种代表性无训练评分——自编码器重建评分（AEROBLADE风格）和噪声扰动特征相似性评分（RIGID风格），以及一个朴素的特征kNN对照，在一个涵盖七种生成器、JPEG压缩质量70和50的1500张图像GenImage衍生基准上进行。审计得出三项警示性发现：(i) 实现细节伪装成方法差异：替换LPIPS主干网络（AlexNet→VGG-16）使整体AUROC提升0.085，切换512分辨率缩放与原分辨率预处理可使各生成器结论翻转达0.38 AUROC。(ii) 评分方向并非方法固有属性，而是其超参数决定：RIGID风格评分在SD1.5和Wukong生成器、噪声水平sigma=0.05时呈反转（AUROC<0.5），在sigma=0.01时对所有生成器恢复至>0.5，而在sigma=0.3时降至0.15。(iii) 数据集格式偏差夸大了鲁棒性声明：未经统一重编码时，AlexNet主干重建评分在JPEG-50条件下的AUROC高于无损条件；经偏差校正后，异常残留集中于单一生成器（BigGAN）。审计评分在各生成器失败模式上互补，但朴素z分数融合未超越最佳单一评分，表明利用互补性需方向感知组合。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates the reliability of training-free AI image detectors by auditing two representative methods — an autoencoder-reconstruction score and a noise-perturbation feature-similarity score — alongside a naive kNN baseline, across seven image generators and JPEG compression levels. The motivation stems from the lack of standardized evaluation for such detectors, which claim universal applicability without retraining. The audit reveals that implementation choices — such as backbone architecture or preprocessing resolution — significantly alter performance metrics, sometimes reversing conclusions. Score direction is shown to be hyperparameter-dependent, not methodological, with RIGID-style scores flipping from poor to excellent performance as noise level changes. Dataset format bias also inflates robustness claims, with one generator (BigGAN) consistently underperforming after bias correction. While the detectors show complementary failure patterns, simple fusion does not outperform the best individual method, suggesting that effective combination requires direction-aware strategies.
该研究通过审计两种代表性训练免检测器——自编码器重建分数与噪声扰动特征相似性分数——以及一个朴素kNN基线，评估其在统一基准（1500张来自七种生成器的图像，JPEG压缩质量70和50）上的可靠性。研究发现，实现细节（如骨干网络架构或预处理分辨率）显著影响性能，AUROC可变化达0.38；分数方向取决于超参数而非方法本身，例如RIGID风格分数在不同噪声水平下会从AUROC<0.5翻转至>0.5；数据格式偏差会夸大鲁棒性，JPEG-50条件下部分方法AUROC高于无损情况，经偏差校正后仅BigGAN出现异常。尽管各方法失败模式互补，但简单融合分数无法超越最佳单个检测器。
Beyond Global Replanning: Hierarchical Recovery for Cross-Device Agent Systems
Authors: Shu Yao, Yuhua Luo, Qian Long, Jingru Fan, Zhuoyuan Yu, Yuheng Wang, Lin Wu, Yufan Dang, Huatao Li, Chen Qian
First: 2026-06-18T17:04:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:04:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-world computer-use tasks often span multiple applications and devices, requiring agents to coordinate heterogeneous environments under dynamic runtime failures. Existing multi-device agent systems support task decomposition and cross-device assignment, but recovery remains largely coarse-grained: when execution fails, they typically retry the same strategy, reassign the subtask, or revise the global plan, without systematically modeling the device-local strategy space. This limits their ability to distinguish failures that can be repaired within the current device from those that require cross-device replanning. We propose \textbf{H-RePlan}, a hierarchical replanning framework for multi-device agents with unified API--CLI--GUI execution. H-RePlan equips each device with interchangeable execution strategies and separates device-local strategy recovery from orchestrator-level global replanning through a compact cross-layer failure abstraction. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{HeraBench}, a fault-injected benchmark that constructs cross-device workflows over Linux and Android devices and injects strategy- and device-level failures. Experiments show that H-RePlan substantially outperforms single-strategy and coarse-grained multi-device baselines, achieving higher completion, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rates while reducing the token cost required for reliable end-to-end success. These results demonstrate that scope-aware hierarchical recovery is essential for robust multi-device agent execution.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越全局重规划：面向跨设备代理系统的分层恢复
现实世界中的计算机使用任务常跨越多个应用和设备，要求代理在动态运行时故障下协调异构环境。现有跨设备代理系统支持任务分解与跨设备分配，但恢复机制仍较粗粒度：当执行失败时，通常仅重试相同策略、重新分配子任务或修订全局计划，未系统建模设备本地策略空间。这限制了其区分可于当前设备内修复的故障与需跨设备重规划故障的能力。我们提出\textbf{H-RePlan}，一种支持统一API--CLI--GUI执行的多设备代理分层重规划框架。H-RePlan为每个设备配备可互换的执行策略，并通过紧凑的跨层故障抽象，将设备本地策略恢复与编排层全局重规划分离。为评估该能力，我们引入\textbf{HeraBench}，一个注入故障的基准测试，构建Linux与Android设备上的跨设备工作流，并注入策略级与设备级故障。实验表明，H-RePlan显著优于单一策略及粗粒度多设备基线，实现更高完成率、指令遵循率与完美通过率，同时降低可靠端到端成功所需的token成本。结果表明，具备范围感知的分层恢复对鲁棒多设备代理执行至关重要。

Experimental results show H-RePlan significantly outperforms baseline approaches in completion rate, instruction adherence, and perfect-pass rate while reducing token cost for reliable execution.
为提升多设备环境下动态故障的鲁棒性协调能力，本文提出H-RePlan框架，通过紧凑的跨层故障抽象，将设备本地策略恢复与全局编排器重规划分离，支持各设备采用可互换的执行策略。通过引入注入策略与设备级故障的HeraBench基准测试，实验表明H-RePlan在任务完成率、指令遵循度和完美通过率上显著优于基线方法，同时降低实现可靠端到端执行所需的token开销。
Optimal Order of Multi-Agent and General Many-Body Systems
Authors: Jake J. Xia
First: 2026-06-18T17:03:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:03:03+00:00
Comments: Key Words: Many body systems, multi agent crowd interactions, feedback loops, agent power, response function, utility function, risk appetite, order, optimal order, fragility, mobility, synchronization, useful energy, entropy, concentration, correlation, task dependency, receiver dependency, collective intelligence, AI model scaling law
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper develops a general framework for analyzing multi-agent systems with feedback loops between agents actions and collective observations. The framework is built on two fundamental agent-level variables: power, which measures agent influence on collective outcomes, and response functions, which determine how agents react to observations. We derive how macroscopic properties, including total power, useful power, entropy, order, fragility, and mobility, emerge from these two variables of heterogeneous agents. To study the trade off between growth and resilience, we introduce a system-level utility function parameterized by a risk-appetite coefficient and derive an optimal degree of order that balances productivity, stability, and adaptability. The analysis suggests that stronger synchronization can increase collective output but may also increase systemic fragility and reduce mobility. We further argue that order, entropy, information, and useful energy are task-dependent and system-relative concepts whose meanings depend on the objectives of the system. By measuring and designing agent power distributions and response functions, it may be possible to better understand, predict, and optimize collective behavior and identify the conditions under which collective intelligence and optimal order emerge.
