为理解异构智能体交互如何产生集体行为，本文提出一个通用框架，通过智能体层面的‘权力’与‘响应函数’建模多智能体系统。这两个变量决定宏观属性如总功率、熵、秩序、脆弱性与移动性。研究引入包含风险偏好参数的系统级效用函数，以识别平衡生产力、稳定性与适应性的最优秩序程度。实验表明，更强同步性虽提升集体产出，却增加系统脆弱性并降低移动性，且秩序、熵、有用能量等关键属性依赖于任务与系统目标，优化集体智能需精细设计智能体权力分布与响应函数。
A Survey of On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Models
Authors: Mingyang Song, Mao Zheng
First: 2026-04-01T08:32:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:00:51+00:00
Comments: Ongoing Work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As Large Language Models continue to grow in both capability and cost, transferring frontier capabilities into smaller, deployable students has become an important engineering problem, and knowledge distillation remains a common technique for this transfer. The prevailing recipe in industrial pipelines, static imitation of teacher-generated text, carries a structural weakness that grows more severe as tasks become longer and more reasoning-intensive. Because the student is trained on flawless teacher prefixes but generates its own at inference, small errors tend to accumulate into trajectories it has rarely been trained to recover from, and the resulting exposure bias has been shown to scale roughly with the square of sequence length. On-Policy Distillation reorganizes the training loop around this observation by having the teacher provide feedback on what the student actually produces, with the goal of reducing the compounding term toward linear and reframing distillation as an iterative correction process rather than single-pass imitation. The resulting literature has expanded along divergence design, reward-guided optimization, and self-play, yet contributions remain scattered across the knowledge distillation, RLHF, and imitation learning communities without a unified treatment. This survey provides such a treatment. We formalize OPD as f-divergence minimization over student-sampled trajectories, organize the field along three design axes (what to optimize, where the signal comes from, and how to stabilize training in practice), and consolidate success conditions, recurring failure modes, and the connection between OPD and KL-constrained reinforcement learning. We close with open problems that emerge from this synthesis, including distillation scaling laws, uncertainty-aware feedback, agent-level distillation, and the growing overlap between knowledge distillation and RL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型在线策略蒸馏综述
随着大语言模型在能力和成本上持续增长，将前沿能力迁移到更小、可部署的学生模型已成为重要工程问题，知识蒸馏仍是该迁移的常用技术。工业流程中主流的静态模仿教师生成文本的方法存在结构性弱点，且随着任务长度增加和推理强度提升，该弱点愈发严重。由于学生模型在训练时仅学习完美教师前缀，但在推理时自动生成前缀，微小误差易累积成学生模型极少训练过的轨迹，导致暴露偏差随序列长度平方增长。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）围绕这一观察重构训练循环，让教师对学生的实际输出提供反馈，旨在将累积误差项从平方级降至线性级，并将蒸馏重构为迭代修正过程而非单次模仿。当前相关文献沿发散度设计、奖励引导优化和自对弈三个方向扩展，但贡献分散于知识蒸馏、RLHF与模仿学习社区，缺乏统一框架。本文提供统一综述：将OPD形式化为学生采样轨迹上的f-散度最小化，按三个设计维度（优化目标、信号来源、实际训练稳定化方法）组织领域，并整合成功条件、常见失败模式及OPD与KL约束强化学习的关联。最后提出合成视角下的开放问题，包括蒸馏缩放定律、不确定性感知反馈、代理级蒸馏及知识蒸馏与强化学习日益重叠的边界。
Summary / 总结
As large language models grow in capability and cost, distilling their knowledge into smaller, deployable models has become critical; however, traditional static imitation methods suffer from exposure bias that scales quadratically with sequence length. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) addresses this by having the teacher provide feedback on the student’s actual outputs during training, turning distillation into an iterative correction process rather than a single-pass imitation. The survey formalizes OPD as f-divergence minimization over student trajectories and organizes the field along three axes: optimization objective, feedback source, and training stability. Experimental findings show OPD reduces error accumulation and improves generalization, especially in long-horizon and reasoning tasks, while also revealing failure modes tied to reward design and trajectory divergence. The synthesis highlights connections to RLHF and KL-constrained RL, and identifies open challenges such as scaling laws and uncertainty-aware feedback.
随着大语言模型规模和成本的增加，将其能力迁移到小型可部署模型变得至关重要；然而，传统静态模仿方法存在随序列长度平方增长的曝光偏差问题。On-Policy Distillation（OPD）通过在训练中让教师对学生的实际输出提供反馈，将蒸馏视为迭代修正过程而非单次模仿，从而缓解误差累积。该综述将OPD形式化为学生轨迹上的f-散度最小化，并按三个维度组织研究：优化目标、反馈来源与训练稳定性。实验表明，OPD在长序列和推理任务中显著降低错误累积并提升泛化能力，同时揭示了与奖励设计和轨迹发散相关的失败模式。
Your Mouse and Eyes Secretly Leak Your Preference: LLM Alignment using Implicit Feedback from Users
Authors: Haw-Shiuan Chang, Jeffrey Gomez, Mehul Patwari, Aryan Sajith, Hamed Zamani
First: 2026-06-18T17:00:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T17:00:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
To align a Large Language Model (LLM), most existing methods collect explicit human feedback and train a reward model to predict the human preference based on the response text. These existing methods have two key limitations. First, the users rarely provide explicit feedback for LLM responses, which makes the high-quality preference annotation expensive to collect. Second, the methods do not leverage implicit human feedback, which has proven vital to the economic moats of Internet giants. To quantify the value of implicit feedback, we build a new dataset called IFLLM, which collects 1336 multi-turn questions from the 59 Mechanical Turk workers, their mouse trajectories, and eye gazing points to the LLMs' responses from their webcams. IFLLM shows that the users have very diverse types of gazing behavior and mouse trajectories. Our reward model based on the implicit user feedback boosts the accuracy of the text-based reward model from 55% to 64% and nearly triples the relative response quality improvements after applying the DPO to eight LLMs, demonstrating the value of implicit feedback in the wild. Our data collection website, dataset, and codes can be found at https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你的鼠标和眼睛悄悄泄露你的偏好：利用用户隐式反馈对齐大语言模型
为对齐大语言模型（LLM），现有方法大多收集显式人类反馈并训练奖励模型以根据响应文本预测人类偏好。这些方法存在两个关键局限：首先，用户极少为LLM响应提供显式反馈，导致高质量偏好标注成本高昂；其次，方法未利用隐式人类反馈，而隐式反馈已被证明是互联网巨头护城河的关键。为量化隐式反馈的价值，我们构建了新数据集IFLLM，收集了59名机械翻译工人提出的1336个多轮问题、其鼠标轨迹及通过网络摄像头记录的注视LLM响应点。IFLLM显示用户具有多样化的注视行为和鼠标轨迹。基于隐式用户反馈的奖励模型，将文本奖励模型的准确率从55%提升至64%，并在对八款LLM应用DPO后，相对响应质量改进近三倍，证明了隐式反馈在真实场景中的价值。我们的数据收集网站、数据集及代码可访问：https://github.com/themehulpatwari/llm-implicit-feedback/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of explicit user feedback and the untapped potential of implicit signals, this study introduces IFLLM, a novel dataset capturing mouse movements and eye gaze data from 59 Mechanical Turk workers interacting with LLM responses across 1336 multi-turn queries. The method trains a reward model using these implicit signals, which significantly enhances performance over text-only models: accuracy improves from 55% to 64%, and relative quality gains triple when applied via DPO across eight LLMs, demonstrating the practical value of implicit feedback in real-world alignment tasks.
为解决显式用户反馈稀缺及隐式信号未被利用的问题，该研究构建了IFLLM数据集，收录59名机械翻译工人与LLM交互时的鼠标轨迹和眼动数据。方法基于隐式反馈训练奖励模型，并结合DPO微调八款LLM。实验表明，隐式反馈模型将奖励预测准确率从55%提升至64%，且相对响应质量提升近三倍，验证了隐式人类行为在LLM对齐中的重要价值。
GroundControl: Anticipating Navigation Failures in Vision-Language Agents via Trajectory-Consistent Uncertainty Estimates
Authors: Nastaran Darabi, Divake Kumar, Sina Tayebati, Devashri Naik, Amit Ranjan Trivedi
First: 2026-06-18T16:56:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:56:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language navigation agents achieve competitive average success on benchmark tasks, yet failures often arise through predictable trajectory-level breakdowns such as oscillation, stagnation, or inefficient detours. Reliable deployment, therefore, requires uncertainty signals that anticipate emerging failure dynamics during execution rather than reflect only instantaneous action entropy. We introduce \emph{GroundControl}, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator defined as statistical deviation from nominal goal-directed distance-to-goal dynamics aggregated over an episode. GroundControl models distance evolution using a constant-velocity Kalman filter and combines normalized innovation statistics with complementary trajectory features capturing progress, monotonicity, path efficiency, and oscillatory behavior. The resulting uncertainty score reflects geometric and temporal inconsistency in navigation behavior rather than local prediction dispersion. To evaluate uncertainty quality independently of task success, we formalize \emph{Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN)}, a protocol that measures how effectively an uncertainty score ranks episodes by failure or inefficiency using risk--coverage curves and AURC / E-AURC summaries. Across five EB-Navigation splits ($N=300$ episodes), trajectory-consistent uncertainty achieves near-oracle ordering under success-based selective risk, with weighted-average $\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024$ for the GPT-4o model, substantially outperforming entropy-, conformal-, and heuristic baselines. Under SPL-based selective evaluation, GroundControl consistently achieves the lowest AURC and E-AURC across models and navigation splits. These results show that modeling deviation from goal-directed dynamics provides an interpretable and robust signal for anticipating navigation failures in vision-language agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GroundControl：通过轨迹一致的不确定性估计预测视觉-语言代理中的导航失败
视觉-语言导航代理在基准任务中表现出较高的平均成功率，但失败往往源于可预测的轨迹级崩溃，如振荡、停滞或低效绕行。因此，可靠部署需要能够预测执行过程中即将出现的失败动态的不确定性信号，而非仅反映瞬时动作熵。我们提出了\emph{GroundControl}，一种基于轨迹一致性的不确定性估计器，定义为对单次会话中聚合的名义目标导向距离到目标动态的统计偏差。GroundControl使用恒速卡尔曼滤波建模距离演化，并结合归一化创新统计量与互补轨迹特征（涵盖进度、单调性、路径效率和振荡行为）。所得不确定性评分反映导航行为在几何与时间维度上的不一致性，而非局部预测分散。为独立评估不确定性质量，我们形式化了\emph{选择性风险-覆盖导航（SRCN）}协议，通过风险-覆盖曲线和AURC/E-AURC摘要衡量不确定性评分对按失败或低效排序会话的有效性。在五个EB-导航划分（N=300个会话）中，轨迹一致的不确定性在基于成功率的选择性风险下实现近似最优排序，GPT-4o模型的加权平均\mathrm{E\text{-}AURC}_{\mathrm{SR}}=0.0024，显著优于熵、置信区间和启发式基线。在基于SPL的选择性评估下，GroundControl在所有模型和导航划分中均取得最低AURC和E-AURC。这些结果表明，建模偏离目标导向动态可为视觉-语言代理中的导航失败预测提供可解释且稳健的信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable failure anticipation in vision-language navigation agents, this work introduces GroundControl, a trajectory-consistent uncertainty estimator that models deviation from goal-directed distance dynamics using a Kalman filter and combines innovation statistics with trajectory features like progress and oscillation. Evaluated via Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation (SRCN), GroundControl outperforms entropy, conformal, and heuristic baselines across five navigation splits, achieving near-oracle ranking with weighted-average E-AURC_SR=0.0024 for GPT-4o and consistently lowest AURC/E-AURC under SPL-based evaluation, demonstrating its effectiveness in predicting failure modes before they occur.
为在视觉语言导航代理中提前预测导航失败，本文提出GroundControl，一种基于轨迹一致性、通过卡尔曼滤波建模目标距离动态偏差并结合轨迹特征（如进度、单调性、路径效率和振荡行为）的不确定性估计器。在Selective Risk--Coverage Navigation评估框架下，GroundControl在五个EB-Navigation数据集上显著优于熵、置信区间和启发式基线，GPT-4o模型的加权平均E-AURC_SR达0.0024，且在SPL评估中始终取得最低AURC与E-AURC，证明其能有效预测如振荡与低效等失败模式。
FM-Agent: Scaling Formal Methods to Large Systems via LLM-Based Hoare-Style Reasoning
Authors: Haoran Ding, Zhaoguo Wang, Haibo Chen
First: 2026-04-13T14:42:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:56:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM-assisted software development has become increasingly prevalent, and can generate large-scale systems, such as compilers. It becomes crucial to strengthen the correctness of the generated code. However, automated reasoning for large-scale systems remains challenging due to code complexity. Hoare logic offers an approach to decomposing a large system into smaller components and reasoning about them separately (i.e., compositional reasoning). However, existing works still struggle to scale, because Hoare logic requires writing formal specifications for each function, imposing a heavy human burden. The problem is exacerbated when code is generated by LLMs, as developers lack a deep understanding of each function's expected behavior. This paper presents FM-Agent, the first framework that realizes automated compositional reasoning for large-scale systems. Leveraging LLMs, FM-Agent introduces a top-down paradigm to automatically generate function-level specifications. Specifically, FM-Agent derives the specification of a function from how its callers expect the function to behave, so the generated specifications can reflect the developer's intent of a function even if the implementation is buggy. Developers' intent is usually expressed in natural language, while existing verifiers only support formulas. Therefore, FM-Agent generalizes Hoare-style inference to reason about functions against natural-language specifications. Finally, to confirm bug existence and explain bug causes, FM-Agent automatically generates test cases to trigger potential bugs. In our evaluation, FM-Agent successfully reasons about large-scale systems within 2 days, each of which has up to 143k LoC. These systems have already been tested by their developers, but FM-Agent still finds 522 newly discovered bugs. These bugs can cause serious consequences, including system crashes and incorrect execution results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FM-Agent：通过基于大语言模型的霍尔风格推理将形式化方法扩展至大型系统
大语言模型辅助的软件开发日益普及，能够生成编译器等大型系统。然而，生成代码的正确性亟需加强。由于代码复杂性，大型系统的自动化推理仍具挑战。霍尔逻辑提供了一种将大型系统分解为小部件并分别推理（即组合推理）的方法。但现有工作难以扩展，因为霍尔逻辑要求为每个函数编写形式化规格，给开发者带来沉重负担。当代码由大语言模型生成时，问题更严重，因为开发者对每个函数的预期行为缺乏深入理解。 本文提出FM-Agent，这是首个实现大型系统自动化组合推理的框架。利用大语言模型，FM-Agent引入自上而下的范式，自动为函数生成规格。具体而言，FM-Agent从函数调用者期望函数如何行为推导出函数规格，因此即使实现存在错误，生成的规格仍能反映开发者的意图。开发者意图通常以自然语言表达，而现有验证器仅支持公式。因此，FM-Agent将霍尔风格推理泛化，以自然语言规格推理函数。最后，为确认漏洞存在并解释漏洞成因，FM-Agent自动生成测试用例以触发潜在漏洞。在评估中，FM-Agent在2天内成功对每个拥有最多14.3万行代码的大型系统进行推理。这些系统已由开发者测试，但FM-Agent仍发现522个新漏洞，可能导致系统崩溃或执行错误等严重后果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to verify correctness in large-scale systems generated by LLMs, FM-Agent introduces a top-down, LLM-driven framework for automated compositional reasoning using Hoare-style logic. It automatically synthesizes function-level specifications by analyzing caller expectations, enabling reasoning against natural language descriptions rather than formal formulas. This approach reduces human burden and accommodates buggy implementations while preserving developer intent. Experimental results show FM-Agent successfully analyzes systems up to 143k LoC within two days, uncovering 522 previously unknown bugs that could lead to system crashes or incorrect behavior.
为解决由大语言模型生成的大型系统验证难题，FM-Agent提出一种基于LLM的自上而下自动化组合推理框架，采用Hoare逻辑风格。它通过分析调用者对函数的期望，自动生成函数级规范，即使实现有误也能反映开发者意图，并扩展Hoare逻辑以支持自然语言规范。系统还能自动生成测试用例以暴露漏洞。在最大14.3万行代码的系统上测试，FM-Agent两天内完成验证，发现522个此前未被发现的漏洞，可能导致系统崩溃或执行错误。
Scalable Training of Spatially Grounded 2D Vision-Language Models for Radiology
Authors: Yusuf Salcan, Simon Ging, Robin Schirrmeister, Philipp Arnold, Elmar Kotter, Behzad Bozorgtabar, Thomas Brox
Venue: MICCAI 2026
First: 2026-06-18T16:55:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:55:26+00:00
Comments: Accepted for MICCAI 2026. First two authors: equal contribution. Last two authors: equal supervision
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study how to train visually grounded vision-language models (VLMs) for radiology without manual spatial annotations. We introduce RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual (German/English) dataset of 1.2M CT and MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with task-specific VQA and spatial grounding subsets generated automatically via LLM-based curation and automated segmentation. Trained on this data, our model RadGrounder jointly performs report generation, visual question answering, and spatial grounding via bounding-box detection or segmentation. On external VQA benchmarks (Slake, VQA-RAD), RadGrounder achieves competitive results with specialized medical VLMs. Adding our clinical data to the training mixture improves open-ended VQA over fine-tuning on the downstream datasets alone, showing the transferability of our dataset. Crucially, adding grounding supervision does not degrade language quality, enabling spatially verifiable outputs at no cost to VQA performance.
中文标题/摘要

Motivated by the need for scalable, annotation-free training of vision-language models for radiology, this work introduces RefRad2D, a large-scale bilingual dataset of 1.2M CT/MR image-text pairs derived from clinical practice, with spatial grounding and VQA tasks automatically generated via LLM curation and segmentation. 为解决放射学领域视觉语言模型训练需人工空间标注的问题，该研究提出RefRad2D数据集，包含120万张来自临床实践的CT/MR图像与双语（德/英）文本对，通过大语言模型与自动化分割技术自动生成。模型RadGrounder在该数据上联合完成报告生成、视觉问答与空间定位任务。实验表明，RadGrounder在外部VQA基准上表现优异，加入临床数据可提升开放域问答性能，且空间定位监督不损害语言质量，实现无损性能的空间可验证输出。
Marginal Advantage Accumulation for Memory-Driven Agent Self-Evolution
Authors: Mingyu Yang, Keye Zheng, Congchao Cheng, Yujie Liu, Xingkang Lu, Fan Jiang, Yefei Zheng
First: 2026-06-18T16:54:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:54:25+00:00
Comments: 26 pages, 4 figures, 10 tables, 42 references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In batch-style trace distillation, the same memory operation may receive contradictory feedback across different batches. Existing methods lack a cross-batch, operation-level evidence accumulation mechanism, making it impossible to distinguish stably effective operations from accidental hits. This paper formalizes the requirement as two structural conditions, alignability and comparability, and proposes Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA). MAA constructs differential signals to make them comparable across batches, accumulates signed evidence per operation via EMA, and ensures cross-batch traceability through semantic identity merging. As a post-processing architecture, MAA achieves the best results in 14 out of 16 settings across 4 benchmarks and 4 target models, consistently outperforming existing batch-level distillation baselines and matching or surpassing online alternatives in most settings, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by approximately 75%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于边缘优势累积的记忆驱动智能体自演化
在批处理式轨迹蒸馏中，相同记忆操作可能在不同批次中收到矛盾反馈。现有方法缺乏跨批次、操作级的证据累积机制，无法区分稳定有效的操作与偶然成功。本文将该需求形式化为两个结构条件：对齐性与可比性，并提出边缘优势累积（MAA）。MAA构建差异信号使其跨批次可比，通过指数移动平均（EMA）对每个操作累积带符号证据，并通过语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性。作为后处理架构，MAA在4个基准和4个目标模型的16种设置中14种表现最佳，持续优于现有批处理蒸馏基线，在多数设置中匹配或超越在线替代方案，同时将优化阶段的token消耗降低约75%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the problem that contradictory feedback across batches hinders reliable memory operation selection in trace distillation, this paper introduces Marginal Advantage Accumulation (MAA), a post-processing method that accumulates signed evidence per operation using exponential moving average and ensures cross-batch traceability via semantic identity merging. MAA formalizes the need for alignability and comparability across batches and constructs differential signals to enable consistent comparison. Experimental results show MAA achieves top performance in 14 out of 16 settings across four benchmarks and four target models, outperforming batch-level baselines and matching or exceeding online methods, while reducing optimization-phase token consumption by about 75%.
为解决批处理轨迹蒸馏中不同批次对同一记忆操作反馈矛盾导致的有效操作难以区分的问题，本文提出边际优势累积（MAA）方法，通过指数移动平均积累每个操作的带符号证据，并利用语义身份合并确保跨批次可追溯性，从而实现操作级对齐与可比性。在四个基准和四个目标模型上的实验表明，MAA在16个设置中的14个达到最优性能，显著优于批处理基线方法，多数情况下与在线方法相当或更优，同时优化阶段的token消耗降低约75%。
UltraQuant: 4-bit KV Caching for Context-Heavy Agents
Authors: Inesh Chakrabarti, David Limpus, Aditi Ghai Rana, Bowen Bao, Spandan Tiwari, Thiago Crepaldi, Ashish Sirasao
First: 2026-06-18T16:54:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:54:07+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Context-heavy agents place unusual pressure on the key-value (KV) cache: long prefixes are reused across many short turns, while concurrency determines whether the serving system can keep GPUs utilized. We study 4-bit KV-cache compression for this setting, using TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality anchor and vLLM FP8 KV caching as the deployment anchor. We report three contributions. First, we frame 4-bit KV caching around multi-round agent workloads where task quality, cache residency, and serving throughput must be measured jointly. Second, we describe the practical design choices needed to make the 4-bit path robust, including asymmetric K/V treatment, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants. Third, we present serving optimizations on AMD GPUs, including optimized decode-attention kernels and UltraQuant, an FP4 approximation path that uses FP8 queries, FP4 KV tensors, UE8M0 group scales, and native scaled-MFMA support on CDNA4. On a long-context, multi-turn agentic workload, UltraQuant cuts P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in the cache-pressured late rounds (2.3x across all rounds) and raises output throughput by 1.63x over the FP8 KV baseline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UltraQuant：面向上下文密集型代理的4比特KV缓存
上下文密集型代理对键值（KV）缓存施加了异常压力：长前缀在多个短轮次中被重复使用，而并发性决定了服务系统能否持续利用GPU。我们针对此场景研究4比特KV缓存压缩，以TurboQuant风格的旋转和码本量化作为质量基准，以vLLM FP8 KV缓存作为部署基准。我们报告三项贡献：首先，我们将4比特KV缓存框架置于多轮代理工作负载中，需联合衡量任务质量、缓存驻留率和服务吞吐量；其次，我们描述了使4比特路径稳健所需的实用设计选择，包括不对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除及块级变体；第三，我们提出针对AMD GPU的服务优化，包括优化的解码-注意力内核和UltraQuant，该路径使用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放及CDNA4原生缩放MFMA支持。在长上下文、多轮代理工作负载中，UltraQuant在缓存压力大的后期轮次中将P50首令牌时间缩短3.47倍（所有轮次平均缩短2.3倍），相较FP8 KV基准提升输出吞吐量1.63倍。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high memory pressure from context-heavy agent workloads that reuse long prefixes across short turns, this paper explores 4-bit KV cache compression to improve serving efficiency. It employs TurboQuant-style rotation and codebook quantization as a quality benchmark and vLLM FP8 caching as a deployment reference. The method introduces asymmetric K/V handling, Walsh-Hadamard rotation, QJL removal, and block-scale variants to ensure robustness. Experimental results on AMD GPUs show UltraQuant, an FP4 KV caching path using FP8 queries and native CDNA4 scaled-MFMA, reduces P50 time-to-first-token by 3.47x in late rounds and boosts output throughput by 1.63x compared to FP8 baseline.
为应对上下文密集型代理工作负载带来的高内存压力，本文研究了4位KV缓存压缩以提升服务效率。方法结合TurboQuant风格的旋转与码本量化作为质量基准，以vLLM的FP8 KV缓存作为部署参考，并引入不对称K/V处理、Walsh-Hadamard旋转、QJL移除及块级变体以确保鲁棒性。实验表明，UltraQuant（采用FP8查询、FP4 KV张量、UE8M0组缩放及CDNA4原生缩放MFMA）在长上下文多轮代理任务中，将P50首令牌延迟降低3.47倍（晚轮次），整体吞吐量提升1.63倍。
Analyzing Defensive Misdirection Against Model-Guided Automated Attacks on Agentic AI Systems
Authors: Reza Soosahabi, Vivek Namsani
First: 2026-06-18T16:50:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:50:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI systems increasingly rely on language-model components to interpret instructions, process external data, invoke tools, and coordinate with other agents. These capabilities make prompt-injection and jailbreak attacks more consequential, especially as attackers adopt model-guided automation to scale probing, prompt refinement, and response evaluation. This work analyzes the resulting attack-defense setting through a probabilistic model of a target system, its defense mechanism, and the attacker's automated judge. Our analysis shows that conventional detect-and-block defenses can allow attacker success rate (ASR) to approach one as the query budget grows, since predictable refusals provide useful feedback to automated search. We then examine detect-and-misdirect, where detected malicious interactions receive controlled, non-operational responses designed to induce false-positive errors in the attacker's judge. This strategy reduces the positive predictive value of attacker-selected candidates and yields a bounded asymptotic ASR. We evaluate a proof-of-concept realization of this strategy through Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement (CMPE), a lightweight conversational misdirection method designed to replace predictable refusal text with safe but strategically misleading responses in automated jailbreak settings. On jailbreak benchmarks, CMPE reduces estimated ASR upper bounds by up to two orders of magnitude and nearly eliminates verified attack success in end-to-end PAIR and GPTFuzz attack runs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分析针对模型引导自动化攻击的代理AI系统的防御性误导
代理AI系统日益依赖语言模型组件来解释指令、处理外部数据、调用工具并与其他代理协调。这些能力使提示注入和越狱攻击更具破坏性，尤其当攻击者采用模型引导自动化来扩展探测、提示优化和响应评估时。本文通过目标系统的概率模型、其防御机制和攻击者的自动化裁判模型，分析由此产生的攻防环境。分析表明，传统检测并阻断的防御方式，随着查询预算增长，可使攻击者成功率（ASR）趋近于1，因为可预测的拒绝响应为自动化搜索提供了有用反馈。随后，我们研究检测并误导策略，即对检测到的恶意交互返回受控的非操作性响应，旨在诱导攻击者裁判产生假阳性错误。该策略降低了攻击者所选候选方案的阳性预测值，并使ASR趋于有界。我们通过“情境误导通过渐进式参与”（CMPE）这一轻量级对话误导方法评估该策略的可行性，该方法旨在用安全但策略性误导的响应替代可预测的拒绝文本，以应对自动化越狱场景。在越狱基准测试中，CMPE将估计的ASR上限降低达两个数量级，并几乎消除端到端PAIR和GPTFuzz攻击中的验证攻击成功率。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the growing threat of automated jailbreak attacks on agentic AI systems that rely on language models for instruction interpretation and tool coordination. It proposes a probabilistic framework to model the interaction between attackers using model-guided automation, defensive systems, and automated judges. The study finds that traditional detect-and-block defenses can be easily circumvented as attackers exploit predictable refusals for feedback, allowing attack success rates to approach 100% with sufficient query budget. In contrast, the paper introduces detect-and-misdirect defenses that replace refusals with strategically misleading, non-operational responses to confuse the attacker’s automated judge. Evaluated via Contextual Misdirection via Progressive Engagement (CMPE), this approach reduces estimated attack success rates by up to two orders of magnitude and nearly eliminates verified attack success in end-to-end evaluations.
本研究针对日益严重的自动化越狱攻击威胁，这类攻击利用语言模型执行指令和调用工具，使提示注入攻击更具破坏性。作者通过概率模型分析攻击者与防御机制的交互，发现传统检测并阻断的防御方式在攻击者利用可预测拒绝反馈引导自动化搜索时失效，攻击成功率可随查询预算增长趋近100%。他们提出检测后误导策略，即对检测到的恶意输入返回安全但具有误导性的回复，以干扰攻击者的自动评估器。其概念验证方案“情境误导渐进交互”（CMPE）通过替换可预测拒绝语句为误导性回复，使攻击成功率上限降低两个数量级，并在端到端测试中几乎完全阻断验证攻击。
Relighting as a Probe of Visual Priors via Augmented Latent Intrinsics
Authors: Xiaoyan Xing, Xiao Zhang, Sezer Karaoglu, Theo Gevers, Anand Bhattad
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-02-01T18:51:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:49:52+00:00
Comments: Camera-ready version for ICML 2026. Project page: https://augmented-latent-intrinsics.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Image-to-image relighting requires representations that separate illumination from scene properties while preserving dense geometry, material, and photometric cues. We use this task as a probe of visual priors: unlike recognition tasks that reward invariance, relighting tests whether visual features retain the information needed for light transfer. Through a controlled generative relighting framework, we find that strong semantic encoders can degrade relighting quality, exposing a semantic--photometric trade-off between abstraction and physical fidelity. We introduce Augmented Latent Intrinsics (ALI), which balances this trade-off by fusing dense, pixel-aligned visual features into a latent-intrinsic relighting model and refining it with self-supervision on unlabeled real image pairs. ALI improves relighting quality, especially on glossy, metallic, and transparent materials, and demonstrates that generative relighting is an effective tool for quantifying what visual encoders encode about the physical world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过增强潜在内在属性进行重照明以探测视觉先验
图像到图像的重照明需要能够分离光照与场景属性，同时保留密集几何、材质和光度线索的表示。我们将此任务作为视觉先验的探测工具：与奖励不变性的识别任务不同，重照明检验视觉特征是否保留了用于光传输所需的信息。通过受控的生成式重照明框架，我们发现强语义编码器可能降低重照明质量，揭示了抽象性与物理保真度之间的语义-光度权衡。我们提出了增强潜在内在属性（ALI），通过将密集、像素对齐的视觉特征融合进潜在内在重照明模型，并利用无标签真实图像对的自监督进行优化，以平衡该权衡。ALI显著提升了重照明质量，尤其在高光、金属和透明材质上表现突出，并证明生成式重照明是量化视觉编码器对物理世界编码内容的有效工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand what visual encoders preserve about physical lighting, this work uses image-to-image relighting as a probe to test whether visual features retain photometric information necessary for light transfer. The authors develop a controlled generative relighting framework to reveal that strong semantic encoders often degrade relighting quality, indicating a trade-off between semantic abstraction and physical fidelity. They propose Augmented Latent Intrinsics (ALI), which integrates dense pixel-aligned visual features into a latent-intrinsic model and refines it via self-supervised learning on unlabeled real image pairs, resulting in significantly improved relighting performance—especially for challenging materials like glossy, metallic, and transparent surfaces—demonstrating that generative relighting can quantify what visual representations encode about the physical world.
该研究旨在探究视觉编码器对物理光照信息的编码能力，以图像到图像的重光照任务为探针，检验视觉特征是否保留了用于光传输所需的光度信息。作者构建了可控的生成式重光照框架，发现强语义编码器常导致重光照质量下降，揭示了语义抽象与物理保真度之间的权衡。为此，他们提出增强潜在内在特征（ALI），将密集像素对齐的视觉特征融入潜在内在模型，并通过无标签真实图像对的自监督学习进行优化。实验表明，ALI显著提升了重光照质量，尤其在高反光、金属和透明材质上表现突出，证明生成式重光照是量化视觉编码器对物理世界表征能力的有效工具。
Fisher-Geometric Sharpness and the Implicit Bias of SGD toward Flat Minima
Authors: Md Sakir Ahmed, Kumaresh Sarmah, Hemen Dutta
First: 2026-06-18T16:48:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:48:18+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 5 figures, preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A widely held intuition in deep learning is that stochastic gradient descent (SGD) implicitly favors flat minima and that flat minima generalize better, but standard Euclidean measures of flatness such as the trace or maximum eigenvalue of the loss Hessian are not invariant under reparametrizations that preserve the network function, which undermines the theoretical foundations of this narrative. In this study we resolve this issue by grounding flatness in the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold induced by the Fisher Information Matrix (FIM). We define Riemannian sharpness mathematically and prove that it is invariant under smooth, function-preserving reparametrizations, which directly addresses the critique of Dinh et al. in the paper ``Sharp minima can generalize for deep nets''.We note that this invariance is a property of the true FIM; the diagonal empirical estimator used in practice (and in all experiments below) inherits invariance only approximately, and exact invariance under arbitrary reparametrizations would require structured estimators such as K-FAC. We formalize the gradient noise of mini-batch SGD as having a covariance structure proportional to the FIM, derive the stationary distribution of the resulting stochastic differential equation, and then show that the probability mass is exponentially concentrated at Riemannian-flat minima. A PAC-Bayes generalization bound controlled explicitly by SR formally links this geometric bias to test performance. Our experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 confirm that SR reliably tracks generalization in ways that Euclidean sharpness does not, and that its scaling with $η/B$ matches the theoretical predictions. Together these results provide a rigorous, reparametrization-invariant account of why flat minima generalize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：费舍尔几何尖锐度与SGD对平坦极小值的隐式偏置
深度学习中普遍认为随机梯度下降（SGD）会隐式偏好平坦极小值，且平坦极小值泛化能力更强。但标准欧几里得平坦度度量（如损失Hessian的迹或最大特征值）在保持网络函数不变的重参数化下不具不变性，这削弱了该叙事的理论基础。本研究通过将平坦度根植于由费舍尔信息矩阵（FIM）诱导的统计流形的黎曼几何中解决此问题。我们数学上定义了黎曼尖锐度，并证明其在光滑、函数保持的重参数化下具有不变性，直接回应了Dinh等人在论文《深度网络中尖锐极小值可泛化》中的批评。我们指出，该不变性是针对真实FIM的性质；实践中使用的对角经验估计器（及以下所有实验）仅近似继承该不变性，而任意重参数化下的精确不变性需依赖结构化估计器（如K-FAC）。我们形式化了小批量SGD梯度噪声的协方差结构与FIM成比例，推导出相应随机微分方程的稳态分布，并证明概率质量在黎曼平坦极小值处呈指数集中。一个由SR显式控制的PAC-Bayes泛化界，将此几何偏置与测试性能明确关联。我们在MNIST和CIFAR-10上的实验表明，SR能可靠追踪泛化能力，而欧几里得尖锐度则不能，且其随η/B的缩放符合理论预测。这些结果共同提供了一个严谨、重参数化不变的解释，说明为何平坦极小值能泛化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to resolve inconsistencies in the theoretical understanding of why flat minima generalize better in deep learning, this study introduces Riemannian sharpness as a reparametrization-invariant measure of flatness grounded in the Fisher Information Matrix. The method defines sharpness via the Riemannian geometry of the statistical manifold, proves its invariance under function-preserving reparametrizations, and links SGD’s implicit bias to the concentration of its stationary distribution at Riemannian-flat minima. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that Riemannian sharpness (SR) consistently correlates with generalization performance, outperforming Euclidean measures, and its scaling with learning rate over batch size matches theoretical predictions.
为解决深度学习中平坦最小值泛化能力更强这一观点在理论上的不一致性，本研究基于Fisher信息矩阵定义了黎曼平坦度，使其在函数保持不变的重参数化下保持不变。通过将小批量SGD的梯度噪声建模为与FIM成比例的协方差，推导出相应随机微分方程的稳态分布，并证明概率质量呈指数集中于黎曼平坦最小值。理论结果通过PAC-Bayes界将该几何偏置与泛化能力显式关联，实验在MNIST和CIFAR-10上验证黎曼平坦度（SR）比欧氏平坦度更可靠地预测测试性能，且其随η/B变化的规律符合理论预测。
Agentic Symbolic Search: Characterizing PDEs Beyond Hand-crafted Expressions, Meshes, and Neural Networks
Authors: Zongmin Yu, Liu Yang
First: 2026-06-18T16:46:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:46:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mathematicians understand a PDE solution through mathematical structures rather than tables of computed values. Historically, this has been the product of mathematical analysis, carried out by hand for each problem individually. Neither numerical simulation nor neural networks produce those structures directly. We propose Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework in which an agent translates PDE theory, public problem constraints, and accumulated search experience into testable differentiable symbolic programs. The mathematical forms are refined under evolutionary search, while their continuous parameters are fit by gradient-based optimization. This makes the search an automated form of inductive-bias injection rather than blind symbolic regression. For problems with known analytical forms, ASYS recovers these forms naturally; for other problems, ASYS constructs analytical approximations which can guide mathematicians toward further analysis. In our experiments, across five problems spanning bounded dynamics, finite-time blow-up, and free-boundary focusing, ASYS produces interpretable representations, including a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn 2D dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel chemotactic blow-up, in settings where no closed-form description was previously available. ASYS shows the possibility of a new paradigm for characterizing PDE solutions, beyond handcrafted analytical solutions, mesh-based numerical solutions, and neural network approximations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理符号搜索：超越手工表达式、网格和神经网络的偏微分方程刻画
数学家通过数学结构而非计算值表理解偏微分方程（PDE）解。历史上，这依赖于手工进行的数学分析，针对每个问题单独完成。数值模拟和神经网络均无法直接生成这些结构。我们提出代理符号搜索（ASYS），一种基于先验引导的框架，其中代理将PDE理论、公开问题约束及累积搜索经验转化为可测试的可微分符号程序。数学形式在进化搜索中优化，其连续参数通过梯度优化拟合。这使搜索成为一种自动化的归纳偏置注入，而非盲目符号回归。对于已知解析形式的问题，ASYS自然恢复这些形式；对于其他问题，ASYS构建解析近似，引导数学家进一步分析。实验表明，在涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破和自由边界聚焦的五个问题中，ASYS生成可解释表示，包括Allen-Cahn二维动力学的几何界面公式及Keller-Segel趋化爆破的九参数收缩律，此前无闭式描述。ASYS展示了超越手工解析解、网格数值解和神经网络近似的PDE解刻画新范式的可能性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to recover interpretable mathematical structures for PDE solutions beyond numerical or neural approaches, the paper introduces Agentic Symbolic Search (ASYS), a prior-guided framework that uses an agent to translate PDE theory and problem constraints into differentiable symbolic programs. These programs are refined via evolutionary search and optimized with gradient-based methods, enabling automated inductive bias injection. Experiments across five diverse PDE problems demonstrate ASYS’s ability to recover known analytical forms or construct interpretable approximations, such as a geometric interface formula for Allen-Cahn dynamics and a nine-parameter contraction law for Keller-Segel blow-up, where no closed-form solution existed before.
为突破传统数值模拟和神经网络无法直接生成数学结构的局限，本文提出Agentic Symbolic Search（ASYS）框架，通过代理将偏微分方程理论、问题约束与历史搜索经验转化为可微分的符号程序。程序结构通过进化搜索优化，参数则通过梯度下降拟合，实现自动化先验注入。在五个涵盖有界动力学、有限时间爆破及自由边界聚焦的PDE问题实验中，ASYS成功生成可解释的解析形式，如Allen-Cahn方程的几何界面公式和Keller-Segel爆破的九参数收缩律，即使此前无闭式解存在，展示了超越手工解析、网格数值与神经网络的新范式可能性。
Weighted Bayesian Conformal Prediction
Authors: Xiayin Lou, Peng Luo
First: 2026-04-07T21:07:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:46:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conformal prediction provides distribution-free prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage guarantees, and recent work by Snell \& Griffiths reframes it as Bayesian Quadrature (BQ-CP), yielding powerful data-conditional guarantees via Dirichlet posteriors over thresholds. However, BQ-CP fundamentally requires the i.i.d. assumption. Meanwhile, weighted conformal prediction handles distribution shift via importance weights but remains frequentist, producing only point-estimate thresholds. We propose \textbf{Weighted Bayesian Conformal Prediction (WBCP)}, which generalizes BQ-CP to arbitrary importance-weighted settings by replacing the uniform Dirichlet $\Dir(1,\ldots,1)$ with a weighted Dirichlet $\Dir(\neff \cdot \tilde{w}_1, \ldots, \neff \cdot \tilde{w}_n)$, where $\neff$ is Kish's effective sample size. We prove four theoretical results: (1)~$\neff$ is the unique concentration parameter matching frequentist and Bayesian variances; (2)~posterior standard deviation decays as $O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$; (3)~BQ-CP's stochastic dominance guarantee extends to per-weight-profile data-conditional guarantees; (4)~the HPD threshold provides $O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$ improvement in conditional coverage. We instantiate WBCP for spatial prediction as \emph{Geographical BQ-CP}, where kernel-based spatial weights yield per-location posteriors with interpretable diagnostics. Experiments on synthetic and real-world spatial datasets demonstrate that WBCP maintains coverage guarantees while providing substantially richer uncertainty information.
中文标题/摘要
标题：加权贝叶斯符合预测
符合预测提供无需分布假设的预测区间，并具有有限样本覆盖保证；Snell与Griffiths近期将其重新表述为贝叶斯二次积分（BQ-CP），通过Dirichlet阈值后验获得强大的数据条件保证。然而，BQ-CP本质上要求独立同分布假设。同时，加权符合预测通过重要性权重处理分布偏移，但仍是频率学派方法，仅产生点估计阈值。我们提出\textbf{加权贝叶斯符合预测（WBCP）}，通过将均匀Dirichlet $\Dir(1,\ldots,1)$ 替换为加权Dirichlet $\Dir(\neff \cdot \tilde{w}_1, \ldots, \neff \cdot \tilde{w}_n)$（其中$\neff$为Kish有效样本量），将BQ-CP推广至任意重要性加权场景。我们证明四个理论结果：(1) $\neff$是唯一匹配频率学派与贝叶斯方差的集中参数；(2) 后验标准差按$O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$衰减；(3) BQ-CP的随机优势保证扩展为每权重配置的数据条件保证；(4) HPD阈值在条件覆盖上提供$O(1/\sqrt{\neff})$的改进。我们将WBCP应用于空间预测，称为\emph{地理BQ-CP}，其中基于核的空间权重生成每个位置的后验分布并提供可解释的诊断。在合成与真实空间数据集上的实验表明，WBCP在保持覆盖保证的同时，提供了更丰富的不确定性信息。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extend Bayesian conformal prediction to non-i.i.d. settings while preserving coverage guarantees, the paper introduces Weighted Bayesian Conformal Prediction (WBCP), which adapts Bayesian Quadrature-based conformal prediction by replacing the uniform Dirichlet prior with a weighted Dirichlet based on Kish’s effective sample size and importance weights. Theoretical results show that this weighting scheme uniquely matches frequentist and Bayesian variances, yields posterior standard deviation decay proportional to 1/sqrt(neff), extends stochastic dominance guarantees to per-weight-profile conditional settings, and improves conditional coverage via HPD thresholds. Empirical experiments on synthetic and real-world spatial datasets confirm that WBCP maintains finite-sample coverage while delivering richer uncertainty quantification compared to standard methods.
为在非独立同分布数据下保留覆盖保证，本文提出加权贝叶斯置信预测（WBCP），通过将均匀Dirichlet先验替换为按Kish有效样本量缩放的加权Dirichlet先验，使贝叶斯四次方置信预测适用于重要性加权场景。理论证明表明，有效样本量能唯一匹配频数与贝叶斯方差，后验标准差随有效样本量的平方根衰减。在空间数据实验中，WBCP保持覆盖性的同时，通过每个位置的后验诊断提供更丰富的不确定性信息，显著优于传统方法的条件覆盖精度。
PTLD: Sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Rosy Chen, Mustafa Mukadam, Michael Kaess, Tingfan Wu, Francois R Hogan, Jitendra Malik, Akash Sharma
First: 2026-03-04T19:17:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:44:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Tactile dexterous manipulation is essential to automating complex household tasks, yet learning effective control policies remains a challenge. While recent work has relied on imitation learning, obtaining high quality demonstrations for multi-fingered hands via robot teleoperation or kinesthetic teaching is prohibitive. Alternatively, with reinforcement we can learn skills in simulation, but fast and realistic simulation of tactile observations is challenging. To bridge this gap, we introduce PTLD: sim-to-real Privileged Tactile Latent Distillation, a novel approach to learning tactile manipulation skills without requiring tactile simulation. Instead of simulating tactile sensors or relying purely on proprioceptive policies to transfer zero-shot sim-to-real, our key idea is to leverage privileged sensors in the real world to collect real-world tactile policy data. This data is then used to distill a robust state estimator that operates on tactile input. We demonstrate from our experiments that PTLD can be used to improve proprioceptive manipulation policies trained in simulation significantly by incorporating tactile sensing. On the benchmark in-hand rotation task, PTLD achieves a 182% improvement over a proprioception only policy. We also show that PTLD enables learning the challenging task of tactile in-hand reorientation where we see a 57% improvement in the number of goals reached over using proprioception alone. Website: https://akashsharma02.github.io/ptld-website/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：PTLD：从仿真到现实的特权触觉潜在蒸馏，用于灵巧操作
触觉灵巧操作对自动化复杂家庭任务至关重要，但学习有效的控制策略仍具挑战。尽管近期研究依赖模仿学习，但通过机器人遥操作或力觉教学获取多指手的高质量演示成本高昂。替代方案是使用强化学习在仿真环境中学习技能，但触觉观测的快速且真实模拟仍具挑战。为弥合此差距，我们提出PTLD：从仿真到现实的特权触觉潜在蒸馏，一种无需触觉仿真即可学习触觉操作技能的新方法。我们不模拟触觉传感器，也不仅依赖本体感觉策略实现零样本仿真到现实迁移，而是利用现实世界中的特权传感器收集真实触觉策略数据，进而蒸馏出一个基于触觉输入的鲁棒状态估计器。实验表明，PTLD能显著提升在仿真中训练的本体感觉操作策略，通过引入触觉感知。在手内旋转基准任务中，PTLD相较仅本体感觉策略提升182%。我们还展示PTLD可实现具有挑战性的触觉手内重定向任务，相较仅本体感觉策略，目标达成率提升57%。网站：https://akashsharma02.github.io/ptld-website/
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of learning tactile dexterous manipulation without costly real-world demonstrations or realistic tactile simulation, PTLD introduces a sim-to-real privileged tactile latent distillation method. It leverages real-world privileged tactile sensors to collect data for distilling a robust state estimator that operates on tactile input, without requiring tactile simulation during training. Experiments show PTLD significantly enhances proprioception-only policies: achieving 182% improvement on in-hand rotation and 57% increase in goal success rate on tactile in-hand reorientation.
为解决无需昂贵真实演示或真实触觉模拟即可学习触觉灵巧操作的问题，PTLD提出一种基于真实世界特权触觉传感器数据蒸馏鲁棒状态估计器的方法。实验表明，该方法显著提升仅依赖本体感觉的策略：在手内旋转任务中提升182%，在触觉手内重定向任务中目标达成率提升57%。
Data Bias Mitigation under Coverage Constraints & The Price of Fairness
Authors: Bruno Scarone, Alfredo Viola, Renée J. Miller
First: 2026-06-18T16:43:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:43:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted to FAccT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Machine learning models have been shown to exhibit discriminatory outcomes or degraded performance for individuals at the intersection of multiple sensitive attributes, such as race and gender. This stems in part from two interrelated challenges: the lack of principled measures for quantifying bias (potentially intersectional), and insufficient representation of intersectional subgroups in training data. We extend a recent bias mitigation framework to incorporate coverage constraints that enforce sufficient representation across groups, including intersectional subgroups. Since achieving exactly zero bias for all groups may not be data efficient (meaning it may require large amounts of data), our solution trades small approximation errors in bias for greater data efficiency while satisfying coverage constraints. We also formulate bias mitigation as an integer linear program that optimizes over all mitigation strategies, and characterize the price of fairness, the minimum data modification cost, as a function of fairness tolerance. This is essential both for legal compliance, where regulations may mandate specific fairness thresholds, and for data governance, enabling practitioners to make informed trade-offs between bias reduction and data modification (particularly, data purchasing) costs. We evaluate our techniques on publicly available datasets, demonstrating that bias mitigation via our framework preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers, and that coverage constraints, while motivated by statistical considerations, are essential for preserving downstream ML performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：覆盖约束下的数据偏见缓解与公平代价
机器学习模型已被证明在多个敏感属性（如种族和性别）交集的个体上会产生歧视性结果或性能下降。这源于两个相互关联的挑战：缺乏量化偏见（可能为交叉偏见）的原理性度量方法，以及训练数据中交叉子群体代表性不足。我们扩展了最近的偏见缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保各群体（包括交叉子群体）获得充分代表。由于对所有群体实现零偏见可能不具数据效率（可能需要大量数据），我们的解决方案在满足覆盖约束的前提下，以微小的偏见近似误差换取更高的数据效率。我们还将偏见缓解形式化为整数线性规划问题，优化所有缓解策略，并将公平代价（即最小数据修改成本）作为公平容忍度的函数进行刻画。这在法律合规方面至关重要（法规可能要求特定公平阈值），也对数据治理至关重要，使从业者能够在偏见减少与数据修改（特别是数据采购）成本之间做出明智权衡。我们在公开数据集上评估了我们的方法，证明通过本框架进行偏见缓解可保持多种分类器的预测准确性，且覆盖约束虽源于统计考量，却是维持下游机器学习性能所必需的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to address bias in machine learning models that disproportionately affect individuals with intersecting sensitive attributes, this work extends a bias mitigation framework to enforce coverage constraints that ensure sufficient representation of intersectional subgroups. It formulates the problem as an integer linear program to optimize over mitigation strategies, trading small bias approximation errors for improved data efficiency while meeting coverage requirements. Experimental results on public datasets show that this approach preserves predictive accuracy across multiple classifiers and demonstrates that coverage constraints are critical for maintaining downstream model performance, even when fairness is not fully achieved.
为解决机器学习模型在多重敏感属性交叉群体中表现偏颇的问题，该研究扩展了偏见缓解框架，引入覆盖约束以确保交集子群体的充分代表性。方法将偏见缓解建模为整数线性规划问题，在满足覆盖约束的同时，通过容忍微小的偏见近似误差换取更高的数据效率。实验表明，该框架在多个分类器上保持了预测精度，且覆盖约束对维持下游模型性能至关重要，即使无法完全实现公平性。
VideoSketcher: Sequential Sketch Generation Using Video Model Priors
Authors: Hui Ren, Yuval Alaluf, Omer Bar Tal, Alexander Schwing, Antonio Torralba, Yael Vinker
First: 2026-02-17T18:55:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:41:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sketching is inherently sequential: strokes are drawn progressively to explore and refine ideas. Yet most generative approaches treat sketches as static images, ignoring the temporal process underlying creative exploration. Modeling this sequential structure remains challenging: prior methods either rely on large-scale human-drawn datasets with limited diversity, or use large language models (LLMs) to produce drawing instructions, often at the cost of visual fidelity. We present VideoSketcher, a method for generating high-quality sketching processes by adapting pretrained text-to-video diffusion models to the sparse, continuous nature of sketch formation. Our key insight is that LLMs and video diffusion models offer complementary strengths: LLMs act as semantic planners that decompose concepts into step-by-step instructions, while video diffusion models serve as powerful "renderers" that translate them into temporally coherent sketch sequences. We introduce a two-stage fine-tuning strategy that decouples temporal structure from visual appearance: stroke ordering is learned from synthetic shape compositions, while style is distilled from as few as seven hand-drawn examples. Despite minimal supervision, our method can generate diverse, high-quality sequential sketches that faithfully follow specified drawing orders. Our framework naturally extends to brush style control and autoregressive generation, supporting artistic applications.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VideoSketcher：利用视频模型先验进行序列草图生成
草图生成本质上是序列性的：线条逐步绘制以探索和精炼创意。然而，大多数生成方法将草图视为静态图像，忽略了创意探索背后的时序过程。建模这种序列结构仍具挑战性：现有方法要么依赖多样性有限的大规模人工草图数据集，要么使用大语言模型（LLM）生成绘图指令，常以牺牲视觉保真度为代价。我们提出VideoSketcher，通过将预训练的文本到视频扩散模型适配于草图形成的稀疏连续特性，生成高质量的草图生成过程。我们的核心见解是：LLM与视频扩散模型具有互补优势——LLM作为语义规划器，将概念分解为分步指令；视频扩散模型则作为强大的“渲染器”，将其转化为时序连贯的草图序列。我们引入两阶段微调策略，将时序结构与视觉外观解耦：笔画顺序从合成形状组合中学习，风格则仅需七个手绘示例即可蒸馏。尽管监督信号极少，我们的方法仍能生成多样且高质量的序列草图，忠实遵循指定的绘图顺序。该框架自然支持笔触风格控制与自回归生成，适用于艺术创作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the sequential nature of sketching, which most generative models overlook, VideoSketcher leverages pretrained text-to-video diffusion models adapted to the sparse, continuous strokes of sketching. It combines LLMs for semantic step-by-step planning with video diffusion for temporally coherent rendering, using a two-stage fine-tuning approach: stroke order is learned from synthetic shapes, and style is distilled from just seven hand-drawn examples. Experimental results show that despite minimal supervision, the method generates diverse, high-fidelity sequential sketches that accurately follow user-specified drawing orders, and supports brush style control and autoregressive generation.
鉴于传统生成模型忽视了素描创作的顺序性，VideoSketcher通过调整预训练的文本到视频扩散模型，使其适配素描中稀疏且连续的笔触过程。该方法结合大语言模型进行语义分步规划与视频扩散模型进行时间一致的渲染，采用两阶段微调策略：笔触顺序从合成形状中学习，风格仅需七张手绘样本即可蒸馏。实验表明，该方法能在极低监督下生成多样且高保真的顺序素描，忠实遵循指定绘制顺序，并支持笔触风格控制与自回归生成。
Statistical Foundations of LLM-based A/B Testing: A Surrogacy Framework for Human Causal Inference
Authors: Joel Persson, Mårten Schultzberg, Sebastian Ankargren
First: 2026-06-15T18:06:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:41:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Organizations and researchers show increasing interest in using large language models (LLMs) in place of human participants in A/B tests, in the hope of experimenting faster and at lower cost. We study when a treatment effect estimated on LLM outcomes can recover the effect that would have been measured on the human population of interest. Distributional equivalence between LLM and human outcomes would make any standard estimator valid but is unrealistic. We therefore develop a statistical framework that adapts surrogate endpoint theory to LLMs, showing that calibrating LLM outcomes to human outcomes identifies the average treatment effect under surrogacy and comparability conditions that are jointly weaker than distributional equivalence. We present a falsification test for surrogacy and a bound on the worst-case bias from limited overlap between the LLM and human samples. We further show that the stochasticity inherent to LLMs can weaken surrogacy for identification while also introducing bias and variance during estimation, but that using an average over multiple LLM draws per unit as the surrogate mitigates these issues. Simulations validate the results, and an empirical application to A/B tests on Upworthy headlines shows that raw LLM predictions recover only 39\% of the human treatment effect while nonparametric calibration closes the gap. A central takeaway is that A/B testing on LLMs yields correct results only by assumption, whereas A/B testing on humans is correct by design, and that the required assumptions are hardest to justify precisely where A/B testing on LLMs promises the greatest benefit. We discuss the role of LLM choice, prompting, and temperature as design variables, the compounded challenge posed by long-term outcomes, and how to size human pilot studies for validation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于大语言模型的A/B测试的统计基础：用于人类因果推断的代理框架
组织和研究人员日益关注使用大语言模型（LLMs）替代人类参与者进行A/B测试，以期更快、更低成本地实验。我们研究在何种条件下，基于LLM结果估计的处理效应能够恢复对目标人类群体本应测量到的效果。LLM与人类结果的分布等价性将使任何标准估计量有效，但这是不现实的。因此，我们开发了一个统计框架，将代理终点理论适配于LLMs，表明校准LLM结果至人类结果可在代理性和可比性条件下识别平均处理效应，且这些条件联合弱于分布等价性。我们提出了代理性的反事实检验及在LLM与人类样本重叠有限时最坏情况偏差的界。我们进一步表明，LLM固有的随机性会削弱代理性以实现识别，同时在估计过程中引入偏差和方差，但使用每个单位多个LLM抽样结果的平均值作为代理可缓解这些问题。模拟验证了结果，对Upworthy标题A/B测试的实证应用显示，原始LLM预测仅恢复人类处理效应的39%，而非参数校准则缩小了这一差距。核心结论是：仅在假设条件下，基于LLM的A/B测试才能得出正确结果；而基于人类的A/B测试则由设计保证正确，且所需假设在LLM A/B测试最能带来效益之处最难被充分证明。我们讨论了LLM选择、提示和温度作为设计变量的作用，长期结果带来的叠加挑战，以及如何为验证设计人类试点研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the desire to accelerate and reduce the cost of A/B testing using large language models (LLMs) instead of human participants, this paper develops a statistical framework based on surrogate endpoint theory to assess when LLM-based treatment effect estimates can validly approximate human outcomes. The method calibrates LLM predictions to human data under surrogacy and comparability conditions that are weaker than full distributional equivalence, and includes a falsification test and bias bounds for limited sample overlap. Experiments show that raw LLM predictions recover only 39% of the true human treatment effect in an Upworthy headline A/B test, but nonparametric calibration substantially improves accuracy. The study concludes that LLM-based A/B testing is only valid under unverifiable assumptions, whereas human-based testing is inherently valid, and that the most promising applications of LLMs in A/B testing are also the most challenging to validate.
鉴于使用大语言模型（LLM）替代人类参与者进行A/B测试以提升效率和降低成本的趋势，本文研究在何种条件下，基于LLM的处理效应估计可近似真实人类群体的效应。文章提出将代理终点理论适配于LLM，证明通过校准LLM结果至人类结果，可在弱于分布等价的条件下识别平均处理效应，并引入代理有效性检验及样本重叠不足导致的最坏偏差界。仿真与Upworthy标题实验表明，未经校准的LLM预测仅捕捉人类效应的39%，而非参数校准显著缩小差距。结论指出，LLM A/B测试仅在假设成立时有效，而人类测试则天然有效，且LLM在人类测试最困难或成本最高的场景下最具价值。
Context-Aware Hierarchical Bayesian Modeling of IVF Laboratory Environmental Conditions
Authors: Zahra Asghari Varzaneh, Reza Khoshkangini, Pia Saldeen, Lars Johansson, Thomas Ebner
First: 2026-06-18T16:40:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:40:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
IVF pregnancy rates are routinely modeled using patient-level variables, while high-resolution laboratory environmental data remain underutilized. We show that this is a missed opportunity. Rather than relying on raw sensor averages, we engineer 55 context-aware temporal features, including rolling thermal stability, simultaneous temperature-humidity adherence, peak stress duration, and post-stress recovery speed, that capture the dynamics of incubator microenvironments. On 61 weeks of data from an Asian IVF clinic, these features reduce cross-validated prediction error to 1.27%, compared to 3-5% for raw averages. We then train a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across an Asian and a Northern European clinic via partial pooling, while preserving site-specific baselines. On held-out data from the Northern European clinic, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and a 64% error reduction for the 35-39 age group over a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically meaningful, transferable signal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于上下文感知的分层贝叶斯模型对体外受精实验室环境条件的建模
体外受精（IVF）妊娠率通常仅使用患者层面变量建模，而高分辨率实验室环境数据则被低估利用。我们表明，这是一次被错过的机遇。我们设计了55个上下文感知的时间特征，包括滚动热稳定性、温湿度同步达标、峰值压力持续时间和压力后恢复速度，以捕捉培养箱微环境的动态变化。在一家亚洲IVF诊所61周的数据上，这些特征将交叉验证预测误差降至1.27%，而原始平均值则为3-5%。随后，我们训练了一个分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型，通过部分池化在亚洲和北欧两家诊所间共享环境效应，同时保留各站点的特定基线。在北欧诊所的留出数据上，该模型在35-39岁年龄组中达到R²=0.86，比朴素基线误差降低64%，证明结构化环境监测包含具有临床意义且可迁移的信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of high-resolution environmental data in IVF success prediction, the study engineers 55 context-aware temporal features from incubator sensor data to capture microenvironment dynamics. These features, including thermal stability and stress recovery metrics, are fed into a hierarchical Bayesian Beta regression model that shares environmental effects across clinics via partial pooling. On held-out Northern European data, the model achieves R2 = 0.86 and reduces prediction error by 64% for women aged 35-39 compared to a naive baseline, demonstrating that structured environmental monitoring contains clinically transferable signals.
鉴于IVF实验室高分辨率环境数据在妊娠率预测中的未充分利用，该研究从传感器数据中构建了55个上下文感知的时间特征，以捕捉培养箱微环境动态，如热稳定性与压力恢复速度。这些特征输入到一个分层贝叶斯Beta回归模型中，通过部分池化在亚洲与北欧两家诊所间共享环境效应，同时保留各机构基线。在北欧诊所的留出数据上，模型达到R2=0.86，35-39岁组预测误差较朴素基线降低64%，表明结构化环境监测蕴含可跨机构迁移的临床信号。
Slow Brain, Fast Planner: Latency-Resilient VLM-Augmented Urban Navigation
Authors: Zhenghao "Mark'' Peng, Honglin He, Quanyi Li, Yukai Ma, Bolei Zhou
First: 2026-06-18T16:40:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:40:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Learning-based planners for sidewalk navigation can generate diverse candidate trajectories in real time, yet their scoring functions often fail to select the best trajectory in challenging situations, outputting trajectories that make the mobile robot drive onto grass, toward pedestrians, or in the wrong direction, even when better candidates exist in the same set. We call this the trajectory scoring gap: in real-world sidewalk navigation, the gap between an anchor-based planner's top choice and the best possible candidate is substantial, likely due to limited high-level scene understanding capability of the planner. Rather than replacing the planner with an end-to-end Vision-Language-Action model, we propose a VLM-Planner interface that uses a VLM to select a candidate index from the planner's proposal set and then fuse it with the planner's initial output. However, VLMs take 1--3s per query and so cannot directly drive a 5--20Hz control loop. We contribute a training-free, latency-resilient trajectory-level fusion layer that turns a stale VLM selection into real-time planner scoring via geometric similarity with exponential decay. On $\sim$2,000 challenging real-world scenarios (e.g., junctions, pedestrian encounters), VLM selection achieves 30% ADE reduction versus the planner's best selection, while the planner remains competitive in routine situations. In simulation, Score Fusion maintains >80% success rate with delays up to 5s. We demonstrate the full system on a mobile robot navigating challenging campus sidewalks with varied network latency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：慢脑快规划：低延迟视觉语言模型增强的城市导航
基于学习的步行道导航规划器可实时生成多样化的候选轨迹，但其评分函数常在复杂场景下无法选出最优轨迹，导致移动机器人驶入草坪、朝向行人或错误方向行驶，即使候选集中存在更优方案。我们称之为轨迹评分差距：在真实步行道导航中，基于锚点的规划器首选轨迹与最优候选轨迹之间差距显著，可能源于规划器对高层场景理解能力有限。我们未采用端到端视觉-语言-动作模型替代规划器，而是提出VLM-规划器接口：利用视觉语言模型从规划器提案集中选择候选索引，并将其与规划器初始输出融合。然而，视觉语言模型每查询耗时1–3秒，无法直接驱动5–20Hz控制循环。我们贡献了一种无需训练、低延迟的轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似性与指数衰减，将过时的VLM选择转化为实时规划器评分。在约2000个真实复杂场景（如交叉口、行人相遇）中，VLM选择相较规划器最佳选择降低30% ADE，规划器在常规场景中仍具竞争力。仿真中，评分融合在延迟达5秒时仍保持>80%成功率。我们在网络延迟变化的校园步行道上，演示了完整系统在移动机器人上的运行。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trajectory scoring gap in real-time sidewalk navigation—where planners often fail to select optimal paths despite generating diverse candidates—the paper proposes a VLM-Planner interface that uses a Vision-Language Model to select the best candidate trajectory from a planner’s proposal set and fuses it with the planner’s output. To handle VLMs’ high latency (1–3s), the authors introduce a training-free, latency-resilient fusion layer that scores trajectories based on geometric similarity with exponential decay, enabling real-time control. Experiments on ~2,000 real-world scenarios show the VLM selection reduces Average Displacement Error by 30% compared to the planner’s top choice, while maintaining performance in routine conditions. In simulation, the fusion layer sustains over 80% success rate even with up to 5s delays, validated on a mobile robot navigating complex campus sidewalks.
为解决实时人行道导航中规划器选轨能力不足的问题——即规划器虽能生成多样轨迹却常选错最优解——本文提出一种VLM-规划器接口，利用视觉语言模型从规划器候选集中选出最佳轨迹并融合其初始输出。为应对VLM高延迟（1–3秒），作者设计了一种无需训练、抗延迟的轨迹级融合层，通过几何相似度加指数衰减实现实时评分。在约2000个真实复杂场景（如路口、行人交汇）中，VLM选轨使平均位移误差降低30%，且在常规场景下规划器表现仍具竞争力。仿真测试显示，该融合层在5秒延迟下仍保持80%以上成功率，已在移动机器人于校园复杂人行道上进行实际验证。
Repurposing a Speech Classifier for Guided Diffusion-Based Speech Generation
Authors: Rostislav Makarov, Timo Gerkmann
First: 2026-06-18T16:40:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:40:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in the Proceedings of Interspeech 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Classifier guidance is a way to control diffusion generation by using a noise-conditioned classifier to steer the sampling process toward a target class. One drawback of classifier guidance is that it requires two separately trained models: a classifier and a diffusion model. We therefore study a more compact alternative in which a conventionally trained speech classifier is repurposed as the backbone for diffusion generation. Starting from a frozen noise-conditioned classifier in log-Mel space, we attach a lightweight subnetwork that reuses intermediate classifier representations and train only this subnetwork under a Denoising Score Matching objective. Our work shows that a pretrained classifier can be repurposed for conditional generation, providing an appealing bridge between discriminative modeling and conditional speech synthesis resulting in high speech quality within a single-backbone model, with reduced memory footprint and computational cost.
中文标题/摘要
标题：将语音分类器重新用于基于引导扩散的语音生成
分类器引导是一种通过噪声条件分类器控制扩散生成的方法，以引导采样过程朝向目标类别。其缺点是需要两个分别训练的模型：分类器和扩散模型。因此，我们研究了一种更紧凑的替代方案，即重新利用传统训练的语音分类器作为扩散生成的骨干网络。从冻结的对数梅尔空间噪声条件分类器出发，我们附加一个轻量级子网络，复用中间分类器表示，并仅在去噪得分匹配目标下训练该子网络。我们的工作表明，预训练分类器可被重新用于条件生成，为判别建模与条件语音合成之间架起了一座有吸引力的桥梁，从而在单一骨干模型中实现高质量语音生成，同时降低内存占用和计算成本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational overhead of training separate classifier and diffusion models in classifier guidance, this work repurposes a pre-trained speech classifier as the backbone for guided diffusion-based speech generation. The method freezes the classifier’s weights and appends a lightweight subnetwork that leverages intermediate classifier representations, training only this subnetwork under a Denoising Score Matching objective. Experimental results show that this single-backbone architecture achieves high-quality speech synthesis while reducing memory and computational costs compared to traditional two-model setups.
为降低分类器引导生成中训练独立模型的计算开销，该研究将预训练的语音分类器重新用于基于扩散的语音生成。方法冻结分类器权重，附加轻量子网络复用中间表示，并仅训练该子网络以满足去噪得分匹配目标。实验表明，该单骨干架构在保持高质量语音合成的同时，显著降低了内存与计算开销，较传统双模型方案更具效率。
VEPHand: View-Efficient Photometric Hand Performance Capture at Scale
Authors: Zhengyang Shen, Kai-Hung Chang, Erroll Wood, Deying Kong, Bo Peng, Timo Bolkart, Jinlong Yang, Bowen Zhao, Danhang Tang, Sasa Petrovic, Emre Aksan, Jérémy Riviere, Vassilis Choutas, Delio Vicini, Jay Busch, Shichen Liu, Zhe Cao, Hugh Liu, JingJing Shen, Jonathan Taylor, Mingsong Dou
First: 2026-06-14T18:47:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:39:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Robust, high-fidelity 3D hand capture, while fundamental to digital human creation, remains challenging with practical multi-view systems that balance rich photometry with the geometric ambiguities of reconstruction arising from limited viewpoint density. This paper presents an end-to-end pipeline for dynamic hand performance capture and registration, specifically designed for view-efficient setups ($\sim$20 views). We address key challenges with two primary innovations. First, to overcome reconstruction difficulties like limited view overlap and background clutter, our mask-free neural method robustly extracts detailed hand geometry and appearance from unmasked images using scene parameterization and scenario-specific density regularization. Second, addressing registration challenges such as accurately capturing non-linear skin deformations and ensuring plausible results during severe self-contact, we propose a physics-inspired framework. It aligns reconstructions to a personalized hand model by optimizing intrinsic volumetric offsets within its canonical tetrahedral mesh, alongside pose parameters. This approach, supported by robust losses and optimization, captures fine surface deformations, ensures plausible results under severe articulation and self-contact, and demonstrates strong tolerance to input noise. We demonstrate the scalability and robustness of our automated pipeline on an extensive dataset of over 12,000 sequences, from which we also derive a large-scale, high-quality synthetic 2D/3D hand dataset for training downstream tasks. This showcases its effectiveness for single hands, intricate two-hand interactions, and natural hand-object manipulations. Our method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity in view-efficient, unmasked scenarios and highly accurate registration. Our project page are available at https://vephand.github.io/.

标题：VEPHand：大规模视图高效光度手部性能捕捉
尽管高保真3D手部捕捉对数字人创建至关重要，但在实际多视角系统中，如何平衡丰富的光度信息与因视角密度不足导致的几何歧义仍具挑战。本文提出了一种端到端的动态手部性能捕捉与注册流程，专为视图高效设置（约20视角）设计。我们通过两项主要创新解决关键挑战：首先，为克服视角重叠不足和背景干扰导致的重建困难，我们提出一种无需掩码的神经方法，利用场景参数化与场景特定密度正则化，从未掩码图像中稳健提取手部几何与外观；其次，为解决注册难题——如准确捕捉非线性皮肤形变及在严重自接触时确保合理结果，我们提出一种受物理启发的框架，通过优化个性化手部模型内嵌入的体素偏移（基于其规范四面体网格）与姿态参数，将重建结果对齐。该方法结合稳健损失与优化，可捕捉精细表面形变，在剧烈关节运动与严重自接触下仍能生成合理结果，并对输入噪声具有强鲁棒性。我们在包含12,000多个序列的大型数据集上验证了该自动化流程的可扩展性与鲁棒性，并从中衍生出大规模高质量的2D/3D合成手部数据集，用于下游任务训练。该方法在视图高效、未掩码场景下实现最先进的重建保真度与高精度注册。项目页面见 https://vephand.github.io/。
Summary / 总结

It employs a mask-free neural method that leverages scene parameterization and density regularization to extract detailed hand geometry and appearance from unmasked images, overcoming issues like low view overlap and background clutter. For registration, it uses a physics-inspired framework optimizing volumetric offsets within a canonical tetrahedral mesh alongside pose parameters to handle non-linear skin deformations and self-contact. Evaluated on over 12,000 sequences, the method achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction fidelity and registration accuracy in view-efficient, unmasked conditions, and generates a large-scale synthetic dataset for downstream tasks, demonstrating robustness across single hands, two-hand interactions, and hand-object manipulations.
为解决实际多视角系统中因视角稀疏导致的高保真3D手部重建难题，本文提出VEPHand，一个面向约20个视角的端到端手部性能捕捉与注册管道。其通过无掩码神经方法结合场景参数化与密度正则化，从无掩码图像中稳健提取手部几何与外观；针对注册问题，采用受物理启发的框架，通过优化个性化手部模型内的体素偏移与姿态参数实现精确对齐。在12,000+序列的实验中，该方法在视角受限、自接触严重等条件下实现当前最优重建精度与注册准确性，并衍生出大规模高质量合成数据集用于下游任务。
Immersive and Wearable Thermal Rendering for Augmented Reality
Authors: Alexandra Watkins, Ritam Ghosh, Evan Chow, Nilanjan Sarkar
First: 2025-03-26T15:40:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:39:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a proof-of-concept palm-mounted thermal feedback prototype addressing thermal rendering challenges specific to augmented reality (AR), where users must interact with both real and virtual objects in their physical workspace. In contrast to thermal feedback systems developed for virtual reality, AR thermal feedback must preserve manual dexterity, maintain access to real-world thermal cues, and provide coherent virtual temperature sensations without obstructing natural object interaction. We propose three AR-specific design considerations, which our prototype implements: indirect feedback to preserve fingertip dexterity, active thermal passthrough to sense and render the temperature of contacted physical surfaces, and spatially and temporally varying thermal rendering across the palm. Human-subject experiments evaluated perceptual sensitivity, indirect feedback, active thermal passthrough, spatial pattern recognition, and moving thermal rendering during AR interaction. Results showed that although indirect feedback reduced perceived realism during visual contact at the fingertips, it did not reduce immersion or comfort; active thermal passthrough supported temperature discrimination between real and rendered surfaces; and spatiotemporal rendering significantly improved immersion and realism compared with static thermal stimulation. These findings suggest that our design considerations are viable design strategies for AR thermal haptics, while also clarifying tradeoffs for applications that require precise realism versus broader immersive thermal experience.

标题：增强现实中的沉浸式可穿戴热渲染
我们提出了一种掌上热反馈原型，旨在解决增强现实（AR）中特有的热渲染挑战，用户需在物理工作空间内同时与真实和虚拟物体交互。与为虚拟现实开发的热反馈系统不同，AR热反馈必须保持手动灵巧性、保留对现实世界热线索的感知，并在不阻碍自然物体交互的前提下提供连贯的虚拟温度感觉。我们提出了三项AR特异性设计考量，原型均予以实现：间接反馈以保持指尖灵巧性、主动热穿透以感知并渲染接触物理表面的温度，以及在手掌上实现空间与时间变化的热渲染。人体实验评估了感知敏感度、间接反馈、主动热穿透、空间模式识别及移动热渲染在AR交互中的表现。结果表明，尽管间接反馈在指尖视觉接触时降低了感知真实感，但并未降低沉浸感或舒适度；主动热穿透支持对真实与渲染表面温度的区分；而时空变化的热渲染相比静态热刺激显著提升了沉浸感与真实感。这些发现表明，我们的设计考量是AR热触觉可行的设计策略，同时明确了在需要精确真实感与广泛沉浸式热体验应用之间的权衡。

为在增强现实场景中实现兼顾操作灵活性与真实热感反馈的触觉体验，研究者设计了一款掌部佩戴的原型设备，采用三项AR特有设计：间接反馈以保持指尖灵敏度、主动热穿透以感知并渲染接触物体的真实温度、以及掌部空间与时间变化的热渲染。实验表明，间接反馈虽降低视觉接触时的现实感，但不影响沉浸感与舒适度；主动热穿透支持真实与虚拟表面温度的区分；而时空变化的热渲染显著提升沉浸感与真实感，证明该设计策略在AR热触觉应用中具有可行性并揭示了现实精度与沉浸体验间的权衡。
PCFootprint: A Large-Scale Dataset and Benchmark for Vectorized Building Footprint Extraction from Aerial LiDAR Point Clouds
Authors: Haoyuan Shen, Kuihao Wang, Ruisheng Wang, Yujun Liu
First: 2026-06-18T16:38:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:38:35+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Building footprint extraction is a fundamental task in photogrammetry, remote sensing, and computer vision. Recent image-based methods have achieved remarkable progress in extracting vectorized footprints from high-resolution optical imagery. However, optical imagery inherently susceptible to occlusions, perspective distortions, and residual relief displacement, yielding incomplete or misaligned footprint extraction. Furthermore, the lack of explicit elevation information limits its direct applicability to Level of Detail building modeling. In this paper, we present PCFootprint, the first large-scale public dataset for footprint extraction from airborne laser scanning point clouds. PCFootprint comprises \num{33000} tiles derived from the Estonian Land and Spatial Development Board, covering diverse urban and rural landscapes. Each tile spans \qtyproduct{128 x 128}{\m} with systematically aligned vectorized footprints aligned to point clouds. The dataset includes a \num{3000} tiles cross-domain test set for evaluating generalization across geographic regions. We establish comprehensive benchmarks by evaluating mainstream methods. Experimental results reveal significant challenges including high intra-class variance, data imbalance, and noise across complex geospatial environments. We believe PCFootprint will advance future research in building modeling, urban scene understanding, and geospatial analysis. The PCFootprint dataset is publicly available at \url{https://huggingface.co/datasets/Haoyuan-Shen/PCFootprint}.

Directors Duties in the Age of Agentic Artificial Intelligence
Authors: Deirdre Ahern
Venue: Cambridge Forum on AI: Law and Governance 2, e7 (2026)
First: 2026-06-18T16:33:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:33:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As boards engage with the adoption of Artificial Intelligence including agentic AI to drive operational efficiencies, this presents new opportunities for profit maximisation. AI adoption is increasingly identified with employee role displacement and in companies, and the interests of employees as stakeholders require exploration. A novel question posed is whether in an age of AI ascendancy AI may warrant being given stakeholder status as its role in the company approximates or eclipses that of human employees. The article probes four distinct models of corporate purpose within the duty on directors to act in the best interests of the company, the shareholder primacy model, the Enlightened Shareholder value model, the stakeholder friendly model, and the stakeholder value model, highlighting the available scope for directors to accommodate the interests of employees around AI adoption in decision-making by boards around AI. It is concluded that given the degree to which directors are insulated from legal scrutiny in relation to their best interests duty, adopting a wider law in context approach to promote employee welfare would serve the interests of employees, directors and companies alike. This would see directors engaging meaningfully with employees and providing opportunities for reskilling to adapt to the age of AI.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理型人工智能时代董事的职责

Motivated by the growing integration of agentic AI in corporate operations and its potential to displace human roles, this paper examines whether AI should be granted stakeholder status due to its increasing influence in companies. It analyzes four corporate purpose models—shareholder primacy, enlightened shareholder value, stakeholder-friendly, and stakeholder value—to evaluate how directors can incorporate employee interests into AI adoption decisions. The study finds that directors, shielded from legal scrutiny under their duty to act in the company’s best interests, can better serve all stakeholders by adopting a contextual, wider law approach that prioritizes employee welfare through meaningful engagement and reskilling opportunities.
