Collaborative Multi-Modal Coding for High-Quality 3D Generation
Authors: Ziang Cao, Zhaoxi Chen, Liang Pan, Ziwei Liu
First: 2025-08-21T04:31:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:33:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D content inherently encompasses multi-modal characteristics and can be projected into different modalities (e.g., RGB images, RGBD, and point clouds). Each modality exhibits distinct advantages in 3D asset modeling: RGB images contain vivid 3D textures, whereas point clouds define fine-grained 3D geometries. However, most existing 3D-native generative architectures either operate predominantly within single-modality paradigms-thus overlooking the complementary benefits of multi-modality data-or restrict themselves to 3D structures, thereby limiting the scope of available training datasets. To holistically harness multi-modalities for 3D modeling, we present TriMM, the first feed-forward 3D-native generative model that learns from basic multi-modalities (e.g., RGB, RGBD, and point cloud). Specifically, 1) TriMM first introduces collaborative multi-modal coding, which integrates modality-specific features while preserving their unique representational strengths. 2) Furthermore, auxiliary 2D and 3D supervision are introduced to raise the robustness and performance of multi-modal coding. 3) Based on the embedded multi-modal code, TriMM employs a triplane latent diffusion model to generate 3D assets of superior quality, enhancing both the texture and the geometric detail. Extensive experiments on multiple well-known datasets demonstrate that TriMM, by effectively leveraging multi-modality, achieves competitive performance with models trained on large-scale datasets, despite utilizing a small amount of training data. Furthermore, we conduct additional experiments on recent RGB-D datasets, verifying the feasibility of incorporating other multi-modal datasets into 3D generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：协作多模态编码用于高质量3D生成
3D内容固有地具有多模态特性，可投影到不同模态（如RGB图像、RGBD和点云）。每种模态在3D资产建模中各有优势：RGB图像包含生动的3D纹理，而点云定义精细的3D几何结构。然而，大多数现有3D原生生成架构要么主要在单模态范式下运行，从而忽略多模态数据的互补优势，要么仅限于3D结构，从而限制可用训练数据集的范围。为全面利用多模态进行3D建模，我们提出TriMM，这是首个从基础多模态（如RGB、RGBD和点云）学习的前馈3D原生生成模型。具体而言，1）TriMM首次引入协作多模态编码，整合模态特异性特征同时保留其独特表征优势；2）此外，引入辅助2D和3D监督以提升多模态编码的鲁棒性和性能；3）基于嵌入的多模态代码，TriMM采用三平面潜在扩散模型生成高质量3D资产，提升纹理与几何细节。在多个知名数据集上的大量实验表明，尽管仅使用少量训练数据，TriMM通过有效利用多模态，仍能实现与在大规模数据集上训练的模型相当的性能。此外，我们在近期RGB-D数据集上进行额外实验，验证将其他多模态数据集纳入3D生成的可行性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the complementary strengths of multi-modal 3D data such as RGB, RGBD, and point clouds, the paper proposes TriMM, a feed-forward 3D generative model that integrates these modalities through collaborative multi-modal coding to preserve their unique representational qualities. The method introduces auxiliary 2D and 3D supervision to enhance robustness and employs a triplane latent diffusion model to generate high-fidelity 3D assets. Experimental results across multiple datasets show that TriMM achieves competitive performance even with limited training data, outperforming single-modality models and demonstrating the feasibility of incorporating diverse multi-modal datasets into 3D generation.
为充分利用RGB图像、RGBD和点云等多模态数据的互补优势，本文提出TriMM，这是首个从多模态数据中学习的前馈3D生成模型。该模型通过协同多模态编码融合各模态特征并保留其独特表达能力，结合辅助2D和3D监督提升编码鲁棒性，并基于三平面潜在扩散架构生成高质量3D资产。实验表明，即使训练数据量少，TriMM仍能取得与大规模数据训练模型相当的性能，优于单模态方法，并验证了引入其他多模态数据集进行3D生成的可行性。
Qwen-RobotNav Technical Report: A Scalable Navigation Model Designed for an Agentic Navigation System
Authors: Jiazhao Zhang, Gengze Zhou, Hale Yin, Yiyang Huang, Zixing Lei, Qihang Peng, Haoqi Yuan, Jie Zhang, Xudong Guo, Xiaoyue Chen, An Yang, Fei Huang, Zhibo Yang, Junyang Lin, Dayiheng Liu, Jingren Zhou, Zhuoyuan Yu, Jingyang Fan, Zhixuan Liang, Pei Lin, Ye Wang, Anzhe Chen, Kun Yan, Xiao Xu, Jiahao Li, Lulu Hu, Minying Zhang, Shurui Li, Wenhu Xiao, Shuai Bai, Xuancheng Ren, Chenxu Lv, Chenfei Wu, Xiong-Hui Chen
First: 2026-06-16T16:17:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:31:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic navigation systems require a base navigation model whose observation strategy can be externally reconfigured at inference time, because instruction following, object search, target tracking, and autonomous driving share the same perception-planning backbone yet demand fundamentally different strategies for consuming the visual stream. We present Qwen-RobotNav, a scalable navigation model built on Qwen-RobotNav that addresses it through a parameterised interface with two complementary dimensions: multiple task modes that select the navigation behaviour, and controllable observation parameters (e.g., token budget, per-camera weights) that govern how visual history is encoded. With training-time randomization over all parameters, Qwen-RobotNav is robust to any inference-time configuration requiring zero architectural modification to the Qwen-RobotNav backbone. We train Qwen-RobotNav on 15.6M samples; co-training with vision-language data prevents the collapse into reactive action-sequence mappers observed in trajectory-only training. The parameterised interface also makes Qwen-RobotNav a natural building block for agentic systems: for long-horizon scenarios, an upper-level planner decomposes goals into sub-tasks and dynamically switches Qwen-RobotNav's task mode and context strategy mid-episode, composing complex behaviours from repeated calls to the same model. Extensive experiments show that Qwen-RobotNav sets new state-of-the-art results across major navigation benchmarks. The model exhibits favourable scaling from 2B to 8B parameters, with joint multi-task training developing a shared spatial-planning substrate that transfers across task families, and demonstrates strong zero-shot generalisation to real-world robots across diverse environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Qwen-RobotNav技术报告：为代理导航系统设计的可扩展导航模型
代理导航系统需要一个基础导航模型，其观察策略可在推理时由外部重新配置，因为指令遵循、物体搜索、目标跟踪和自动驾驶共享相同的感知-规划骨干，但对视觉流的消费策略却根本不同。我们提出了Qwen-RobotNav，一个基于Qwen-RobotNav构建的可扩展导航模型，通过参数化接口解决该问题，该接口包含两个互补维度：多个任务模式用于选择导航行为，以及可控的观察参数（如token预算、每摄像头权重）用于控制视觉历史的编码方式。通过训练时对所有参数的随机化，Qwen-RobotNav对任何推理时配置均具有鲁棒性，且无需对Qwen-RobotNav骨干架构进行任何修改。我们在1560万样本上训练Qwen-RobotNav；与视觉-语言数据联合训练可避免仅用轨迹训练时出现的反应式动作序列映射器崩溃现象。参数化接口也使Qwen-RobotNav成为代理系统自然的构建模块：在长时序场景中，高层规划器将目标分解为子任务，并在回合中动态切换Qwen-RobotNav的任务模式和上下文策略，通过重复调用同一模型组合出复杂行为。大量实验表明，Qwen-RobotNav在主要导航基准测试中均取得新SOTA结果。该模型在2B至8B参数规模间表现出良好的扩展性，联合多任务训练发展出可跨任务家族迁移的共享空间规划基底，并在多样化环境中对真实机器人展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for flexible visual perception strategies in agentic navigation systems, Qwen-RobotNav introduces a parameterized interface enabling dynamic reconfiguration of navigation behavior at inference time. It combines multiple task modes for different navigation goals and controllable observation parameters like token budget and camera weights to adapt visual encoding. Trained on 15.6M samples with vision-language co-training to avoid collapse into reactive action mappers, the model achieves state-of-the-art performance across major benchmarks. It scales favorably from 2B to 8B parameters, develops a shared spatial-planning foundation through multi-task learning, and shows strong zero-shot generalization to real-world robots in diverse environments.
为支持指令跟随、物体搜索、目标跟踪和自动驾驶等不同任务在推理时灵活调整视觉处理策略，Qwen-RobotNav设计了参数化接口，支持任务模式切换与视觉历史编码参数（如token预算、相机权重）的动态控制。该模型在训练时通过随机化参数配置增强鲁棒性，无需修改底层架构。结合视觉-语言数据联合训练，避免了仅用轨迹数据训练导致的反应式动作映射崩溃问题，并在多个导航基准测试中达到当前最优性能。模型在2B至8B参数规模下表现良好，多任务联合训练构建了可跨任务迁移的空间规划基础，且在真实机器人环境中展现出强大的零样本泛化能力。
Beyond Reasoning Gains: Mitigating General-Capability Forgetting in Large Reasoning Models
Authors: Hoang Phan, Xianjun Yang, Yuanshun Yao, Jingyu Zhang, Shengjie Bi, Xiaocheng Tang, Madian Khabsa, Lijuan Liu, Deren Lei
First: 2025-10-24T19:08:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:29:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has delivered impressive gains in mathematical and multimodal reasoning and has become a standard post-training paradigm for contemporary language and vision-language models. However, the RLVR recipe introduces a significant risk of capability regression, in which models forget foundational skills after prolonged training without employing regularization strategies. We empirically confirm this concern, observing that open-source reasoning models suffer performance degradation on core capabilities such as perception and faithfulness. While imposing regularization terms like KL divergence can help prevent deviation from the base model, these terms are computed on the current task and therefore do not guarantee preservation of broader knowledge. Meanwhile, commonly used experience replay across heterogeneous domains makes it nontrivial to decide how much training emphasis each objective should receive. To address this, we propose RECAP-a replay strategy with dynamic objective reweighting for general knowledge preservation. Our reweighting mechanism adapts online using short-horizon signals of convergence and instability, shifting the post-training focus away from saturated objectives and toward underperforming or volatile ones. Our method is end-to-end and readily applicable to existing RLVR pipelines without training additional models or heavy tuning. Extensive experiments on benchmarks using Qwen2.5-VL-3B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, which not only preserves general capabilities but also improves reasoning by enabling more flexible trade-offs among in-task rewards.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越推理能力提升：缓解大推理模型中的通用能力遗忘
可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）在数学和多模态推理方面取得了显著进展，并已成为当代语言和视觉-语言模型后训练的标准范式。然而，RLVR方法引入了能力退化的重要风险，即在长期训练中未采用正则化策略时，模型会遗忘基础技能。我们通过实证验证了这一担忧，发现开源推理模型在感知和忠实性等核心能力上出现性能下降。虽然引入KL散度等正则化项有助于防止模型偏离基线，但这些项仅基于当前任务计算，无法保证更广泛知识的保留。同时，跨异构领域常用的经验回放使如何分配各目标的训练权重变得复杂。为此，我们提出RECAP——一种具有动态目标重加权的回放策略，用于保留通用知识。我们的重加权机制在线适应，利用短期收敛与不稳定信号，将后训练重点从饱和目标转向表现不佳或波动较大的目标。该方法端到端实现，可直接应用于现有RLVR流程，无需额外训练模型或复杂调参。在Qwen2.5-VL-3B和Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型上的大量实验表明，我们的方法不仅有效保留通用能力，还能通过更灵活的在任务奖励间权衡，提升推理性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of general-capability forgetting in large reasoning models trained via reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the paper proposes RECAP, a replay strategy that dynamically reweights objectives during post-training to preserve foundational skills. By adapting weights based on short-horizon signals of convergence and instability, RECAP shifts focus toward underperforming or volatile tasks, avoiding overemphasis on saturated objectives. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B and Qwen2.5-VL-7B show that RECAP not only prevents degradation in core capabilities like perception and faithfulness but also enhances reasoning performance by enabling more flexible reward trade-offs without requiring additional models or heavy tuning.
为缓解强化学习与可验证奖励训练中大模型遗忘通用能力的问题，本文提出RECAP方法，通过动态重加权训练目标，在后训练阶段保留基础能力。该方法根据短期收敛与不稳定信号在线调整目标权重，将训练重点从已饱和任务转向表现不佳或波动较大的任务，无需额外训练或复杂调参。在Qwen2.5-VL-3B和Qwen2.5-VL-7B模型上的实验表明，RECAP不仅有效防止了感知与忠实性等核心能力的退化，还通过更灵活的奖励权衡提升了推理性能。
SSH-Net: A Deep Neural Network for Predicting Failure Time Distribution Functions under Competing Risks with Application to GPU Data
Authors: Jie Min, Yueyao Wang, Mengkun Chen
First: 2026-06-18T16:29:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:29:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Competing risks are commonly observed in engineering fields and can bring challenges to time-to-event data modeling when the application scenarios are complicated. Recently, deep neural networks have received great attention for prediction with competing risks, due to their flexibility and high learning capability. However, the complexity of neural network structure brings extra difficulty in hyperparameter tuning based on different data inputs. Additionally, when an engineered system has complex physical structures with multiple hierarchical levels, treating all structural levels as a single group of inputs may fail to capture critical information. To address the issues, we propose a Structured Segmented Hazard Deep Neural Network (SSH-Net) for failure time prediction under cause-specific competing risks framework. Our approach associates neural network structure with data structures, and allows different covariate groups to impact the failure prediction through separate sub-networks. The neural network is constructed based on a cause-specific competing risks model. The SSH-Net outputs cause-specific hazard functions, and utilizes the penalized log-likelihood as the loss function. The prediction accuracy of SSH-Net is validated through simulation studies by evaluating the Brier score, the area under receiver operating characteristic curves (AUC), and the root mean square error (RMSE) of the predicted cause-specific cumulative incident function. We further demonstrate the model's ability to predict failure time distribution functions using the Titan GPU failure time data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SSH-Net：一种用于在竞争风险下预测故障时间分布函数的深度神经网络及其在GPU数据中的应用
竞争风险在工程领域中普遍存在，当应用场景复杂时，会对时间至事件数据建模带来挑战。近年来，由于其灵活性和强大的学习能力，深度神经网络在竞争风险预测中受到广泛关注。然而，神经网络结构的复杂性给基于不同数据输入的超参数调优带来了额外困难。此外，当工程系统具有复杂的物理结构和多层级时，将所有结构层级视为单一输入组可能无法捕捉关键信息。为解决这些问题，我们提出了一种结构化分段风险深度神经网络（SSH-Net），用于在特定原因竞争风险框架下预测故障时间。我们的方法将神经网络结构与数据结构相结合，允许不同协变量组通过独立子网络影响故障预测。该神经网络基于特定原因竞争风险模型构建，SSH-Net输出特定原因的风险函数，并采用惩罚对数似然作为损失函数。通过模拟研究，我们通过评估Brier分数、接收者操作特征曲线下面积（AUC）和预测特定原因累积事件函数的均方根误差（RMSE）验证了SSH-Net的预测准确性。我们进一步利用Titan GPU故障时间数据展示了该模型预测故障时间分布函数的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of modeling failure time under competing risks in complex engineering systems, this paper introduces SSH-Net, a deep neural network that aligns neural architecture with data structure by using separate sub-networks for different covariate groups. The model is built upon a cause-specific competing risks framework and outputs cause-specific hazard functions, optimizing via penalized log-likelihood. Experimental results on simulated data and real Titan GPU failure data show that SSH-Net outperforms baseline models in prediction accuracy, as measured by Brier score, AUC, and RMSE of predicted cumulative incidence functions.
为解决复杂工程系统中竞争风险下时间到事件数据建模的挑战，本文提出SSH-Net，一种将神经网络结构与数据结构对齐的深度神经网络，通过为不同协变量组构建独立子网络，融入特定原因竞争风险框架，输出特定原因的危险函数，并以惩罚对数似然作为损失函数。在模拟数据和Titan GPU故障数据上的实验表明，SSH-Net在Brier分数、AUC和累积发生函数RMSE等指标上优于基线模型，预测精度更高。
InfantFace: Detecting infant faces in neonatal clinical environments
Authors: Abdullah Bin-Obaid, Maria M. Cobo, Rebeccah Slater, Lionel Tarassenko, Mauricio Villarroel
First: 2026-06-18T16:28:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:28:00+00:00
Comments: 32 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables; supplementary information included
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reliable localisation of the neonatal face is the first step for several video-camera based non-contact assessments such as pain and distress related facial expression analysis, pain scoring, cardiorespiratory signal extraction and cessation of breathing alerts. However, major challenges persist in neonatal clinical environments. Cluttered backgrounds, illumination changes and poor lighting conditions can reduce the accuracy of face detection models. Clinical interventions, monitoring equipment and, in some cases, medical devices can obstruct the face, making visual assessment difficult. We propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model tailored for face detection of infants in neonatal clinical environments. We combined multiple publicly available datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE) to train and evaluate our proposed model. We then fine-tuned our model on a neonatal research dataset involving 228 videos from 114 recording sessions of 113 independent infants. Before fine-tuning, our model achieved an AP50 of 0.87, surpassing the performance of three state-of-the-art general face detectors. Performance improved further to an AP50 of 0.96 after clinical-domain adaptation. Evaluating face detection performance across different datasets remains a challenge due to the lack of publicly available neonatal datasets. Prioritising the creation of such datasets, while upholding appropriate privacy safeguards and ethical standards in their creation and use, would greatly support further progress in this field.
中文标题/摘要
标题：InfantFace：新生儿临床环境中婴儿面部检测
可靠的新生儿面部定位是多种基于视频摄像头的非接触式评估（如疼痛和痛苦相关面部表情分析、疼痛评分、心肺信号提取及呼吸停止警报）的第一步。然而，新生儿临床环境中仍存在重大挑战：杂乱背景、光照变化和照明条件差会降低面部检测模型的准确性；临床干预、监测设备，有时还有医疗设备会遮挡面部，使视觉评估困难。我们提出了一种基于YOLOv11m的一阶段模型，专为新生儿临床环境中婴儿面部检测设计。我们结合多个公开数据集（VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB、WIDER FACE）训练和评估该模型，随后在包含113名独立婴儿114次录制会话的228个视频的新生儿研究数据集上进行微调。微调前，模型AP50达到0.87，优于三个最先进的通用面部检测器；临床领域适配后，性能进一步提升至AP50 0.96。由于缺乏公开的新生儿数据集，跨不同数据集评估面部检测性能仍具挑战性。优先创建此类数据集，并在创建和使用过程中确保适当隐私保护和伦理标准，将极大推动该领域进一步发展。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reliably detecting infant faces in cluttered, low-light neonatal clinical settings where standard face detectors fail due to occlusions and poor illumination, the authors propose a one-stage YOLOv11m-based model specifically adapted for this domain. Trained on a combination of public face datasets (VGGFace2, CelebA, FDDB, WIDER FACE), the model achieved an AP50 of 0.87 before fine-tuning, outperforming three state-of-the-art general detectors. After fine-tuning on a neonatal dataset of 228 videos from 113 infants, performance improved to AP50 of 0.96, demonstrating strong adaptation to clinical conditions. The study highlights the scarcity of public neonatal datasets and advocates for their creation under ethical and privacy-preserving frameworks to advance research in neonatal visual assessment.
为解决新生儿临床环境中因背景杂乱、光照不足及医疗设备遮挡导致的婴儿面部检测难题，作者提出一种基于YOLOv11m的一阶段检测模型，专为该场景优化。模型首先在VGGFace2、CelebA、FDDB和WIDER FACE等公开数据集上训练，AP50达0.87，优于三款主流通用人脸检测器；随后在包含113名婴儿、228段视频的新生儿研究数据集上微调，性能提升至AP50 0.96，显著适应临床环境挑战。作者同时指出，缺乏公开新生儿数据集仍是该领域发展的瓶颈，亟需在保障隐私与伦理前提下构建更多此类数据集以推动技术进步。
Stabilizing Bandits using Regularization: Precise Regret and A Quantitative Central Limit Theorem
Authors: Budhaditya Halder, Ishan Sengupta, Koustav Chowdhury, Samya Praharaj, Koulik Khamaru
First: 2026-03-10T19:27:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:25:07+00:00
Comments: Updated rate of convergence and precise regret in version 2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Statistical inference with bandit data presents fundamental challenges owing to adaptive sampling, which violates the independence assumptions underlying classical asymptotic theory. Recent work has identified stability~\citep{laiwei82} as a sufficient condition for valid inference under adaptivity. This paper first provides a refined stability condition, stated in terms of the iterates of an online algorithm, and shows that a large class of regularized stochastic-mirror-descent-style algorithms satisfy it. This refined condition allows us to strengthen the asymptotic results of~\citet{laiwei82} in several ways. First, we derive a non-asymptotic Berry--Esseen bound for the empirical reward estimates under adaptive sampling. Second, we derive matching non-asymptotic upper and lower bounds on the regret of the proposed algorithm, yielding a precise characterization of its regret. Third, we show that these regularized algorithms preserve asymptotic normality and valid inference under a prescribed level of adversarial corruption. Finally, we show that regularization is necessary rather than incidental: Lai--Wei stability is incompatible with the optimal $O(\sqrt{T})$ regret rate -- the rate attained by unregularized algorithms such as EXP3 -- so that a controlled, polylogarithmic inflation in regret is the price of valid inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：使用正则化稳定多臂赌博机：精确遗憾与定量中心极限定理
由于自适应采样违反了经典渐近理论所依赖的独立性假设，基于多臂赌博机数据的统计推断面临根本性挑战。近期研究已将稳定性~\citep{laiwei82}确认为适应性条件下有效推断的充分条件。本文首先提出一个更精细的稳定性条件，以在线算法的迭代形式表述，并证明一大类正则化随机镜像下降式算法满足该条件。该精细条件使我们能在多个方面强化~\citet{laiwei82}的渐近结果：首先，我们推导出在自适应采样下经验奖励估计的非渐近Berry--Esseen界；其次，我们推导出所提算法遗憾的匹配非渐近上下界，从而精确刻画其遗憾；第三，我们证明这些正则化算法在指定对抗性污染水平下仍保持渐近正态性与有效推断；最后，我们证明正则化是必要的而非偶然的：Lai--Wei稳定性与最优$O(\sqrt{T})$遗憾率（如EXP3等未正则化算法所达到）不兼容，因此有效推断需付出可控的对数阶遗憾膨胀代价。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of performing valid statistical inference under adaptive bandit sampling, which breaks classical independence assumptions, this paper introduces a refined stability condition based on online algorithm iterates. It demonstrates that many regularized stochastic-mirror-descent algorithms satisfy this condition, enabling stronger asymptotic results. The authors derive non-asymptotic Berry--Esseen bounds for reward estimates, precise matching upper and lower regret bounds, and prove asymptotic normality under adversarial corruption. Crucially, they show that regularization is necessary: Lai--Wei stability prevents achieving the optimal O(√T) regret rate, forcing a polylogarithmic regret inflation for valid inference.
为了解决自适应多臂赌博机采样破坏经典独立性假设从而导致统计推断失效的问题，本文提出了一种基于在线算法迭代的精细化稳定性条件，并证明大量正则化随机镜像下降类算法满足该条件，从而获得更强的理论保证。作者推导了自适应采样下经验奖励估计的非渐近Berry-Esseen界，给出了所提算法的精确非渐近上下界后悔值，证明了在对抗性干扰下仍保持渐近正态性。更重要的是，他们指出正则化是必要的：Lai-Wei稳定性与最优O(√T)后悔率不兼容，因此为实现有效推断必须付出对数阶后悔膨胀的代价。
Reinforcement Learning Foundation Models Should Already Be A Thing
Authors: Abdelrahman Zighem, Jill-Jênn Vie
First: 2026-06-17T08:27:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:24:52+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Foundation models for language and vision are powered by internet-scale data, while structured domains such as tabular prediction are powered by synthetic data. This substitute shifts the challenge from collection to prior design. Such priors already exist for many structured tasks: TabPFN and its successors solve tabular classification with a transformer pretrained on a synthetic Bayesian prior. We make two points. \textbf{First}, reinforcement learning is the conspicuous gap: sampling a synthetic MDP is as feasible as sampling a synthetic tabular dataset, yet no in-context RL work treats prior design as a primary objective. \textbf{Second}, MDPs admit a fixed-size sufficient statistic, independent of the episodes observed and tabular in shape, which makes them directly amenable to the attention-based architectures used for tabular foundation models, with a policy head replacing the supervised target. Together these define the agenda for an RL foundation model. As a proof of concept, we train a Graph Attention Network entirely on synthetic MDPs and show that, with no task-specific tuning, it solves held-out tabular benchmarks in context, both online and offline: online, in far fewer episodes than UCB-VI and tabular Q-learning, and offline, competitively with VI-LCB.
中文标题/摘要
标题：强化学习基础模型本应早已存在
语言和视觉的基础模型由互联网规模的数据驱动，而结构化领域（如表格预测）则依赖合成数据。这种替代将挑战从数据收集转向先验设计。许多结构化任务已有此类先验：TabPFN 及其后续模型通过在合成贝叶斯先验上预训练的 Transformer 解决表格分类问题。 我们提出两点：\textbf{第一}，强化学习是明显缺失的领域：采样合成 MDP 与采样合成表格数据一样可行，但目前尚无在上下文强化学习中将先验设计作为首要目标的工作。\textbf{第二}，MDP 具有固定大小的充分统计量，且与观察到的轨迹无关，呈表格形式，使其可直接适配用于表格基础模型的注意力架构，仅需用策略头替代监督目标。这两点共同定义了强化学习基础模型的研究议程。 作为概念验证，我们完全在合成 MDP 上训练图注意力网络，并证明其无需任务特定调优即可在上下文内解决保留的表格基准测试，无论是在线还是离线：在线时，所需轨迹远少于 UCB-VI 和表格 Q 学习；离线时，表现与 VI-LCB 竞争。
Summary / 总结
The paper argues that reinforcement learning (RL) should adopt foundation model principles by leveraging synthetic MDPs as priors, mirroring how tabular models use synthetic Bayesian priors. It proposes training RL models with attention-based architectures, such as Graph Attention Networks, on synthetic MDPs to enable in-context learning without task-specific tuning. Experiments show the model solves tabular RL benchmarks both online and offline: online, it achieves faster convergence than UCB-VI and tabular Q-learning; offline, it matches the performance of VI-LCB.
该论文指出，强化学习应借鉴基础模型理念，利用合成MDP作为先验，类似表格任务中使用合成贝叶斯先验。其方法是训练基于注意力机制的强化学习模型（如图注意力网络），在合成MDP上进行训练，用策略头替代监督目标。实验表明，该方法无需任务特定调优即可在表格基准上表现优异：在线学习时比UCB-VI和表格Q学习收敛更快；离线学习时性能可媲美VI-LCB。
Topological Data Analysis for High-Dimensional Dynamic Process Monitoring
Authors: Angan Mukherjee, Tyler A. Soderstrom, Michael J. Kurtz, Victor M. Zavala
First: 2026-06-18T16:22:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:22:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real-time process monitoring requires methods that extract actionable information from high-dimensional time-series data. In this work, we present a new approach for process monitoring that combines tools of topological data analysis (TDA) and machine learning. In the proposed approach, we represent multivariate time-series data as manifolds and use topological descriptors to summarize the structure of such data; we then use a neural ordinary differential equation to learn the dynamic evolution of the topological structure of the system. Using real data from an industrial process, we show that this trajectory-based event detection approach is effective at detecting diverse types of events. We contrast this approach against reconstruction-based approaches such as principal component analysis and autoencoders and against a trajectory-based approach that uses Koopman autoencoders.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高维动态过程监控的拓扑数据分析方法
实时过程监控需要从高维时间序列数据中提取可操作信息的方法。本文提出了一种结合拓扑数据分析（TDA）工具与机器学习的新方法。在该方法中，我们将多变量时间序列数据表示为流形，并使用拓扑描述符总结其结构；随后利用神经常微分方程学习系统拓扑结构的动态演化。通过工业过程的真实数据，我们证明了该基于轨迹的事件检测方法能有效检测多种类型事件。我们将其与基于重构的方法（如主成分分析和自编码器）以及使用Koopman自编码器的基于轨迹的方法进行了对比。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to extract meaningful insights from high-dimensional time-series data in real-time industrial monitoring, this study introduces a method that combines topological data analysis and machine learning. It represents multivariate time-series as manifolds, extracts topological descriptors to capture structural patterns, and employs a neural ordinary differential equation to model the dynamic evolution of these topological features. Experiments on real industrial data demonstrate that this trajectory-based event detection method outperforms traditional reconstruction-based methods like PCA and autoencoders, as well as a competing trajectory-based approach using Koopman autoencoders, in detecting diverse process anomalies.
为解决高维时序数据在工业实时监控中提取有效信息的难题，该研究提出一种结合拓扑数据分析与机器学习的方法。该方法将多变量时序数据建模为流形，通过拓扑描述符捕捉其结构特征，并利用神经常微分方程建模拓扑结构的动态演化。在真实工业数据上的实验表明，该基于轨迹的事件检测方法在检测多种过程事件方面，优于基于重构的PCA与自编码器方法，以及基于轨迹的Koopman自编码器方法。
Evolutionary Two-Stage Hyperparameter Optimization Strategies for Physics-Informed Neural Networks
Authors: Fedor Buzaev, Dmitry Efremenko, Egor Bugaev, Andrei Ermakov, Denis Derkach, Daria Pugacheva, Fedor Ratnikov
Venue: ICLR 2026
First: 2026-06-18T16:22:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:22:52+00:00
Comments: Equal advising: Daria Pugacheva and Fedor Ratnikov. Accepted to the ICLR 2026 Workshop on AI and PDEs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve Partial Differential Equations (PDEs) by embedding physical laws into neural network training. However, their performance suffers from unstable convergence, training plateaus, and strong sensitivity to architectural and optimization hyperparameters due to the highly non-convex and multi-term structure of the physics-informed loss. In this setting, the outer-loop hyperparameter search is a noisy and black-box optimization problem over heterogeneous parameters, where classical local or gradient-based strategies are easily trapped in suboptimal regions. Evolutionary algorithms, with their population-based exploration and ability to handle mixed, non-differentiable search spaces, provide a more robust mechanism for discovering promising configurations. We propose and investigate a two-stage approach based on evolutionary algorithms that combines exploration and exploitation parts of PINNs training to improve solution accuracy and robustness under fixed computational budgets. In the first stage, we perform low-fidelity training runs with truncated epochs to rapidly screen candidate configurations, treating hyperparameter selection as a black-box outer-loop problem. In the second stage, only the most promising candidates are fully trained with standard gradient-based optimizers to refine the solution. Evaluated on three popular problems, namely Advection, Klein-Gordon and Helmholtz equations, our method consistently outperforms standard training and achieves significantly lower mean error within constrained computational resources.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向物理信息神经网络的进化两阶段超参数优化策略
物理信息神经网络（PINNs）通过将物理定律嵌入神经网络训练中求解偏微分方程（PDEs），但其性能受物理信息损失函数高度非凸和多项结构的影响，常出现收敛不稳定、训练停滞及对架构与优化超参数高度敏感等问题。在此背景下，外层超参数搜索是一个噪声大、黑箱的异构参数优化问题，经典局部或梯度优化方法易陷入局部最优。进化算法凭借其基于种群的探索能力及处理混合、不可微搜索空间的优势，可更稳健地发现优质配置。本文提出并研究一种基于进化算法的两阶段方法，结合PINNs训练中的探索与利用阶段，以在固定计算预算下提升解的精度与鲁棒性。第一阶段采用截断训练周期的低保真训练快速筛选候选配置，将超参数选择视为黑箱外层优化问题；第二阶段仅对最有前景的候选配置进行完整训练，使用标准梯度优化器进一步优化解。在三个经典问题——对流方程、克莱因-戈尔登方程和亥姆霍兹方程上评估，本方法在受限计算资源下持续优于标准训练，显著降低平均误差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and sensitivity of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) due to their non-convex, multi-term loss structure, this work proposes a two-stage evolutionary hyperparameter optimization strategy. The first stage uses truncated, low-fidelity training runs to explore candidate hyperparameter configurations via evolutionary algorithms, treating the outer-loop search as a noisy black-box problem. The second stage refines only the top-performing candidates with full gradient-based training. Experiments on Advection, Klein-Gordon, and Helmholtz equations show that this approach consistently outperforms standard PINN training, achieving significantly lower mean error under fixed computational budgets.
为了解决物理信息神经网络（PINN）因损失函数高度非凸而导致的收敛不稳定和对超参数敏感的问题，本文提出一种两阶段进化优化策略。第一阶段通过截断训练的低保真度运行，利用进化算法探索超参数配置，将外层搜索视为黑盒问题；第二阶段仅对表现最佳的候选配置进行完整梯度优化。在阿德沃森、克莱因-戈登和亥姆霍兹方程三个典型问题上，该方法在计算资源受限条件下，持续优于标准训练方法，显著降低平均误差。
A High-Resolution Landscape Dataset for Concept-Based XAI With Application to Species Distribution Models
Authors: Augustin de la Brosse, Damien Garreau, Thomas Houet, Thomas Corpetti
First: 2026-04-14T19:16:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:20:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Mapping the spatial distribution of species is essential for conservation policy and invasive species management. Species distribution models (SDMs) are the primary tools for this task, serving two purposes: achieving robust predictive performance while providing ecological insights into the driving factors of distribution. However, the increasing complexity of deep learning SDMs has made extracting these insights more challenging. To reconcile these objectives, we propose the first implementation of concept-based Explainable AI (XAI) for SDMs. We leverage the Robust TCAV (Testing with Concept Activation Vectors) methodology to quantify the influence of landscape concepts on model predictions. To enable this, we provide a new open-access landscape concept dataset derived from high-resolution multispectral and LiDAR drone imagery. It includes 653 patches across 15 distinct landscape concepts and 1,450 random reference patches, designed to suit a wide range of species. We demonstrate this approach through a case study of two aquatic insects, Plecoptera and Trichoptera, using two Convolutional Neural Networks and one Vision Transformer. Results show that concept-based XAI helps validate SDMs against expert knowledge while uncovering novel associations that generate new ecological hypotheses. Robust TCAV also provides landscape-level information, useful for policy-making and land management. Code and datasets are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于基于概念的可解释人工智能的高分辨率景观数据集及其在物种分布模型中的应用
绘制物种的空间分布对于保护政策和入侵物种管理至关重要。物种分布模型（SDMs）是完成此任务的主要工具，其作用包括：实现稳健的预测性能，同时提供关于分布驱动因素的生态学见解。然而，随着深度学习SDMs复杂性的增加，提取这些见解变得更加困难。为协调这些目标，我们提出了首个针对SDMs的概念性可解释人工智能（XAI）实现方案。我们利用鲁棒TCAV（基于概念激活向量的测试）方法量化景观概念对模型预测的影响。为此，我们提供了一个新的开放访问景观概念数据集，该数据集源自高分辨率多光谱和LiDAR无人机影像，包含15种不同景观概念的653个样本和1,450个随机参考样本，适用于多种物种。我们通过两个水生昆虫（石蝇科和毛翅目）的案例研究，使用两个卷积神经网络和一个视觉Transformer进行验证。结果表明，基于概念的XAI有助于将SDMs与专家知识进行验证，同时揭示新的关联，生成新的生态学假设。鲁棒TCAV还提供了景观层面的信息，对政策制定和土地管理具有实用价值。代码和数据集均已公开。

为解决复杂深度学习模型在物种分布预测中难以提取生态解释的问题，本研究首次将基于概念的可解释人工智能（XAI）应用于物种分布模型（SDM）。采用Robust TCAV方法量化景观概念对模型预测的影响，并依托一个由无人机多光谱与激光雷达影像构建的新高分辨率景观概念数据集（含653个概念样本和1450个参考样本）。在两种水生昆虫物种的案例中，使用CNN与Vision Transformer模型验证了SDM结果与专家知识的一致性，并发现新的生态关联，为生态保护与土地管理提供了可操作的景观级信息。
Interpretable Sperm Morphology Classification via Attention-Guided Deep Learning
Authors: Zahra Asghari Varzaneh, Reza Khoshkangini, Thomas Ebner, Lars Johansson
First: 2026-06-18T16:19:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:19:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Male infertility is a major cause of couple infertility, often linked to abnormal sperm morphology. While deep learning models offer automated analysis, most lack interpretability, limiting their clinical adoption. This study proposes an attention-guided deep learning framework for sperm morphology classification. We combine a pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to focus on key areas of the sperm head, improving both accuracy and interpretability. Evaluated on the SMIDS and HuSHem public datasets, our model achieves accuracies of 90.2% and 93.9% (macro F1 scores of 0.913 and 0.948), outperforming SimpleCNN and standard EfficientNet-B0. Furthermore, we use Grad-CAM++ visualizations to highlight features influencing the model's decisions. The results demonstrate that this accurate and transparent framework is a practical tool for automated sperm analysis in fertility clinics.

Driven by the need for accurate and interpretable sperm morphology analysis in male infertility diagnosis, this study introduces an attention-guided deep learning model combining pretrained EfficientNet-B0 with CBAM to focus on critical sperm head regions. 为解决男性不育诊断中精子形态自动分析缺乏可解释性的问题，本研究提出一种结合预训练EfficientNet-B0与卷积块注意力模块（CBAM）的注意力引导深度学习模型，聚焦精子头部关键区域。在两个公开数据集上，该模型准确率达90.2%和93.9%（宏F1分数分别为0.913和0.948），优于SimpleCNN和标准EfficientNet-B0。通过Grad-CAM++可视化验证了模型决策依据，证明其具备临床实用价值，可用于辅助精子形态自动化分析。
HEPTv2: End-to-End Efficient Point Transformer for Charged Particle Reconstruction
Authors: Siqi Miao, Shitij Govil, Jack P. Rodgers, Mia Liu, Javier Duarte, Shih-Chieh Hsu, Yuan-Tang Chou, Pan Li
First: 2026-06-18T16:18:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:18:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Charged-particle tracking -- reconstructing trajectories from sparse detector measurements -- is a fundamental high-energy-physics inference problem and a canonical example of learning under extreme combinatorial ambiguity. At the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider (HL-LHC), tracking must remain accurate and efficient despite unprecedented collision densities. Graph neural networks perform strongly, but incur substantial costs from graph construction and processing, while transformer-based approaches rely on auxiliary stages that prevent end-to-end optimization. To address this, we present HEPTv2, an end-to-end point-transformer architecture that reconstructs tracks from detector hits in one trainable pipeline. HEPTv2 combines a locality-aware point encoder with a track decoder that predicts complete trajectories without graph-building, clustering, or filtering. The encoder uses locality-sensitive hashing in detector coordinate space to preserve tracking-relevant geometry while enabling efficient local attention. The decoder resolves ambiguities through sectorized decoding and direct hit-to-track prediction under joint encoder-decoder supervision, allowing the full pipeline to be optimized end-to-end. On TrackML, HEPTv2 achieves 98.6% double-majority tracking efficiency at a 0.8% fake rate, while requiring only $\sim$15~ms inference time and 0.4~GB peak memory per event on a NVIDIA A100 GPU. Latency and memory scale approximately linearly for events with up to $5\times10^5$ hits. HEPTv2 establishes a new state of the art in the accuracy-latency trade-off, improving efficiency by 4.5% over the strongest prior transformer and by 1.1--2.2% over optimized graph-based pipelines, while reducing latency by factors of 7 and 38--52, respectively. These results show end-to-end transformers can deliver the accuracy and efficiency required for real-time particle reconstruction at the HL-LHC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HEPTv2：用于带电粒子重建的端到端高效点变换器
带电粒子轨迹重建——从稀疏探测器测量中重构轨迹——是高能物理中的基础推断问题，也是在极端组合模糊性下学习的典型范例。在高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）中，尽管碰撞密度空前，追踪仍需保持准确性和高效性。图神经网络表现优异，但图构建与处理成本高昂；基于变换器的方法则依赖辅助阶段，阻碍端到端优化。为此，我们提出HEPTv2，一种端到端点变换器架构，通过单一可训练流程从探测器击中点重建轨迹。HEPTv2结合局部感知点编码器与轨迹解码器，无需图构建、聚类或滤波即可预测完整轨迹。编码器在探测器坐标空间中使用局部敏感哈希，保留与追踪相关的几何结构，同时支持高效局部注意力。解码器通过扇区化解码与联合编码器-解码器监督下的直接击中点到轨迹预测，解决歧义，使整个流程可端到端优化。在TrackML数据集上，HEPTv2在0.8%假阳性率下实现98.6%的双多数追踪效率，单事件推理时间约15毫秒，峰值内存占用0.4GB（NVIDIA A100 GPU）。对于最多5×10⁵击中点的事件，延迟与内存消耗近线性增长。HEPTv2在精度-延迟权衡中建立新标杆，相比最强前序变换器效率提升4.5%，相比优化后的图基架构提升1.1–2.2%，延迟分别降低7倍和38–52倍。结果表明，端到端变换器可满足HL-LHC实时粒子重建所需的精度与效率。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of reconstructing charged particle trajectories from sparse detector hits under extreme combinatorial ambiguity at the HL-LHC, HEPTv2 introduces an end-to-end point-transformer architecture that eliminates graph construction and auxiliary stages. It employs a locality-aware point encoder using locality-sensitive hashing to preserve geometric structure efficiently, paired with a track decoder that predicts full trajectories via sectorized decoding and direct hit-to-track mapping under joint supervision. Experiments on TrackML show HEPTv2 achieves 98.6% double-majority tracking efficiency with only 0.8% fake rate, requiring just 15ms inference and 0.4GB memory per event on an A100 GPU, with linear scaling for up to 500,000 hits. It outperforms prior transformers and graph-based methods in accuracy-latency trade-off, improving efficiency by 4.5% and reducing latency by factors of 7 and 38–52 respectively.
为应对高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）下极端碰撞密度对粒子轨迹重建的精度与效率要求，HEPTv2提出一种端到端的点变换器架构，避免了传统图神经网络的昂贵图构建和辅助阶段。其采用基于局部敏感哈希的点编码器以保留几何结构并实现高效局部注意力，搭配通过扇区化解码与直接击中到轨迹预测的解码器，并在编码器-解码器联合监督下优化整个流程。在TrackML数据集上，HEPTv2实现98.6%双多数跟踪效率与0.8%假阳性率，单事件推理仅需15毫秒、峰值内存0.4GB（A100 GPU），相较最优Transformer提升4.5%效率，相较优化图方法提升1.1–2.2%，延迟分别降低7倍和38–52倍。
Indexed Bellman Information Complexity
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:16:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop indexed Bellman information complexity, a representation-level theory of interactive decision making centered on information indices and reference histories. The representation strips away problem-specific syntax and retains only the ingredients needed for dynamic programming and information accounting, thereby unifying the earlier framework of indexed algorithmic information ratios (AIR). On the upper-bound side, regret is controlled by Bellman supersolutions or potential identities whose gradient bracket is paid for by indexed information. Upper-confidence-bound (UCB), estimation-to-decision/decision-estimation-coefficient (E2D/DEC), and adaptive-minimax-sampling or exploration-by-optimization (AMS/EBO) methods appear as three relaxations of this same identity. On the lower-bound side, the posterior-reference trajectory supplies both the information telescope and the ghost quantile of small-regret trajectories. The resulting critical radius in the lower bound is an effective-dimension-scale quantity, as in Fano and local-prior-mass lower bounds, rather than the constant radius of a two-point Le Cam argument. The examples show that DEC is best viewed as a one-step relaxation of indexed Bellman information complexity, not as a universally tight conversion mechanism. We illustrate the framework through several applications, with particular emphasis on kernel bandits. In this setting, the active action marginal provides a concrete basis for comparing UCB, E2D, and AMS/EBO.
中文标题/摘要
标题：索引贝尔曼信息复杂度
我们发展了索引贝尔曼信息复杂度，这是一种以信息索引和参考历史为中心的交互式决策表示层面理论。该表示剥离了问题特定的语法，仅保留动态规划和信息核算所需的要素，从而统一了先前的索引算法信息比率（AIR）框架。在上界方面，遗憾由贝尔曼超解或势函数恒等式控制，其梯度括号由索引信息支付。上置信界（UCB）、估计到决策/决策-估计系数（E2D/DEC）以及自适应最小最大采样或优化探索（AMS/EBO）方法均表现为该恒等式的三种松弛形式。在下界方面，后验参考轨迹同时提供信息望远镜和小遗憾轨迹的幽灵分位数。所得下界中的关键半径是一个有效维度尺度量，如Fano和局部先验质量下界中的情况，而非两点Le Cam论证中的常数半径。示例表明，DEC最好被视作索引贝尔曼信息复杂度的一步松弛，而非普遍紧致的转换机制。我们通过多个应用说明该框架，尤其强调核带宽场景。在此场景中，活跃动作边际为比较UCB、E2D和AMS/EBO提供了具体基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify and generalize information-theoretic frameworks for interactive decision making, the paper introduces indexed Bellman information complexity, a representation-level theory that abstracts away problem-specific syntax to focus on dynamic programming and information accounting via information indices and reference histories. This framework generalizes earlier algorithmic information ratio (AIR) methods and provides a common structure for analyzing regret bounds. Upper bounds are derived from Bellman supersolutions or potential identities, where regret is controlled by the gradient bracket paid for via indexed information, encompassing UCB, E2D/DEC, and AMS/EBO as relaxations of the same identity. Lower bounds are established using posterior-reference trajectories to define an effective-dimension-scale critical radius, replacing the constant-radius Le Cam argument with more refined Fano- and local-prior-mass-style bounds. Experimental examples, especially in kernel bandits, show that DEC is best understood as a one-step relaxation rather than a universal tight conversion, and the framework enables direct comparison of UCB, E2D, and AMS/EBO through the active action marginal.
为构建交互式决策的统一表示层理论，本文提出索引贝尔曼信息复杂度，剥离问题特定语法，聚焦动态规划与信息核算所需的核心要素——信息索引与参考历史。该方法通过贝尔曼超解与势函数恒等式控制遗憾，将信息成本以梯度形式通过索引信息支付，统一了UCB、E2D/DEC与AMS/EBO三类方法作为同一恒等式的松弛形式。实验表明，DEC更宜视为一步松弛而非普适紧致转换器，且下界分析揭示关键半径与有效维度相关，而非传统Le Cam两点论证中的常数半径。
Multi-View Decompilation for LLM-Based Malware Classification
Authors: Bercan Turkmen, Vyas Raina
First: 2026-06-18T16:15:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:15:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Malware analysts often inspect compiled binaries through decompiled pseudo-C, when source code is unavailable. Recent work suggests that large language models (LLMs) can assist this process by classifying decompiled code as benign or malicious, but existing pipelines typically rely on a single decompiler view. We argue that this assumption is fragile: decompilers are lossy heuristic tools, and different decompilers can expose different artefacts of the same binary. We curate a benchmark of benign utilities and malicious programs spanning a range of threat behaviors. Each sample is compiled and decompiled with both Ghidra and RetDec, yielding matched pseudo-C views. Across a range of LLMs from major model families, we find that providing both decompiler views improves malicious-class F1, mainly by increasing recall on malicious samples. Agreement analyses further show that Ghidra and RetDec make partially different errors, supporting the view that decompiler outputs provide complementary evidence. Our results suggest that multi-decompiler prompting is a simple, training-free way to improve LLM-based malware triage in practical settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于多视角反编译的LLM恶意软件分类
当源代码不可用时，恶意软件分析师常通过反编译得到伪C代码来检查编译后的二进制文件。近期研究表明，大型语言模型（LLM）可通过将反编译代码分类为良性或恶意来辅助此过程，但现有流程通常依赖单一反编译视角。我们认为这一假设脆弱：反编译器是损失性启发式工具，不同反编译器会暴露同一二进制文件的不同特征。我们整理了一个涵盖多种威胁行为的良性工具与恶意程序基准数据集。每个样本均使用Ghidra和RetDec进行编译与反编译，生成匹配的伪C视图。在多个主流模型家族的LLM中，我们发现提供双反编译视角可提升恶意样本的F1分数，主要通过提高召回率实现。一致性分析进一步表明，Ghidra与RetDec的错误部分不同，支持反编译输出提供互补证据的观点。我们的结果表明，多反编译器提示是一种简单且无需训练的方法，可提升LLM在实际场景中的恶意软件分拣效果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-decompiler approaches in LLM-based malware classification, this study proposes using multiple decompiler views to enhance detection accuracy. The method involves compiling and decompiling the same binaries using both Ghidra and RetDec, then feeding both pseudo-C outputs to LLMs for classification. Experimental results across multiple LLM families show that combining decompiler views significantly improves malicious-class F1 scores, primarily by boosting recall, and analysis reveals that the two decompilers make complementary errors, supporting the value of multi-view input.
鉴于单一反编译器方法在基于LLM的恶意软件分类中的脆弱性，该研究提出结合多个反编译器视图以提升检测精度。研究构建了一个包含良性工具与恶意程序的基准数据集，对每个样本分别使用Ghidra和RetDec进行反编译，生成配对的伪C代码。在多个LLM模型上实验发现，融合两种反编译器输出可显著提升恶意样本的F1分数，主要通过提高召回率，且一致性分析表明两种反编译器暴露了互补的代码特征，减少了错误重叠。
Sparsity, Superposition, and Forgetting: A Mechanistic Study of Representation Retention in Continual Learning
Authors: Jan Wasilewski, Jędrzej Kozal, Michał Woźniak, Bartosz Krawczyk
First: 2026-06-18T16:10:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:10:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual learning (CL) systems often forget previously acquired knowledge, yet the mechanisms driving forgetting remain hard to isolate in practice because real datasets entangle many factors. We present a controlled, toy-world framework that makes these mechanisms observable and testable. Using a synthetic generator-separator pipeline, we define ground-truth latent features, build tasks with tunable sparsity and overlap, and introduce measurable quantities for representation strength and superposition (directional overlap among features). We then study retention dynamics-the temporal change of representation strength by fitting sparse dynamical relations (via SINDy) between retention, superposition, and exposure history. A complementary task-level analysis based on effective rank characterizes how representational capacity is allocated across tasks. Our controlled experiments yield three takeaways. (1) Superposition tends to increase over time with transient dips at task boundaries, suggesting boundary-specific interference rather than steady drift. (2) Higher feature sparsity induces more superposition yet does not inevitably cause forgetting; when representations remain strong, forgetting can be reduced despite overlap. (3) Task-level effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage under sparse regimes. Together, these results nuance the common intuition that more superposition leads to more forgetting by showing that overlap interacts with representation strength and capacity allocation. Our toy analysis provides falsifiable hypotheses and diagnostic tools for CL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：稀疏性、叠加与遗忘：持续学习中表征保留的机制研究
持续学习（CL）系统常会遗忘先前习得的知识，但实际中驱动遗忘的机制难以分离，因为真实数据集混杂多种因素。我们提出一个受控的玩具世界框架，使这些机制可观察且可测试。通过合成生成-分离管道，我们定义真实潜在特征，构建具有可调稀疏性和重叠度的任务，并引入表征强度和叠加（特征间方向性重叠）的可测量量。随后，我们研究保留动态——通过拟合稀疏动态关系（使用SINDy）分析保留、叠加与暴露历史之间的时变关系。基于有效秩的互补任务级分析刻画了表征容量在任务间的分配方式。我们的受控实验得出三点结论：（1）叠加随时间趋于增加，任务边界处出现短暂下降，表明边界特异性干扰而非持续漂移；（2）更高特征稀疏性导致更多叠加，但未必必然引发遗忘；当表征保持强时，即使存在重叠，遗忘也可减少；（3）任务级有效秩随稀疏性增加，表明在稀疏条件下更广泛地使用容量。这些结果修正了普遍认为“更多叠加导致更多遗忘”的直觉，表明重叠与表征强度及容量分配相互作用。我们的玩具分析提供了可证伪的假设和CL诊断工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of isolating forgetting mechanisms in continual learning due to confounding factors in real data, this study introduces a controlled synthetic framework using a generator-separator pipeline to define ground-truth features and tunable sparsity and overlap. It measures representation strength and superposition, then applies sparse dynamical modeling (SINDy) to track retention dynamics over time, complemented by effective rank analysis at the task level. Results show superposition increases over time with dips at task boundaries, suggesting interference rather than drift; higher sparsity increases superposition but does not guarantee forgetting if representations remain strong; and effective rank grows with sparsity, indicating broader capacity usage. These findings challenge the assumption that superposition always causes forgetting by demonstrating its interaction with representation strength and capacity allocation.
为了解决真实数据中遗忘机制难以分离的问题，该研究构建了一个可控的合成框架，通过生成器-分离器管道定义真实潜在特征并调节任务稀疏性和重叠度，测量表征强度与叠加度，并利用稀疏动态关系（SINDy）建模遗忘动态，同时用有效秩分析任务层面的容量分配。实验发现叠加度随时间增加，任务边界处短暂下降，表明干扰具有边界特异性；高稀疏度虽增加叠加度，但若表征强度保持，遗忘未必加剧；稀疏度越高，有效秩越大，说明容量使用更广泛。这些结果挑战了‘叠加度必然导致遗忘’的普遍认知，揭示了叠加度与表征强度及容量分配的交互作用。
ARC: Adaptive Robust Joint State and Covariance Estimation
Authors: Alexandre Hadji-Thomas, Andrew Stirling, James R. Forbes
First: 2026-06-18T16:09:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:09:50+00:00
Comments: Submitted to information IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L), June 2026. 8 pages, 7 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sensor measurements are frequently corrupted by outliers and non-Gaussian noise. These imperfections in the sensor data can cause classical state estimators to generate biased and unreliable state and uncertainty estimates. Robust estimators reject or downweight outliers but do not perform measurement covariance estimation, whereas joint state and covariance estimators assume Gaussian residuals and fixed loss shape parameters. Integrating these two capabilities into a single framework is an opportunity to simultaneously estimate both state and covariance in the presence of outliers. This paper proposes a unified Block-Coordinate Descent framework that combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, an Iteratively Reweighted Least-Squares state update, and a Minimum Weighted Covariance Determinant covariance estimator, yielding a self-tuning joint state and covariance estimator. The framework is evaluated in a Monte-Carlo simulation and on real-world ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered non-line-of-sight environments. Results show that the proposed estimator consistently recovers the true inlier measurement covariance and matches or exceeds the state estimation accuracy of all baselines, without requiring any manual parameter tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ARC：自适应鲁棒联合状态与协方差估计
传感器测量常受离群值和非高斯噪声干扰，导致经典状态估计器产生偏置和不可靠的状态与不确定性估计。鲁棒估计器可拒绝或降低离群值权重，但不进行测量协方差估计；而联合状态与协方差估计器则假设残差服从高斯分布且损失函数形状参数固定。将这两种能力整合到单一框架中，可同时在存在离群值的情况下估计状态与协方差。本文提出一种统一的块坐标下降框架，结合感知范数的自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新及最小加权协方差行列式协方差估计器，实现自调优的联合状态与协方差估计。该框架在蒙特卡洛仿真及真实世界超宽带定位实验（复杂非视距环境）中进行了评估。结果表明，所提估计器能稳定恢复真实内点测量协方差，并在无需手动参数调优的前提下，达到或超越所有基线方法的状态估计精度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of classical state estimators under non-Gaussian noise and outliers, this paper introduces ARC, a unified framework that jointly estimates state and measurement covariance while being robust to outliers. It combines a norm-aware adaptive robust loss, iteratively reweighted least-squares for state updates, and a minimum weighted covariance determinant estimator for covariance. Evaluated via Monte Carlo simulations and real ultra-wideband localization experiments in cluttered, non-line-of-sight environments, ARC consistently recovers true inlier covariance and achieves state estimation accuracy matching or surpassing all baselines without manual parameter tuning.
为应对传感器数据中异常值与非高斯噪声对状态与协方差估计的干扰，本文提出ARC框架，通过自适应鲁棒损失、迭代重加权最小二乘状态更新及最小加权协方差行列式协方差估计器，实现状态与协方差的联合自适应估计。在蒙特卡洛仿真和真实超宽带定位实验（复杂非视距环境）中，ARC稳定恢复真实内点测量协方差，状态估计精度优于或不劣于所有基线方法，且无需人工调参。
TaCauchy: An Extensible FEM Framework for Vision-Based Tactile Simulation
Authors: Hengfei Zhao, Yifan Xie, Junhao Gong, Yue Sun, Kai Zhu, Weihua He, Shoujie Li, Haohuan Fu, Wenbo Ding
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-06-18T16:08:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:08:45+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-based tactile sensors require high-fidelity simulation for reinforcement learning, yet existing approaches struggle to provide accurate mechanical stress fields within GPU-accelerated robotics platforms. We present TaCauchy, an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework that integrates rigorous physics-based force computation into Isaac Sim. Built on the Unified Incremental Potential Contact (UIPC) solver, TaCauchy directly computes Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic constitutive laws and projects them onto contact surfaces to obtain traction forces and pressure distributions, providing mechanical ground truth from first principles rather than empirical estimation. Our framework features automatic mesh generation with geometry-aware adaptive refinement and a modular sensor interface enabling rapid integration of diverse sensors (GelSight Mini, DIGIT, 9DTact) with minimal configuration. Performance benchmarks demonstrate 33.40 FPS for single environments and 555 FPS aggregate throughput across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction overhead under 1 ms. Physical validation experiments show strong agreement between simulated and real tactile responses across force ranges from 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N, achieving SSIM above 0.93, confirming the framework's capability to provide accurate, physically-grounded force supervision for downstream robotic manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TaCauchy：一种面向视觉触觉模拟的可扩展有限元方法框架
基于视觉的触觉传感器需要高保真度的仿真以支持强化学习，但现有方法难以在GPU加速的机器人平台上提供精确的机械应力场。我们提出了TaCauchy，一种可扩展的有限元方法（FEM）框架，将严谨的基于物理的力计算集成到Isaac Sim中。该框架基于统一增量势接触（UIPC）求解器，直接从超弹性本构定律计算Cauchy应力张量，并将其投影到接触面上以获得牵引力和压力分布，从而从第一性原理提供机械真实值，而非经验估计。该框架具备自动网格生成功能，支持几何感知的自适应细化，并配备模块化传感器接口，可快速集成多种传感器（GelSight Mini、DIGIT、9DTact），配置需求极低。性能基准测试显示，单环境帧率为33.40 FPS，60个并行环境总吞吐量达555 FPS，应力提取开销低于1毫秒。物理验证实验表明，在1.2556 N至4.7332 N的力范围内，仿真与真实触觉响应高度一致，SSIM值超过0.93，证实该框架能够为下游机器人操作任务提供准确、物理基础的力监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity tactile simulation in vision-based robotic systems for reinforcement learning, TaCauchy introduces an extensible Finite Element Method (FEM) framework integrated into Isaac Sim. It leverages the Unified Incremental Potential Contact solver to compute Cauchy stress tensors from hyperelastic material laws and project them onto contact surfaces to derive traction forces and pressure distributions, offering physically grounded force supervision. The framework supports automatic geometry-aware mesh generation and modular sensor integration, enabling rapid deployment with tactile sensors like GelSight Mini and DIGIT. Experimental results show 33.40 FPS per environment and 555 FPS across 60 parallel environments, with stress extraction under 1 ms, and physical validation confirms strong alignment between simulated and real tactile responses across 1.2556 N to 4.7332 N forces, achieving SSIM above 0.93.
为满足视觉驱动机器人系统在强化学习中对高保真触觉模拟的需求，TaCauchy提出了一种可扩展的有限元方法框架并集成于Isaac Sim中。该框架基于统一增量势接触求解器，从超弹性本构模型直接计算柯西应力张量，并将其投影至接触表面以获得牵引力与压力分布，避免经验估算。框架支持自动几何感知网格生成与模块化传感器接口，可快速适配GelSight Mini、DIGIT等触觉传感器。实验表明，单环境帧率为33.40 FPS，60个并行环境总吞吐量达555 FPS，应力提取延迟低于1毫秒；物理验证显示，在1.2556 N至4.7332 N的力范围内，模拟与真实触觉响应高度一致，SSIM超过0.93。
LIT-GS: LiDAR-Inertial-Thermal Gaussian Splatting for Illumination-Robust Mapping
Authors: Shikuan Shi, Chunran Zheng, Jiaming Xu, Tianyong Ye, Tao Yu, Yukang Cui
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-06-18T16:06:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:06:52+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gaussian Splatting has enabled real-time neural rendering, yet existing LiDAR-inertial-visual (LIV) Gaussian mapping pipelines remain fragile under illumination changes and texture-deficient scenes due to their reliance on RGB photometric cues. We present LIT-GS, a LiDAR-inertial-thermal Gaussian Splatting framework that injects LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint in both pose/structure refinement and Gaussian optimization. Specifically, we exploit LIV visual map points as confidence-aware cross-modal anchors to establish reliable thermal-LiDAR associations, and incorporate weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals into bundle adjustment to jointly refine camera poses and 3D points under weak thermal supervision. Building on the refined structure, we further introduce a LiDAR-plane-regularized differentiable splatting objective that constrains rendered 3D points to align with locally observed planes, mitigating surface thickening and structural drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experiments on proprietary sequences and public datasets demonstrate that LIT-GS consistently improves geometric accuracy and rendering quality over state-of-the-art LIV-based Gaussian Splatting baselines, particularly in challenging lighting conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LIT-GS：用于光照鲁棒建图的激光雷达-惯性-热高斯点渲染
高斯点渲染已实现神经渲染的实时性，但现有激光雷达-惯性-视觉（LIV）高斯建图流程因依赖RGB光度线索，在光照变化和纹理贫乏场景下仍易失效。本文提出LIT-GS框架，通过将激光雷达推导的平面几何作为显式约束，融入位姿/结构优化与高斯点优化过程。具体而言，我们利用LIV视觉地图点作为置信度感知的跨模态锚点，建立可靠的热成像-激光雷达关联，并将加权激光雷达点到平面残差纳入束调整，联合优化相机位姿与3D点，在弱热成像监督下实现协同优化。基于优化后的结构，我们进一步引入激光雷达平面正则化的可微分点渲染目标，约束渲染点与局部观测平面对齐，缓解低对比度热成像下的表面增厚与结构漂移问题。在自有序列与公开数据集上的实验表明，LIT-GS在几何精度与渲染质量上持续优于现有LIV高斯点渲染基线，尤其在挑战性光照条件下表现突出。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragility of existing LiDAR-inertial-visual Gaussian mapping under varying illumination and texture-poor scenes, LIT-GS introduces a thermal-aware framework that integrates LiDAR-derived plane geometry as an explicit constraint during pose and structure optimization. It leverages visual map points as confidence-weighted anchors to link thermal and LiDAR data, then refines camera poses and 3D points via weighted LiDAR point-to-plane residuals in bundle adjustment. A differentiable splatting objective further enforces alignment with local planes, reducing surface thickening and drift in low-contrast thermal imagery. Experimental results on proprietary and public datasets show consistent improvements in geometric accuracy and rendering quality over prior LIV-based Gaussian Splatting methods, especially under challenging lighting conditions.
为解决现有LiDAR-惯性-视觉高斯映射在光照变化和纹理贫乏场景下的脆弱性，LIT-GS提出一种融合热成像的框架，将LiDAR推导的平面几何作为显式约束引入位姿与结构优化。该方法利用视觉地图点作为置信度加权锚点建立热成像与LiDAR的可靠关联，并将加权LiDAR点到平面残差融入光束法平差，实现弱热成像监督下的位姿与3D点联合优化。进一步引入LiDAR平面正则化的可微分渲染目标，约束渲染点与局部观测平面对齐，缓解低对比度热成像下的表面增厚与结构漂移。实验表明，LIT-GS在专有及公开数据集上显著优于现有SOTA基线，尤其在复杂光照条件下表现更优。
Spectral Query-Key Product Weight Steering for Training-Free VLM Hallucination Mitigation
Authors: Karn Tiwari, Varnith Chordia, Prathosh A P
First: 2026-06-18T16:03:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:03:26+00:00
Comments: Under Review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision-language models (VLMs) often generate fluent but visually unsupported descriptions, especially by mentioning objects absent from the image. We propose QK Product Steering, a data-free, training-free, and zero-inference-cost weight edit for reducing object hallucination. The method directly edits the per-head query-key product, the operator that produces pre-softmax attention logits, by suppressing a small number of dominant singular modes in selected middle layers. The edited product is then mapped back to the query weights through a closed-form query-only update while keeping shared key weights fixed, making the edit compatible with grouped-query attention. We further decompose the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to distinguish mutual content-similarity patterns from directional attention patterns. Across three GQA-based VLMs, QK Product Steering achieves an average relative CHAIR$_s$ reduction of $4.0\%$, while matched random-mode controls show negligible change. Interpretability ablations show that the hallucination signal is specific to dominant QK modes and is primarily localized to the symmetric mutual-attention channel. Overall, QK Product Steering offers a simple alternative to decoding-time mitigation, requiring no additional data, fine-tuning, or inference-time overhead while largely preserving general multimodal capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于谱查询-键积权重引导的训练免视觉语言模型幻觉抑制方法
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）常生成流畅但缺乏视觉支持的描述，尤其常提及图像中不存在的对象。我们提出QK积引导法，一种无需数据、无需训练、且推理无额外开销的权重编辑方法，用于减少对象幻觉。该方法通过抑制选定中间层中若干主导奇异模式，直接编辑每头查询-键积（即生成预softmax注意力logits的算子）。编辑后的积通过闭式查询仅更新映射回查询权重，同时保持共享键权重不变，使编辑兼容分组查询注意力。我们进一步将QK积分解为对称与反对称分量，以区分相互内容相似性模式与方向性注意力模式。在三个基于GQA的VLMs上，QK积引导法平均相对CHAIR_s降低4.0%，而匹配的随机模式对照组变化微乎其微。可解释性消融实验表明，幻觉信号特异于主导QK模式，主要集中在对称互注意力通道。总体而言，QK积引导法提供了一种简单替代解码时缓解方法，无需额外数据、微调或推理开销，同时基本保留多模态通用能力。
Summary / 总结
To reduce object hallucinations in vision-language models without training or extra inference cost, the paper introduces QK Product Steering, a weight-editing method that modifies the query-key product in selected middle layers by suppressing dominant singular modes. It updates query weights via a closed-form formula while preserving shared key weights, ensuring compatibility with grouped-query attention. The method further separates the QK product into symmetric and antisymmetric components to isolate mutual similarity from directional attention. Experiments on three GQA-based VLMs show a 4.0% average relative reduction in CHAIRs hallucination metric, with random-mode controls showing no significant change, and ablations confirming that hallucination signals are concentrated in symmetric mutual-attention channels.
为解决视觉语言模型中的物体幻觉问题，且无需训练或额外推理开销，该文提出QK乘积引导方法，通过在选定中间层直接抑制查询-键乘积中的主导奇异模式来修改权重。该方法采用闭式查询仅更新方式，保持共享键权重不变，兼容分组查询注意力。方法进一步将QK乘积分解为对称与反对称分量，以区分互内容相似性与方向性注意力。在三个基于GQA的视觉语言模型上，该方法平均降低CHAIRs幻觉指标4.0%，随机模式对照组无显著变化，消融实验表明幻觉信号主要集中在对称互注意力通道。
Neural network surrogates with uncertainty quantification for inverse problems in partial differential equations
Authors: Christian Jimenez-Beltran, Aretha L. Teckentrup, Antonio Vergari, Konstantinos C. Zygalakis
First: 2026-06-18T16:02:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:02:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inverse problems for differential equations arise throughout science and engineering, where one seeks to infer unknown model parameters from noisy or incomplete observations. Traditional numerical methods for these problems are often computationally expensive, particularly in Bayesian settings where evaluating the likelihood becomes costly for complex forward models and high-dimensional parameter spaces. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate for differential equation solvers that provides uncertainty-aware predictions, reducing overconfident inference when training data are limited. To evaluate the fidelity of the surrogate-induced posterior approximations in practice, we show that a short run of delayed-acceptance Markov chain Monte Carlo can serve as an effective diagnostic. Across a range of numerical experiments, DeepGaLA delivers forward-model approximations with accuracy comparable to established Gaussian-process surrogates, while better maintaining efficiency as parameter dimension grows. Moreover, it can incorporate differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear settings. Overall, these results indicate that uncertainty-quantified neural surrogates can enable scalable and reliable Bayesian inference for inverse problems in complex systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于偏微分方程反问题的带不确定性量化神经网络代理模型
微分方程的反问题在科学与工程中普遍存在，其目标是从噪声或不完整的观测数据中推断未知模型参数。传统数值方法常因计算成本高昂而难以处理，尤其在贝叶斯框架下，复杂正向模型和高维参数空间会使似然函数评估变得昂贵。为应对这一挑战，我们提出DeepGaLA，这是一种提供不确定性感知预测的神经网络代理模型，用于微分方程求解器，可减少训练数据有限时的过度自信推断。为评估代理模型诱导后验近似的实际保真度，我们表明延迟接受马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛的短时运行可作为有效诊断工具。在一系列数值实验中，DeepGaLA提供的正向模型近似精度与现有高斯过程代理相当，且在参数维度增大时保持更优效率。此外，它还能融入微分方程约束，包括非线性情形。总体而言，这些结果表明，带不确定性量化的神经网络代理可实现复杂系统中反问题的可扩展且可靠的贝叶斯推断。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational expense of Bayesian inference in inverse problems for differential equations, this work introduces DeepGaLA, a neural-network surrogate that provides uncertainty quantification to improve reliability in parameter estimation. The method trains a neural network to approximate the forward model and its uncertainty, enabling efficient posterior inference even in high-dimensional parameter spaces. Experimental results show that DeepGaLA achieves accuracy comparable to Gaussian-process surrogates while maintaining better scalability with increasing parameter dimension and effectively incorporating differential-equation constraints, including in nonlinear cases.
为解决微分方程逆问题中贝叶斯推断计算成本高的问题，本文提出DeepGaLA，一种提供不确定性量化能力的神经网络代理模型。该方法通过训练神经网络近似正向模型及其不确定性，结合延迟接受MCMC实现高效后验推断。实验表明，DeepGaLA在精度上可媲美高斯过程代理，且随参数维度增长更具扩展性，同时支持非线性微分约束，适用于复杂高维逆问题的可靠推断。
On the Redundancy of Timestep Embeddings in Diffusion Models
Authors: José A. Chávez
First: 2026-06-18T16:01:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:01:39+00:00
Comments: 17 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models rely heavily on explicit timestep embeddings to modulate the denoising process across various noise scales. In this work, we challenge the necessity of these temporal signals by analyzing their impact on U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Beyond empirical evidence, we provide a theoretical framework demonstrating that, under certain conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion training objective can be achieved without explicit timestep conditioning. Our findings reveal a surprising robustness when timestep embeddings are completely removed. Extensive ablation studies on the CelebA and CIFAR-10 datasets show that these time-agnostic models can maintain high structural fidelity and even surpass their conditioned counterparts in competitive metrics, including FID, precision, and recall. Our analysis suggests these architectures can implicitly infer noise scales from the corrupted input under specific assumptions, rendering explicit temporal conditioning redundant. This study challenges long-standing temporal conditioning paradigms and paves the way for more efficient and structurally focused generative architectures.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩散模型中时间步嵌入的冗余性研究
扩散模型依赖显式时间步嵌入来调节不同噪声尺度下的去噪过程。本文通过分析其在U-Net和扩散Transformer架构中的影响，质疑这些时间信号的必要性。除实证证据外，我们提供理论框架证明，在特定条件下，扩散训练目标的全局最小值无需显式时间步条件即可实现。研究发现，完全移除时间步嵌入时模型表现出惊人的鲁棒性。在CelebA和CIFAR-10数据集上的大量消融实验表明，这些无时间依赖模型可保持高结构保真度，并在FID、精确率和召回率等竞争性指标上超越有时间条件的模型。我们的分析表明，在特定假设下，这些架构可从被破坏的输入中隐式推断噪声尺度，使显式时间条件冗余。本研究挑战了长期存在的时间条件范式，为更高效、结构导向的生成架构铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
This study questions the necessity of explicit timestep embeddings in diffusion models by analyzing their role in U-Net and Diffusion Transformer architectures. Through both theoretical analysis and empirical ablation experiments on CelebA and CIFAR-10, the authors demonstrate that under specific conditions, the global minimizer of the diffusion objective can be achieved without timestep conditioning. Removing these embeddings surprisingly does not degrade performance, and in some cases, time-agnostic models outperform their conditioned counterparts in metrics like FID, precision, and recall, suggesting that noise levels can be implicitly inferred from corrupted inputs. This challenges the conventional reliance on explicit temporal signals and supports the development of more efficient generative architectures.
该研究质疑扩散模型中显式时间步嵌入的必要性，通过分析其在U-Net和扩散Transformer架构中的作用，结合理论分析与在CelebA和CIFAR-10上的消融实验，证明在特定条件下，扩散目标的全局最小值无需时间步条件即可实现。移除时间嵌入后模型表现反而不降反升，在FID、精确率和召回率等指标上甚至优于带条件模型，表明噪声水平可从被破坏的输入中隐式推断。这一发现挑战了传统依赖显式时间信号的范式，为更高效、结构更聚焦的生成架构铺平道路。
Pseudo-Feature Padding: A Lightweight Defense Against False Data Injection in Power Grids
Authors: Farhin Farhad Riya, Shahinul Hoque, Yingyuan Yang, Jinyuan Sun, Kevin Tomsovic
First: 2026-06-18T16:01:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T16:01:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deep Neural Networks DNNs have achieved remarkable accuracy in various tasks including their application in CyberPhysical Systems CPS for detecting False Data Injection Attacks FDIA during critical operations However the unique infrastructure of CPS makes DNNs vulnerable to exploitation by attackers aiming to evade detection Additionally the distinct nature of CPS presents challenges for conventional defense mechanisms against FDIA This paper proposes an innovative defense framework that strengthens DNNs against such attacks by introducing an additional input layer that performs padding in the input samples using pseudofeature values derived from the inputs statistical distribution This padding increases the input dimensionality in a randomized and dataaware manner making adversarial attacks computationally infeasible due to the nontransferable nature of crafted perturbations and the unpredictability of the padded structure Our method is lightweight modelagnostic and requires no modifications to the core architecture making it highly deployable in realworld CPS settings We evaluated our framework on critical power grid applications such as state estimation using the IEEE 14bus 30bus 118bus and 300bus systems Experiments under adversarial settings demonstrate that our padding strategy significantly improves model robustness with negligible impact on performance and effectively mitigates attacks that would otherwise bypass conventional defenses
中文标题/摘要
标题：伪特征填充：一种轻量级的电力系统虚假数据注入攻击防御方法
深度神经网络（DNN）在包括网络物理系统（CPS）在内的多种任务中取得了显著精度，尤其在关键操作期间用于检测虚假数据注入攻击（FDIA）。然而，CPS的独特基础设施使DNN易受攻击者利用以逃避检测，且CPS的特性也对传统FDIA防御机制构成挑战。本文提出一种创新防御框架，通过在输入层引入伪特征值填充机制，利用输入数据的统计分布生成伪特征，从而在随机且数据感知的方式下增加输入维度。该填充使对抗攻击因生成扰动的不可迁移性和填充结构的不可预测性而计算上不可行。本方法轻量、模型无关，无需修改核心架构，高度适用于现实CPS部署。我们在IEEE 14节点、30节点、118节点和300节点电力系统中评估了该框架在状态估计等关键电力应用中的表现。实验表明，该填充策略显著提升模型鲁棒性，对性能影响微乎其微，并有效缓解传统防御机制无法应对的攻击。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of deep neural networks in cyber-physical systems like power grids to false data injection attacks, this paper proposes a lightweight, model-agnostic defense called pseudo-feature padding. The method introduces an additional input layer that randomly pads input samples with pseudofeature values derived from the input’s statistical distribution, increasing dimensionality in a data-aware manner. This disrupts adversarial perturbation transferability and makes attacks computationally infeasible without altering the core model. Experiments on IEEE power grid benchmarks (14bus, 30bus, 118bus, 300bus) under adversarial settings show the method significantly enhances robustness against FDIA while preserving model accuracy and requiring no architectural changes.
为应对深度神经网络在电力等物理信息系统中易受虚假数据注入攻击的弱点，本文提出一种轻量级、模型无关的防御方法——伪特征填充。该方法在输入样本中引入基于输入统计分布生成的伪特征值进行随机填充，以数据感知的方式增加输入维度，从而破坏对抗扰动的可迁移性，使攻击计算上不可行，且无需修改核心模型结构。在IEEE电力系统（14节点、30节点、118节点、300节点）的对抗性实验中，该方法显著提升了模型对攻击的鲁棒性，同时几乎不影响性能。
VibeCheck: Using Active Acoustic Tactile Sensing for Contact-Rich Manipulation
Authors: Kaidi Zhang, Do-Gon Kim, Eric T. Chang, Hua-Hsuan Liang, Zhanpeng He, Kathryn Lampo, Philippe Wu, Ioannis Kymissis, Matei Ciocarlie
Venue: IROS 2025
First: 2025-04-22T02:25:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:59:11+00:00
Comments: Published at IROS 2025. 8 pages, 7 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
The acoustic response of an object can reveal a lot about its global state, for example its material properties or the extrinsic contacts it is making with the world. In this work, we build an active acoustic sensing gripper equipped with two piezoelectric fingers: one for generating signals, the other for receiving them. By sending an acoustic vibration from one finger to the other through an object, we gain insight into an object's acoustic properties and contact state. We use this system to classify objects, estimate grasping position, estimate poses of internal structures, and classify the types of extrinsic contacts an object is making with the environment. Using our contact type classification model, we tackle a standard long-horizon manipulation problem: peg insertion. We use a simple simulated transition model based on the performance of our sensor to train an imitation learning policy that is robust to imperfect predictions from the classifier. We finally demonstrate the policy on a UR5 robot with active acoustic sensing as the only feedback. Videos can be found at https://roamlab.github.io/vibecheck .
中文标题/摘要
标题：VibeCheck：利用主动声学触觉感知实现接触密集型操作
物体的声学响应可揭示其全局状态，例如材料属性或与外界的接触情况。本研究构建了一种主动声学感知夹爪，配备两个压电手指：一个用于发射信号，另一个用于接收信号。通过将声学振动从一个手指经物体传递至另一个手指，我们可了解物体的声学特性和接触状态。我们利用该系统对物体进行分类、估计抓取位置、估计内部结构姿态，并分类物体与环境的外部接触类型。基于接触类型分类模型，我们解决了一个标准的长时程操作问题：销钉插入。我们基于传感器性能构建了一个简单的模拟转移模型，用以训练一个模仿学习策略，使其对分类器的不完美预测具有鲁棒性。最后，我们在仅使用主动声学感知作为反馈的UR5机器人上验证了该策略。视频见 https://roamlab.github.io/vibecheck。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential of acoustic signals to reveal object properties and contact states, this work develops an active acoustic sensing gripper with piezoelectric fingers that transmit and receive vibrations through objects. The system classifies objects, estimates grasp positions and internal structure poses, and identifies extrinsic contact types. Applied to peg insertion, a long-horizon manipulation task, the method trains an imitation learning policy using a simulated transition model based on sensor predictions, which remains robust despite classifier inaccuracies. Experiments on a UR5 robot confirm the system’s effectiveness when using acoustic sensing as the sole feedback.
为利用声学信号揭示物体属性与接触状态，该研究提出VibeCheck系统，配备两个压电手指——一个发射振动，一个接收振动。通过向物体发送振动并分析其声学响应，系统可实现物体分类、抓取位置估计、内部结构姿态推断及外部接触类型识别。该方法应用于插销任务，基于传感器性能构建的简化过渡模型用于训练模仿学习策略，使其在接触分类不完美时仍具鲁棒性。实验在UR5机器人上验证，仅依赖声学传感实现成功操作。
Direct Advantage Estimation for Scalable and Sample-efficient Deep Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Hsiao-Ru Pan, Bernhard Schölkopf
First: 2026-06-18T15:58:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:58:48+00:00
Comments: Accepted at RLC2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) has been shown to improve the sample efficiency of deep reinforcement learning algorithms. However, its reliance on full environment observability limits its applicability in realistic settings, and its requirement to model transition probabilities incurs substantial computational overhead for high-dimensional observations. In the present work, we address both limitations. First, we extend the theoretical framework of DAE to partially observable domains with minimal modifications. Second, we reduce its computational complexity by introducing discrete latent dynamics models that efficiently approximate transition probabilities. We evaluate our approach on the Arcade Learning Environment and find that DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity while retaining high sample efficiency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：直接优势估计用于可扩展且样本高效的深度强化学习
直接优势估计（DAE）已被证明能提升深度强化学习算法的样本效率。然而，其依赖完整环境观测限制了其在现实场景中的适用性，且其要求建模转移概率对高维观测带来显著计算开销。本研究解决了这两个限制：首先，我们将DAE的理论框架扩展至部分可观测域，仅需最小修改；其次，通过引入离散潜在动态模型高效近似转移概率，降低其计算复杂度。我们在街机学习环境中评估了该方法，发现DAE在函数近似器容量扩展时仍保持高样本效率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance sample efficiency in deep reinforcement learning while reducing computational burden, this work extends Direct Advantage Estimation (DAE) to partially observable environments and introduces discrete latent dynamics to approximate transition probabilities without modeling full state transitions. Experimental results on the Arcade Learning Environment demonstrate that the modified DAE scales effectively with function approximator capacity and maintains high sample efficiency, even under partial observability and high-dimensional state spaces.
为提升深度强化学习的样本效率并解决直接优势估计在部分可观测环境中的适用性及计算开销问题，本文将其理论框架扩展至部分可观测域，并引入离散潜在动态模型以近似转移概率，从而降低计算负担。在街机学习环境上的实验表明，改进后的DAE能随函数逼近器容量有效扩展，同时在部分可观测条件下仍保持高样本效率。
LLM agent safety, multi-turn red-teaming, jailbreak benchmarks, adversarial robustness, safety-critical systems
Authors: Hanwool Lee, Dasol Choi, Bokyeong Kim, Seung Geun Kim, Haon Park
First: 2026-06-18T15:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:57:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly proposed as supervisory components for safety-critical systems, yet their robustness under sustained, adaptive adversarial pressure remains poorly characterized. We present NRT-Bench, a benchmark for multi-turn red-teaming of LLM agents acting as operators of a safety-critical system, instantiated in a simulated nuclear power plant control room. A five-role operator team, each backed by a configurable LLM, runs a plant governed by six critical safety functions (CSFs), while adversaries inject messages over four channels in bounded multi-turn sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is an objective signal rather than LLM-judged text: a run terminates the moment any CSF is lost, attributed to the causing message. Evaluating four frontier operator models under a fixed-attack paired-replay protocol, we find that adaptive multi-turn attacks reliably push the operator team past a safety limit: across the four models, between 8.7% and 12.1% of attack sessions end with the plant losing a critical safety function. Although the four models look almost equally robust by this aggregate rate, their failures barely overlap: of $149$ sessions, none defeat all four models while a third defeat at least one, so vulnerabilities are nearly disjoint across models rather than nested. The effect of added defences is strongly model-dependent: the same guardrail stack or safety-advisor agent that lowers attack success for one model can raise it for another. We release the simulation venue, attack dataset, and replay tooling for reproducible safety evaluation of LLM agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型代理安全、多轮红队攻击、越狱基准、对抗鲁棒性、安全关键系统
大语言模型（LLM）代理正日益被提议作为安全关键系统的监督组件，但其在持续、自适应对抗压力下的鲁棒性仍缺乏充分表征。我们提出了NRT-Bench，这是一个针对LLM代理作为安全关键系统操作员的多轮红队攻击基准，场景设定在模拟的核电站控制室。一个由五名角色组成的操作员团队，每名成员由可配置的LLM支持，运行一个由六个关键安全功能（CSFs）控制的电厂，而攻击者通过四个通道在有回合反馈的有限多轮会话中注入消息。危害以客观信号而非LLM评判的文本形式体现：一旦任何CSF丢失，即终止运行，并将责任归于导致该损失的消息。在固定攻击配对重放协议下评估四个前沿操作员模型，我们发现自适应多轮攻击能可靠地使操作员团队突破安全阈值：在四个模型中，8.7%至12.1%的攻击会话最终导致电厂失去关键安全功能。尽管四个模型按此聚合成功率看似几乎等鲁棒，但其失败模式几乎不重叠：在149个会话中，无一攻击能同时击败所有四个模型，而三分之一的攻击至少击败一个模型，表明各模型的脆弱性几乎互不重叠而非嵌套。防御措施的效果高度依赖模型：同一套护栏或安全顾问代理，对一个模型降低攻击成功率，却可能对另一个模型提升成功率。我们发布模拟环境、攻击数据集和重放工具，以支持LLM代理安全评估的可复现性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess how large language model (LLM) agents handle persistent adversarial pressure in safety-critical systems, the authors introduce NRT-Bench, a multi-turn red-teaming benchmark set in a simulated nuclear power plant. Five LLM agents, each assigned a role in operating the plant’s six critical safety functions, are challenged by adversaries injecting messages across four channels over bounded sessions with per-turn feedback. Harm is measured by actual loss of safety functions, not text-based judgments. Although the models appeared similarly robust by aggregate failure rate, their failure patterns were nearly disjoint—no single attack defeated all four, but a third defeated at least one.
