为评估大语言模型代理在安全关键系统中面对持续对抗攻击时的鲁棒性，研究者构建了NRT-Bench多轮红队攻防基准，场景为模拟核电站控制室。五个LLM代理分别扮演操作角色，控制六个关键安全功能，而攻击者通过四个通道注入消息并获得每轮反馈。系统失效以实际安全功能丢失为判定标准，而非文本评估。在固定攻击重放协议下测试四个前沿模型，发现8.7%至12.1%的攻击会引发系统失效，且各模型漏洞几乎不重叠——无攻击能同时击败全部模型，但三分之一攻击至少击败一个模型。防御措施效果高度依赖模型：同一套防护机制对不同模型可能降低或提升攻击成功率。
FlowBender: Feedback-Aware Training for Self-Correcting Conditional Flows
Authors: Daniel Gilo, Sven Elflein, Ido Sobol, Or Litany
First: 2026-06-18T15:56:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:56:07+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://flow-bender.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Conditional diffusion and flow models routinely fail to satisfy the very constraints that define their task. For instance, a depth-conditioned model often produces images whose re-extracted depth disagrees with the input, even though the forward operator--the depth predictor defining the constraint--is available during both training and inference. Existing approaches generally fall into two categories: supervised models that treat the conditioning signal as a static cue and ignore alignment information at inference, and guidance-based methods that consult it through hand-tuned linear updates, typically trading fidelity to the condition against the plausibility of the generated sample. We argue that the fundamental gap in both paradigms is that the model is never trained to utilize its own alignment error. We introduce FlowBender, a closed-loop framework that treats this error as a first-class input, training the network to learn a correction policy conditioned on inference-time feedback. At each step, an unguided look-ahead pass estimates the clean signal, a task-specific deviation is computed via the forward operator, and a refinement pass consumes this signal to produce a corrected velocity. We propose several variants of FlowBender, including a gradient-based formulation for differentiable operators and a zero-order variant for non-differentiable settings such as JPEG compression. For efficient sampling, we introduce a prior-step shortcut that enables closed-loop correction at a minimal additional computational cost. Across image-to-image translation, restoration, and 3D mesh texturing, FlowBender consistently outperforms standard supervised baselines, alignment-loss-augmented training, and state-of-the-art inference-time guidance, improving fidelity and plausibility simultaneously rather than trading them against each other. Project page: https://flow-bender.github.io/
中文标题/摘要
标题：FlowBender：面向自修正条件流的反馈感知训练
条件扩散与流模型通常无法满足其任务所定义的约束。例如，深度条件模型常生成重新提取深度与输入不一致的图像，尽管前向操作（即定义约束的深度预测器）在训练和推理时均可用。现有方法主要分为两类：将条件信号视为静态线索的监督模型，推理时忽略对齐信息；以及通过人工调优线性更新咨询条件信号的引导方法，通常以牺牲条件保真度换取生成样本的合理性。我们认为，两类范式的根本差距在于模型从未被训练以利用自身的对齐误差。我们提出FlowBender，一种闭环框架，将该误差视为一等输入，训练网络学习基于推理时反馈的修正策略。每一步中，无引导的前瞻传递估计干净信号，通过前向操作计算任务特定偏差，再通过精炼传递消耗该信号生成修正速度。我们提出FlowBender的若干变体，包括适用于可微算子的梯度形式，以及适用于非可微场景（如JPEG压缩）的零阶变体。为高效采样，我们引入先验步捷径，使闭环修正仅需极小额外计算开销。在图像到图像翻译、图像修复及3D网格纹理化任务中，FlowBender持续优于标准监督基线、对齐损失增强训练及最新推理时引导方法，同时提升保真度与合理性，而非二者取舍。项目页面：https://flow-bender.github.io/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the failure of conditional flow models to satisfy their own constraints despite having access to forward operators during training and inference, the authors propose FlowBender, a closed-loop training framework that treats alignment error as a first-class input. The method uses an unguided look-ahead pass to estimate a clean signal, computes a task-specific deviation via the forward operator, and applies a refinement pass to correct the velocity based on this error. Variants include gradient-based and zero-order formulations for differentiable and non-differentiable settings, with a prior-step shortcut enabling efficient closed-loop correction. Experiments across image-to-image translation, restoration, and 3D mesh texturing show FlowBender consistently outperforms supervised baselines, alignment-loss-augmented training, and state-of-the-art guidance methods by simultaneously improving fidelity to the condition and sample plausibility without trade-offs.
FlowBender 解决了条件生成模型在推理时难以满足自身约束的问题，即使约束算子在训练和推理时均可用。该方法提出一种闭环训练框架，将对齐误差作为主要输入，使模型能学习基于自身输出反馈的修正策略。其核心流程包括：先进行无引导的前瞻预测以估计干净信号，再通过前向算子计算偏差，并用修正步骤调整速度。方法提供梯度型和零阶型变体，分别适用于可微和不可微场景，并引入前置步捷径以提升采样效率。在图像到图像翻译、图像修复和3D网格纹理生成等任务中，FlowBender 一致优于监督基线、增强对齐损失的方法及当前最先进引导技术，同时提升条件保真度与生成样本合理性，无需二者取舍。
CADBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for AI-Assisted CAD Program Generation
Authors: Anna C. Doris, Jacob Thomas Sony, Ghadi Nehme, Era Syla, Amin Heyrani Nobari, Faez Ahmed
First: 2026-05-11T17:25:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:54:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recovering editable CAD programs from images or 3D observations is central to AI-assisted design, but progress is difficult to measure because existing evaluations are fragmented across datasets, modalities, and metrics. We introduce CADBench, a unified benchmark for multimodal CAD program generation. CADBench contains 18,000 evaluation samples spanning six benchmark families derived from DeepCAD, Fusion 360, ABC, MCB, and Objaverse; five input modalities including clean meshes, noisy meshes, single-view renders, photorealistic renders, and multi-view renders; and six metrics covering geometric fidelity, executability, and program compactness. STEP-based families are stratified by B-rep face count and all families are diversity-sampled to support controlled analysis across complexity and object variation. We benchmark eleven CAD-specialized and general-purpose vision-language systems, generating more than 1.4 million CAD programs. Under idealized inputs, specialized mesh-to-CAD models substantially outperform code-generating VLMs, which remain far from reliable CAD program reconstruction. CADBench further reveals three recurring failure modes: reconstruction quality degrades with geometric complexity, CAD-specialized models can be brittle under modality shift, and model rankings change across metrics. Together, these results position CADBench as a diagnostic testbed for measuring progress in editable 3D reconstruction and multimodal CAD understanding. The benchmark is publicly available at https://github.com/anniedoris/CADBench.

The Significance of Style Diversity in Annotation-Free Synthetic Data Generation
Authors: Zahra Abbasiantaeb, Zeno Belligoli, Omar Essam, Mohammad Aliannejadi
First: 2026-06-18T15:53:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:53:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generating high-utility synthetic data for intent classification typically requires human-annotated seed data, which is often unavailable in fast-paced industrial settings. In this paper, we propose a framework for synthetic dialogue generation that works entirely without human-annotated data, relying solely on intent definitions. Our proposed dialogue generation framework utilizes two different types of topic and style attributes to improve data diversity. Also, we propose two novel post-hoc stylization models called Univ and Exam to transform synthetic LLM-generated utterances into more varied, human-like linguistic styles. To enhance data quality, we utilize an LLM-as-a-judge filtering process. Experimental results on both industrial and public datasets demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance obtained using human-annotated training data. Crucially, the findings reveal that style diversity is more critical than topic diversity for synthetic data utility, as it prevents models from learning spurious stylistic correlations. Furthermore, the study shows that incorporating style attributes during the generation process is more effective than post-hoc style adaptation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无标注合成数据生成中风格多样性的意义
为意图分类生成高价值合成数据通常需要人工标注的种子数据，但在快节奏的工业环境中这类数据往往难以获取。本文提出一种完全无需人工标注数据的合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义。该框架利用两种不同类型的主题和风格属性以提升数据多样性，并提出两种新颖的后处理风格化模型Univ和Exam，将合成LLM生成的语句转化为更具多样性和人类语言风格的表达。为提升数据质量，采用LLM作为裁判的过滤流程。在工业和公开数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法可达到人工标注训练数据性能的93.3%。关键发现表明，风格多样性比主题多样性对合成数据效用更为关键，因为它能防止模型学习到虚假的风格相关性。此外，研究显示在生成过程中引入风格属性比后处理风格调整更有效。

Motivated by the scarcity of human-annotated data in industrial settings, this paper proposes an annotation-free synthetic dialogue generation framework that relies only on intent definitions. The method introduces two types of attributes—topic and style—to guide generation, and employs two novel post-hoc stylization models, Univ and Exam, to diversify linguistic style. An LLM-as-a-judge filtering step further refines output quality. Experiments show the approach achieves up to 93.3% of the performance of human-annotated data, with key findings indicating that style diversity significantly outperforms topic diversity in preventing spurious correlations and that incorporating style during generation is more effective than post-hoc adaptation.
为了解决工业场景中人工标注数据稀缺的问题，本文提出一种无需人工标注的合成对话生成框架，仅依赖意图定义。该方法引入话题与风格两类属性引导生成，并设计了两个新颖的后处理风格化模型Univ和Exam以增强语言多样性，同时采用大模型作为裁判进行数据过滤。实验表明，该方法在工业与公开数据集上达到人工标注数据93.3%的性能，关键发现是风格多样性比话题多样性对数据效用更关键，能避免模型学习虚假风格关联，且生成阶段引入风格比后处理风格化更有效。
An adaptive framework for the axisymmetric pulsar magnetosphere using physics-informed Kolmogorov-Arnold networks
Authors: Spyros Rigas, Ioannis Contopoulos, Georgios Alexandridis, Antonios Nathanail
First: 2026-06-09T10:44:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-06-18T15:50:35+00:00
Comments: 25 pages, 10 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The pulsar magnetosphere has only recently been addressed using Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs), by deploying a domain-decomposition approach and treating the separatrix and equatorial current sheet as infinitesimally thin discontinuities. However, this baseline requires extensive manual hyperparameter tuning, achieves limited final accuracy and demands several hours of training. We refine this framework by introducing domain-specific neural architectures based on Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, an automated adaptive training pipeline and a physics-based convergence criterion that eliminate the need for manual calibration. The proposed methodology delivers self-consistent axisymmetric magnetosphere solutions with mean squared errors of the PDE residuals at O(1e-6) in double precision - an improvement of two orders of magnitude over the baseline - while achieving convergence in under 20 minutes in single precision. Importantly, the method reliably resolves stellar radii reduced by up to 80% compared to the baseline, overcoming the severe spatial scale disparities that also challenge traditional solvers. Furthermore, by varying the flux that opens to infinity, we provide a correction to the equation that connects it to the equatorial T-point's position. The complete framework is released as the open-source library PulsarX.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于物理信息的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络的轴对称脉冲星磁层自适应框架
脉冲星磁层最近才开始使用物理信息神经网络（PINNs）进行研究，通过域分解方法并把分离面和赤道电流片视为无限薄不连续面。然而，该基准方法需要大量手动超参数调整，最终精度有限，且训练需数小时。我们通过引入基于柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络的领域特定神经架构、自动化自适应训练流程和基于物理的收敛标准，改进了该框架，消除了手动校准需求。所提方法可自洽求解轴对称磁层，双精度下PDE残差均方误差达O(1e-6)——比基准提升两个数量级，单精度下收敛时间少于20分钟。更重要的是，该方法能可靠解析比基准缩小达80%的恒星半径，克服了传统求解器面临的严重空间尺度差异问题。此外，通过调节通向无穷远的磁通量，我们修正了连接其与赤道T点位置的方程。完整框架以开源库PulsarX发布。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) methods for pulsar magnetosphere modeling—namely manual hyperparameter tuning, low accuracy, and long training times—the authors propose an adaptive framework using Kolmogorov-Arnold networks, automated training, and physics-based convergence criteria. This approach eliminates manual calibration, achieving self-consistent axisymmetric solutions with PDE residual errors at O(1e-6) in double precision, a two-order improvement over prior methods, and converging in under 20 minutes in single precision. It also reliably resolves stellar radii reduced by up to 80% compared to baseline methods, overcoming severe spatial scale challenges, and introduces a flux-dependent correction to the equatorial T-point position.
为解决现有物理信息神经网络（PINN）方法在脉冲星磁层建模中存在的人工调参繁琐、精度低、训练耗时长等问题，作者提出了一种基于物理信息的柯尔莫哥洛夫-阿诺德网络的自适应框架。该方法采用领域特定神经架构、自动化训练流程和基于物理的收敛准则，无需人工调参。实验表明，该框架在双精度下可实现自洽的轴对称磁层解，偏微分方程残差误差达O(1e-6)，较基线方法提升两个数量级，单精度下训练时间少于20分钟。同时，其能可靠解析比基线缩小80%的恒星半径，有效克服空间尺度差异难题，并修正了与赤道T点位置相关的无穷远通量方程。
